function waveCoef(w){
const a=new Float64Array(256),b=new Float64Array(256),PI=Math.PI;
for(let n=1;n<256;n++){
const odd=n&1;
if(w===1)b[n]=1/n;
else if(w===2)b[n]=1/(n*n);
else if(w===3)b[n]=odd?((n>>1)&1?-1:1)/(n*n):0;
else if(w===4)b[n]=odd?1/n:0;
else if(w===5){if(n===1)b[n]=.5;else if(!odd)a[n]=-2/(PI*(n*n-1));}
else if(w===6){a[n]=Math.sin(PI*n/2)/(PI*n);b[n]=(1-Math.cos(PI*n/2))/(PI*n);}
else if(w===7)b[n]=n===1?1:n===2? .5:0;
else if(w===8)b[n]=n===1?1:n===3? .5:0;
else if(w===9)b[n]=4*n/PI*(1/(4*n*n-1)-1/(4*n*n-9));
else if(w===10)b[n]=n===1?5/16:n===2?-.25:n===3?1/16:0;
}
if(w===11){let r=0x9E3779B9;for(let n=1;n<256;n++){
r^=r<<13;r^=r>>>17;r^=r<<5;const ph=((r>>>0)/4294967296)*2*PI;a[n]=Math.cos(ph);b[n]=Math.sin(ph);}}
return[a,b];
}
function waveTab(w){

T.push(x);
}
let pk=0;for(const v of T[0])pk=Math.max(pk,Math.abs(v));const sc=pk>0?1/pk:1;
T=T.map(x=>{const t=new Float32Array(WV_N+1);for(let i=0;i<WV_N;i++)t[i]=x[i]*sc;t[WV_N]=t[0];return t;});
T.push(T[8]);
return(WAVES[w]=T);
}

function fmxSig(P){

}
function envDx(e){

if(e&&e.f==='pt'&&Array.isArray(e.y)&&Array.isArray(e.t)&&e.y.length>=2){
const y=e.y,t=e.t,k=y.length-1,sus=e.sus>=1&&e.sus<=k?e.sus:Math.min(3,k);
const rt=ms=>c(99-7.2*Math.log2(Math.max(3,+ms||3)/3),99),L=[y[1],y[Math.min(2,sus)],y[sus],y[k]];
return{r:[rt(t[0]),rt(t[1]),rt(t[2]),rt(t[k-1])],l:L.map((v,i)=>c(v,i<3?99:0))};
}
return{r:[99,99,99,80],l:[99,99,99,0]};
}

function ptCompile(e,pit,pr){

}
function riseG(a,b){let t=0,x=a;while(x<b){const bd=Math.floor(x/256),nx=Math.min(b,(bd+1)*256);t+=(nx-x)/Math.max(1,16-bd);x=nx;}return t;}
function riseAt(a,b,tau){let x=a;while(x<b&&tau>0){const bd=Math.floor(x/256),m=Math.max(1,16-bd),nx=Math.min(b,(bd+1)*256),need=(nx-x)/m;if(tau<=need)return x+tau*m;tau-=need;x=nx;}return b;}
function ptDist(E,a,b){if(E.pit||E.lin||b<=a)return Math.abs(a-b);return riseG(b>1716&&a<1716?1716:a,b);}
function ptRelScale(E,aN,cur,b){const d=ptDist(E,cur,b);if(!(d>0))return 1;const dn=ptDist(E,aN,b);return d/(dn>1e-9?dn:E.pit?12:256);}
function qRc(q){if(q<=0)return LVL_PER_SEC(0)*Math.pow(2,q/4);if(q>=63)return LVL_PER_SEC(63)*Math.pow(2,(q-63)/4);const n=Math.floor(q),a=LVL_PER_SEC(n);return a*Math.pow(LVL_PER_SEC(n+1)/a,q-n);}
function qInv(R){const r0=LVL_PER_SEC(0),r63=LVL_PER_SEC(63);if(R<=r0)return 4*Math.log2(R/r0);if(R>=r63)return 63+4*Math.log2(R/r63);
let n=0;while(n<62&&LVL_PER_SEC(n+1)<=R)n++;const a=LVL_PER_SEC(n);return n+Math.log(R/a)/Math.log(LVL_PER_SEC(n+1)/a);}
const hN=rr=>(rr<77?EG_STATICS[rr]:Math.max(1,20*(99-rr)));
function hC(x){x=Math.max(0,Math.min(99,x));const n=Math.min(98,Math.floor(x)),a=hN(n),b=hN(n+1);return a===b?a:a*Math.pow(b/a,x-n);}
function hInv(N){if(N>=hN(0))return 0;let n=0;while(n<98&&hN(n+1)>=N)n++;const a=hN(n),b=hN(n+1);return a===b||N<=b?n+1:n+Math.log(N/a)/Math.log(b/a);}
function ptDxF(E,j,car,rsd,ofs0,atk,dec,rel,bite,ofs){
if(E.pit)return 1;
const relSeg=E.sus>=0&&j>E.sus,off=Math.round(j===1?atk:relSeg?-rel:car?-dec:0),bt=!car&&j>1&&!relSeg?bite:0;
const rs=E.r?E.r[j]:-1;
if(!rsd&&!off&&!bt&&!(rs>=0&&ofs!=null&&ofs!==ofs0))return 1;
const tg=(y,o)=>Math.max(16,((scaleOL(y|0)>>1)<<6)+o),a=tg(E.v[j-1],ofs0),b=tg(E.v[j],ofs0),sec=E.tm[j]/1000,bf=bt?Math.pow(2,bt/10):1;
if(a===b&&j!==E.sus+1){const x=rs>=0&&Math.abs(Math.log(hN(rs)/(sec*44100)))<.02?rs:hInv(sec*44100),xr=Math.abs(x-Math.round(x))<.02?Math.round(x):x;return hC(Math.max(0,Math.min(99,xr+off))+rsd)/hC(xr)/bf;}
const d=a===b?256:ptDist(E,a,b);if(!(d>0)||!(sec>0))return 1;
const R0=d/sec;let R1,rr=-1;
if(rs>=0&&Math.abs(Math.log(Math.max(d/LVL_PER_SEC((rs*41)>>6),.0005)/sec))<.02)rr=rs;
else{const q0=qInv(R0),qi=Math.round(q0);
if(Math.abs(q0-qi)<.02&&qi>=0&&qi<=63){rr=Math.ceil(qi*64/41);if(((rr*41)>>6)!==qi)rr=Math.round(qi*64/41);}
else R1=q0>=63?qRc(q0+Math.min(0,off*41/64)):qRc(Math.min(63,q0+off*41/64+rsd));}
if(rr>=0)R1=LVL_PER_SEC(Math.min(63,((Math.max(0,Math.min(99,rr+off))*41)>>6)+rsd));
const dc=rr>=0&&rr===rs&&ofs!=null&&ofs!==ofs0&&a!==b?ptDist(E,tg(E.v[j-1],ofs),tg(E.v[j],ofs))/d:1;
if(bt){let q=R1*bf;if(bf>1&&b<a&&a-b>=510)q=Math.min(q,Math.max(R1,LVL_PER_SEC(Math.min(63,((82*41)>>6)+rsd))));R1=q;}
return R0/R1*dc;
}
function ptVal(E,a,b,x,G){
if(x>=1)return b;
if(E.pit){if(!E.lin)return a+(b-a)*x;const A=Math.pow(2,a/12),B=Math.pow(2,b/12);return 12*Math.log2(A+(B-A)*x);}
if(E.lin){const A=Math.pow(2,a/256),B=Math.pow(2,b/256);return 256*Math.log2(A+(B-A)*x);}
if(b<=a||!(G>0))return a+(b-a)*x;
return riseAt(a,b,x*G);
}
function ptState(){return{pS:new Int8Array(12),pH:new Uint8Array(12),pN:new Float64Array(12),pX:new Float64Array(12),pA:new Float64Array(12),
pB:new Float64Array(12),pG:new Float64Array(12),pV:new Float64Array(12),pT:new Float64Array(12),pM:new Uint8Array(12)};}
function sameEdges(E,A){
const a=E.map(e=>e[0]*6+e[1]).sort((x,y)=>x-y).join(','),b=[];
A.mods.forEach((ms,t)=>{for(const s of ms)b.push(s*6+t);});
return a===b.sort((x,y)=>x-y).join(',');
}
function fmCopy(d,P){
if(!d){d={};for(const k in P){const x=P[k];d[k]=Array.isArray(x)?x.map(o=>(o&&typeof o==='object'?(Array.isArray(o)?o.slice():fmCopy(null,o)):o)):x&&typeof x==='object'?fmCopy(null,x):x;}return d;}
for(const k in P){const x=P[k];
if(Array.isArray(x)){if(!Array.isArray(d[k])||d[k].length!==x.length){d[k]=fmCopy(null,{a:x}).a;continue;}const y=d[k];for(let i=0;i<x.length;i++){const o=x[i];if(o&&typeof o==='object'){if(Array.isArray(o)){if(Array.isArray(y[i])&&y[i].length===o.length)for(let j=0;j<o.length;j++)y[i][j]=o[j];else y[i]=o.slice();}else y[i]=fmCopy(y[i]&&typeof y[i]==='object'?y[i]:null,o);}else y[i]=o;}}
else if(x&&typeof x==='object')d[k]=fmCopy(d[k]&&typeof d[k]==='object'&&!Array.isArray(d[k])?d[k]:null,x);
else d[k]=x;}
for(const k in d)if(!(k in P))delete d[k];
return d;
}
function fmxCompile(X){
const ops=X&&X.ops;if(!Array.isArray(ops)||ops.length<6)return null;
const E=fmxEdges(X),srcs=[[],[],[],[],[],[]],tg=[[],[],[],[],[],[]];
for(const[a,b]of E)if(!srcs[b].includes(a)){srcs[b].push(a);tg[a].push(b);}
for(const s of srcs)s.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
const fbK=new Int8Array(6).fill(-1),fbS=new Float64Array(6);
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const o=ops[k]||{},s=(o.fbSrc|0)-1,amt=Math.min(7,+o.fbAmt||0);
if(s>=0&&s<6){fbK[k]=s;fbS[k]=amt>0?Math.pow(2,amt-8):0;}}
if((fbK[5]===3&&sameEdges(E,ALGS[3]))||(fbK[5]===4&&sameEdges(E,ALGS[5])))fbK[5]=5;
const pre=srcs.map(s=>s.slice()),nx=tg.map(t=>t.slice());
const reach=(a,b)=>{const seen=new Uint8Array(6),st=[a];while(st.length){const x=st.pop();if(x===b)return true;if(seen[x])continue;seen[x]=1;for(const y of nx[x])st.push(y);}return false;};
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const f=fbK[k];if(f>=0&&f!==k&&fbS[k]>0&&!reach(k,f)){pre[k].push(f);nx[f].push(k);}}
const deg=pre.map(p=>p.length),ord=[],pos=new Int8Array(6);
while(ord.length<6){
let pick=-1;for(let k=5;k>=0;k--)if(deg[k]===0&&!ord.includes(k)){pick=k;break;}
if(pick<0)return null;
pos[pick]=ord.length;ord.push(pick);for(const y of nx[pick])deg[y]--;
}
let ps=false;for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const f=fbK[k];if(f>=0&&f!==k&&fbS[k]>0&&pos[f]>pos[k])ps=true;}
const car=new Uint8Array(6);let nCar=0;for(let k=0;k<6;k++)if(!tg[k].length){car[k]=1;nCar++;}
const gx={ord:Int8Array.from(ord),srcs:srcs.map(s=>Int8Array.from(s)),car,nCar,outScale:0.5/Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,nCar)),fbK,fbS,ps,
wave:new Uint8Array(6),on:new Float64Array(6),ph0:new Float64Array(6),phm:new Uint8Array(6),pit:[],am:new Float64Array(6),amG:new Float64Array(6),fmS:new Float64Array(6),sig:fmxSig({x:X}),nE:E.length,
pe:[],anyPt:false,anyFree:false,anyLoop:false};
const fops=[];
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
const o=ops[k]||{},A=envDx(o.amp),fixed=o.fixed?1:0;
fops.push({on:1,fixed,coarse:o.coarse|0,fine:+o.fine||0,det:o.det|0,ol:Math.max(0,Math.min(99,o.ol|0)),r:A.r,l:A.l,
vs:Math.max(-7,Math.min(7,o.vs|0)),rs:o.rs|0,ks:o.ks&&typeof o.ks==='object'?o.ks:undefined});
const w=o.wave|0;gx.wave[k]=w>0&&w<12?w:0;
gx.on[k]=o.on===0||!(o.ol>0)?0:1;gx.ph0[k]=(Math.max(0,Math.min(127,o.ph|0))*33554432);gx.phm[k]=PHM[o.phm]||0;
const P=o.pit;
gx.pit.push(P&&P.f==='dx'&&!fixed&&(o.pr==null||o.pr==='dx')&&Array.isArray(P.l)&&!P.l.every(x=>(x|0)===50)?envDx(P):null);
gx.pe[k]=ptCompile(o.amp,false);gx.pe[6+k]=ptCompile(P,true,o.pr);
const lA=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,o.lfoAm|0)),pmK=X.pmd!=null&&X.pmd>=0?Math.min(99,+X.pmd)/99:1,amK=X.amd!=null&&X.amd>=0?Math.min(99,+X.amd)/99:-1;
if(amK>=0&&car[k]){gx.am[k]=0;gx.amG[k]=amK*.5*(lA<=42?lA/42*.3:lA<=85? .3+(lA-42)/43*.3:.6+(lA-85)/42*.4);}
else gx.am[k]=lA*LFOAM_DB*(amK>=0?amK:1);
gx.fmS[k]=LFOFM_HT[Math.max(0,Math.min(127,o.lfoFm|0))]*pmK;
if(k===0){gx.pmK=pmK;gx.amK=amK;}
}
for(const E of gx.pe)if(E){gx.anyPt=true;if(E.free)gx.anyFree=true;if(E.la>=0)gx.anyLoop=true;}
return{fm:{trans:X.trans|0,ops:fops,peg:null,x:true},alg:{car,mods:gx.srcs,fbDst:-1,fbSrc:-1,nCar},gx};
}
function mtof(n){return 440*Math.pow(2,(n-69)/12);}
const LT0=-48,LTR=8,LTN=(176-LT0)*LTR,FREQ=new Float64Array(LTN+2),GTAN=new Float64Array(LTN+2);
for(let i=0;i<LTN+2;i++){const f=mtof(LT0+i/LTR);FREQ[i]=f;GTAN[i]=Math.tan(Math.PI*Math.min(f,.45*SR)/SR);}
function lut(T,n){let x=(n-LT0)*LTR;if(x<0)x=0;else if(x>LTN)x=LTN;const i=x|0;return T[i]+(T[i+1]-T[i])*(x-i);}
function clamp(x,a,b){return x<a?a:x>b?b:x;}
function tg(v){const a=v*v;return a<9?(27+a)/(27+9*a):1/Math.sqrt(a);}

return{

function envCoefs(E,a,d,s,r,curve){
const ratioA=0.02+2*(1-curve)*(1-curve);
const ratioDR=Math.pow(10,-4+3.5*(1-curve));
const k=(t,ratio)=>Math.exp(-Math.log((1+ratio)/ratio)/Math.max(1,t*SR));
E.aC=k(a,ratioA);E.aT=1+ratioA;
E.dC=k(d,ratioDR);E.dT=s-ratioDR;
E.rC=k(r,ratioDR);E.rT=-ratioDR;
E.s=s;
E.aCB=Math.pow(E.aC,CR);E.dCB=Math.pow(E.dC,CR);E.rCB=Math.pow(E.rC,CR);
}
function newEnv(){return{aC:0,aT:1,dC:0,dT:0,rC:0,rT:0,s:1,aCB:0,dCB:0,rCB:0};}
function envBlock(E,st,out){
if(st===1){out=E.aT+(out-E.aT)*E.aCB;if(out>=1){out=1;st=2;}}
else if(st===2){out=E.dT+(out-E.dT)*E.dCB;if(out<=E.s){out=E.s;st=3;}}
else if(st===3)out+=(E.s-out)*.2;
else if(st===4){out=E.rT+(out-E.rT)*E.rCB;if(out<=0){out=0;st=0;}}
ENV_ST=st;return out;
}

function wavePhi(w,pw){return w===W_SAW? .5:w===W_PULSE?pw/2-.25:w===W_TRI? .25:0;}
function cloneVoice(v){
const c=new Voice(v.idx);
for(const k of Object.keys(v)){const x=v[k],y=c[k];

return c;
}

function mkwAdd(m){

sets.push(L);}
MKWT[n]={r:+m.r||1,o:m.o?1:0,s:sets,N:Ls.slice(),e:m.o&&m.e&&m.e.P?m.e:null};
}

function mkEnvA(e,k,F){
if(e.t===2){
if(k&(k-1))return 0;
const x=k*F/2,o=Math.log2(x/110),E=e.E;let L;
if(o<=0)L=E[0]+6*o;else if(o>=6)L=E[6]-8*(o-6);else{const j=Math.floor(o),t=o-j;L=E[j]+(E[j+1]-E[j])*t;}
return Math.pow(10,L/20);
}
const h=k*F/e.hz,Ld=e.Ld,K=e.K;
if(h<=1)return Math.pow(10,(Ld[1]-12*Math.log2(1/h))/20);
if(h<=K){const m=Math.min(K-1,Math.floor(h)),t=Math.log(h/m)/Math.log((m+1)/m);return Math.pow(10,(Ld[m]+(Ld[m+1]-Ld[m])*t)/20);}
return e.roll?e.ab*Math.pow(h/22.6,e.s/6.02):0;
}
function mkntLv(p,r){const f=Math.min(440*Math.pow(2,(p-69)/12),.45*SR),q=Math.log2(f/r)+MK_WQ;return q>0?Math.min(9,Math.ceil(q)):0;}
function mkntG(e,F){
const g=e.g;if(!g||g.length<2)return g&&g[0]?g[0]:1;
let o=Math.log2(F/e.f0);if(o<=0)return g[0];if(o>=g.length-1)return g[g.length-1];
const j=Math.floor(o),t=o-j;return Math.exp(Math.log(g[j])*(1-t)+Math.log(g[j+1])*t);
}
function mkntN(W,L){return W.N[L];}
function mkntScr(N){
let T=MKNT_X[N];if(T)return T;
T=MKNT_X[N]={c:new Float64Array(N/2),s:new Float64Array(N/2),re:new Float64Array(N),im:new Float64Array(N)};
for(let k=0;k<N/2;k++){T.c[k]=Math.cos(2*Math.PI*k/N);T.s[k]=Math.sin(2*Math.PI*k/N);}
return T;
}
function mkntRun(j,bud){
const W=j.W,e=W.e;
for(;;){
const L=j.l,N=mkntN(W,L),X=mkntScr(N),re=X.re,im=X.im;
if(j.ph===0){
const K=Math.min(L>=10?1:512>>L,N/2-1),P=e.P;
if(j.ka===1){re.fill(0);im.fill(0);bud-=N>>3;}
while(j.ka<=K&&bud>0){const k=j.ka++,a=mkEnvA(e,k,j.F);if(a){re[k]=a*Math.sin(P[k]);im[k]=-a*Math.cos(P[k]);}bud-=4;}
if(j.ka<=K)return 0;
for(let i=1,q=0;i<N;i++){let b=N>>1;for(;q&b;b>>=1)q^=b;q^=b;if(i<q){let x=re[i];re[i]=re[q];re[q]=x;x=im[i];im[i]=im[q];im[q]=x;}}
bud-=N>>2;j.ph=1;j.len=2;j.i0=0;
if(bud<=0)return 0;
}
const C=X.c,S=X.s;
while(j.len<=N){
const len=j.len,h=len>>1,st=N/len;
for(;j.i0<N&&bud>0;j.i0+=len){const i=j.i0;
for(let q=0;q<h;q++){const c=C[q*st],s=S[q*st],k=i+q,l=k+h,tr=re[l]*c-im[l]*s,ti=re[l]*s+im[l]*c;re[l]=re[k]-tr;im[l]=im[k]-ti;re[k]+=tr;im[k]+=ti;}
bud-=h;}
if(j.i0<N)return 0;
j.len=len<<1;j.i0=0;
}
const t=new Float32Array(N+1),g=j.g;for(let i=0;i<N;i++)t[i]=re[i]*g;t[N]=t[0];
j.L[L]=t;bud-=N>>2;j.l++;j.ph=0;j.ka=1;
if(j.l>j.hi){const A=j.L;for(let l=0;l<11;l++)A[l]=A[Math.max(j.lo,Math.min(j.hi,l))];j.t={n:j.n,P:j.P,L:A};j.W=null;return bud;}
if(bud<=0)return 0;
}
}
function mkntReq(n,W,key,P){
let j=MKNT.get(key);
if(j){if(j.t){MKNT.delete(key);MKNT.set(key,j);}j.want=currentFrame;return j;}
const F=440*Math.pow(2,(P-69)/12),lo=mkntLv(P-MK_NTR,W.r),hi=Math.min(10,mkntLv(P+MK_NTR,W.r)+1);
j={k:key,n,P,F,W,lo,hi,l:lo,ph:0,ka:1,len:2,i0:0,L:null,t:null,want:currentFrame,g:mkntG(W.e,F)};
MKNT.set(key,j);MKNT_Q.push(j);
if(MKNT.size>MK_NTMAX)for(const[k,x]of MKNT)if(x.t){MKNT.delete(k);break;}
return j;
}
function mkntWork(){
if(currentFrame===MKNT_F)return;
MKNT_F=currentFrame;const b0=MKNT_BF<60000?512:16384;let bud=b0;
while(MKNT_Q.length&&bud>0){
const j=MKNT_Q[0];
if(currentFrame-j.want>.2*SR){MKNT_Q.shift();MKNT.delete(j.k);continue;}
if(!j.L)j.L=[null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null];
bud=mkntRun(j,bud);
if(j.t)MKNT_Q.shift();
}
MKNT_BF+=b0-Math.max(0,bud);
}

function mkwLv(L,a,w,ph){
const A=L[a],na=A.length-1,xa=ph*na,ia=xa|0,ya=A[ia]+(A[ia+1]-A[ia])*(xa-ia);
if(w<=0)return ya;
const B=L[a+1],nb=B.length-1,xb=ph*nb,ib=xb|0;
return ya+(B[ib]+(B[ib+1]-B[ib])*(xb-ib)-ya)*w;
}
function mkwRd(W,a,w,j,o,ph){
if(!W.o)return mkwLv(W.s[0],a,w,ph);

}
function mkSaw(t,dt){let a=2*t-1;if(t<dt){const x=t/dt;a-=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t>1-dt){const x=(t-1)/dt;a-=x*x+x+x+1;}return a;}
function mkT(){

f:{type:0,cut:127,res:0,eg:0,vel:0,kbd:0},a:{lvl:127,pan:0,gate:0,dist:0,vel:0,kbd:0},
e1:{a:0,d:64,s:0,r:0,rst:1},e2:{a:0,d:64,s:127,r:8,rst:1},
l1:{w:2,ks:0,sync:0,f:64,note:6},l2:{w:2,ks:0,sync:0,f:64,note:6},
p:[{s:0,d:0,i:0},{s:2,d:4,i:0},{s:3,d:0,i:0},{s:4,d:5,i:0}],seq:mkSqT()};
}
const MK_DLN=Math.ceil(6*SR)+4;
const MK_DXF=Math.round(.04*SR);
const MK_FXF=Math.round(.03*SR);
let MK_MDN=64;while(MK_MDN<.0125*SR+8)MK_MDN<<=1;
const MK_MDM=MK_MDN-1,MK_TQ=3e-6;
const MK_KQ=Math.max(1,Math.round(.01*SR/128));
function mkRdM(b,w,d){const x=w-d,i=Math.floor(x),f=x-i,xm=b[(i-1)&MK_MDM],x0=b[i&MK_MDM],x1=b[(i+1)&MK_MDM],x2=b[(i+2)&MK_MDM];
return x0+.5*f*(x1-xm+f*(2*xm-5*x0+4*x1-x2+f*(3*(x0-x1)+x2-xm)));}
function mkApC(r,p){const fc=MKW.pha.lo*Math.exp(r*(.5+.5*SINE[(p*SINE_N)|0])),t=Math.tan(Math.PI*Math.min(fc,.45*SR)*ISR);return(t-1)/(t+1);}
function mkCs(x){return Math.cos(x*1.5707963267948966);}
const MK_FQ=128,MK_SMQ=1-Math.exp(-MK_FQ/(.005*SR)),MK_SME=1-Math.exp(-CR/(.005*SR));
function mkSmv(a,b){const d=b-a;return d<1e-6&&d>-1e-6?b:a+d*MK_SMQ;}
function mkSme(a,b){const d=b-a;return d<1e-6&&d>-1e-6?b:a+d*MK_SME;}
const MK_SMW=1-Math.exp(-MK_FQ/(.02*SR));
function mkSmw(a,b){const d=b-a;return d<1e-5&&d>-1e-5?b:a+d*MK_SMW;}
function mkFl(s){return s<1e-15&&s>-1e-15?0:s;}
class MkFx{
constructor(host){
this.h=host;
this.mt=0;this.ms=64;this.md=0;this.dt=0;this.dsy=0;this.dtm=64;this.dn=8;this.dd=0;
this.lf=320;this.lg=0;this.hf=6000;this.hg=0;this.lfT=this.hfT=NaN;this.lfL=this.hfL=0;
this.sMd=0;this.sDd=0;this.sLg=0;this.sHg=0;this.sLf=Math.log(320);this.sHf=Math.log(6000);
this.w0=0;this.lv0=0;this.sW=0;this.mDh=0;
this.mOn=false;this.dOn=false;this.lOn=false;this.hOn=false;
this.ph=0;this.frz=-1;this.mCur=0;this.mOld=-1;this.mX=0;this.mPend=-1;this.dPend=-1;
this.cL=new Float32Array(MK_MDN);this.cR=new Float32Array(MK_MDN);this.eM=new Float32Array(MK_MDN);this.mw=0;
this.cd=new Float64Array(2);this.ed=new Float64Array(6);this.ap=new Float64Array(12);this.pa=new Float64Array(4);this.py=new Float64Array(2);
this.dL=null;this.dR=null;this.dw=0;this.dA=0;this.dB=0;this.dX=-1;this.dWr=0;this.dCur=0;this.dOld=-1;this.dTX=0;
this.q=new Float64Array(8);this.lc=new Float64Array(6);this.hc=new Float64Array(6);this.lk=[NaN,NaN];this.hk=[NaN,NaN];
const Q=MK_FQ;this.bL=new Float32Array(Q);this.bR=new Float32Array(Q);this.wL=new Float64Array(Q);this.wR=new Float64Array(Q);this.oL=new Float64Array(Q);this.oR=new Float64Array(Q);
this.qf=-1;this.pend=false;
this.quiet=1e9;this.tin=null;this.tinP=0;this.bytes=0;this.kq=0;
}
load(pm){
const n=(o,k,d)=>(o&&typeof o[k]==='number'&&isFinite(o[k])?o[k]:d);
const m=pm&&pm.mfx,d=pm&&pm.dly,e=pm&&pm.eq;
this.set('mfx','type',n(m,'type',0));this.ms=n(m,'spd',64);this.md=n(m,'dep',0);
this.set('dly','type',n(d,'type',0));this.dsy=n(d,'sync',0);this.dtm=n(d,'time',64);this.dn=n(d,'note',8);this.dd=n(d,'dep',0);
this.lf=n(e,'lf',320);this.lg=n(e,'lg',0);this.hf=n(e,'hf',6000);this.hg=n(e,'hg',0);
this.alloc();
}
set(b,k,v){
if(b==='mfx'){if(k==='type'){const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Math.round(v)));this.mt=t;if(this.mOld>=0)this.mPend=t;else this.mType_(t);}
else if(k==='spd')this.ms=v;else if(k==='dep')this.md=v;}
else if(b==='dly'){if(k==='type'){const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Math.round(v)));this.dt=t;if(this.dOld>=0)this.dPend=t;else this.dType_(t);}
else if(k==='sync')this.dsy=v;else if(k==='time')this.dtm=v;else if(k==='note')this.dn=v;else if(k==='dep'){this.dd=v;this.alloc();}}
else if(b==='eq'){if(k==='lf')this.lf=v;else if(k==='lg')this.lg=v;else if(k==='hf')this.hf=v;else if(k==='hg')this.hg=v;}
}
mType_(t){if(t===this.mCur)return;if(this.mOn){this.mOld=this.mCur;this.mX=0;this.mClear(t);if(t===1)this.ed[0]=-1;else if(t===0)this.cd.fill(-1);else this.pa.fill(NaN);}this.mCur=t;}
dType_(t){if(t===this.dCur)return;if(this.dOn){this.dOld=this.dCur;this.dTX=0;}this.dCur=t;}
alloc(){
if(this.dL||!(this.dd>0))return;
this.dL=new Float32Array(MK_DLN);this.dR=new Float32Array(MK_DLN);this.bytes=8*MK_DLN;
try{this.h.port.postMessage({mkFxMem:this.bytes});}catch(e){}
}
mClear(t){
if(t===0){this.cL.fill(0);this.cR.fill(0);}else if(t===1)this.eM.fill(0);else{this.ap.fill(0);this.py.fill(0);}
}
active(){return this.md>0||this.sMd>0||this.sW>0||(this.dL!==null&&(this.dd>0||this.sDd>0))||this.lg!==0||this.sLg!==0||this.hg!==0||this.sHg!==0||this.tin!==null;}
hot(){if(!this.active())return false;if(this.tin||this.kq)return true;const dl=this.dOn?Math.max(this.dA,this.dX>=0?this.dB:0)+MK_DXF:0;return this.quiet<dl+4096;}
idle(n){this.kq=0;if(this.frz<0)this.ph=(this.ph+MKW.fxHz(this.ms)*n*ISR)%1;}
dLen(){const W=MKW;const D=(this.dsy>=.5?W.DSYNC[Math.max(0,Math.min(14,Math.round(this.dn)))]*4*60/this.h.bpm:W.dlyS(Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.dtm))))*SR;
return Math.max(1,Math.min(MK_DLN-3,Math.round(D)));}
mFresh(t){this.mClear(t);if(t===1)this.ed[0]=-1;else if(t===0)this.cd.fill(-1);else this.pa.fill(NaN);}
settle(){
const W=MKW,mdT=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.md)),ddT=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.dd));
if(this.mOld>=0||this.mPend>=0||this.mCur!==this.mt){if(this.mCur!==this.mt)this.mFresh(this.mt);this.mOld=-1;this.mPend=-1;this.mX=0;this.mCur=this.mt;}
this.sMd=mdT;this.mDh=mdT;
if(mdT>0){this.mFresh(this.mCur);this.sW=this.w0=W.fxW(mdT);this.mOn=true;}else{this.sW=this.w0=0;this.mOn=false;}
this.sDd=this.dL!==null?ddT:0;this.dOld=-1;this.dPend=-1;this.dTX=0;this.dCur=this.dt;this.dX=-1;
if(this.dL!==null&&ddT>0){if(!this.dOn)this.dWr=0;this.dA=this.dLen();this.lv0=ddT/127;this.dOn=true;}else{this.lv0=0;this.dOn=false;}
const lgT=Math.max(-12,Math.min(12,this.lg)),hgT=Math.max(-12,Math.min(12,this.hg)),q=this.q;
this.sLg=lgT;this.sHg=hgT;this.sLf=Math.log(Math.max(40,Math.min(1000,this.lf)));this.sHf=Math.log(Math.max(1000,Math.min(18000,this.hf)));
if(lgT!==0){if(!this.lOn)q[0]=q[1]=q[2]=q[3]=0;this.lOn=true;}else this.lOn=false;
if(hgT!==0){if(!this.hOn)q[4]=q[5]=q[6]=q[7]=0;this.hOn=true;}else this.hOn=false;
this.kq=0;
}
kill(){if(this.hot()){if(!this.kq)this.kq=MK_KQ;}else this.wipe();}
wipe(){this.dWr=0;this.mClear(0);this.mClear(1);this.mClear(2);this.cd.fill(-1);this.ed[0]=-1;this.pa.fill(NaN);this.kq=0;}
run(Lo,Ro){
const xL=this.bL,xR=this.bR,n=MK_FQ,W=MKW,off=0,kq=this.kq,kEnd=kq===1;this.pend=false;this.qf=-1;if(kq)this.kq--;
if(this.tin){const T=this.tin,p=this.tinP;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const j=p+i;if(j<T.l.length){xL[i]+=T.l[j];xR[i]+=T.r[j];}}this.tinP+=n;if(this.tinP>=T.l.length)this.tin=null;}
const sm=mkSmv;
const mdT=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.md));this.sMd=sm(this.sMd,mdT);this.sDd=sm(this.sDd,Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.dd)));
const mOn=this.md>0||this.sMd>0||this.sW>0;
if(mOn&&!this.mOn){this.mClear(this.mCur);this.mOld=-1;this.mPend=-1;this.w0=0;this.sW=0;this.sMd=mdT;this.cd.fill(-1);this.ed[0]=-1;this.pa.fill(NaN);}
this.mOn=mOn;
if(mOn){
const ph0=this.frz>=0?this.frz:this.ph,ph1=this.frz>=0?this.frz:(this.ph+W.fxHz(this.ms)*n*ISR)%1;this.ph=ph1;
if(mdT>0)this.mDh=this.sMd;
this.sW=mkSmw(this.sW,W.fxW(mdT));
const dep=mdT>0?this.sMd:this.mDh,w0=this.w0,w1=kq?this.w0*(kq-1)/kq:this.sW,wL=this.wL,wR=this.wR;
this.mType(this.mCur,xL,xR,wL,wR,dep,ph0,ph1,n);
if(this.mOld>=0){
const oL=this.oL,oR=this.oR;this.mType(this.mOld,xL,xR,oL,oR,dep,ph0,ph1,n);
const a0=this.mX/MK_FXF,a1=Math.min(1,(this.mX+n)/MK_FXF),c0=mkCs(a0),c1=mkCs(a1),s0=mkCs(1-a0),s1=mkCs(1-a1);
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const u=(i+1)/n;wL[i]=wL[i]*(s0+(s1-s0)*u)+oL[i]*(c0+(c1-c0)*u);wR[i]=wR[i]*(s0+(s1-s0)*u)+oR[i]*(c0+(c1-c0)*u);}
this.mX+=n;if(this.mX>=MK_FXF){this.mOld=-1;if(this.mPend>=0){const p=this.mPend;this.mPend=-1;this.mType_(p);}}
}
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const w=w0+(w1-w0)*(i+1)/n;xL[i]=xL[i]*(1-w)+wL[i]*w;xR[i]=xR[i]*(1-w)+wR[i]*w;}
this.w0=w1;
this.mw=(this.mw+n)&MK_MDM;
if(mdT===0&&this.sW===0&&this.sMd===0)this.mOn=false;
if(kEnd){this.mOn=false;this.sW=0;this.mOld=-1;if(this.mPend>=0){this.mCur=this.mPend;this.mPend=-1;}}
}
const dOn=this.dL!==null&&(this.dd>0||this.sDd>0),D=this.dLen();
if(dOn&&!this.dOn){this.dWr=0;this.dA=D;this.dX=-1;this.dOld=-1;this.dPend=-1;this.lv0=0;}
this.dOn=dOn;
if(dOn){
if(this.dX<0&&Math.abs(D-this.dA)>1e-3){this.dB=D;this.dX=0;}
const L=this.dL,R=this.dR,N=MK_DLN,lv0=this.lv0,lv1=this.sDd/127,fbk=W.dlyFb,t=this.dCur,to=this.dOld;
const xf=this.dX>=0,A=this.dA,B=this.dB,a0=xf?this.dX/MK_DXF:0,a1=xf?Math.min(1,(this.dX+n)/MK_DXF):0;
const ca0=mkCs(a0),ca1=mkCs(a1),sb0=mkCs(1-a0),sb1=mkCs(1-a1);
const r0=to>=0?this.dTX/MK_FXF:1,r1=to>=0?Math.min(1,(this.dTX+n)/MK_FXF):1;
let w=this.dw,wr=this.dWr;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
const u=(i+1)/n,lv=lv0+(lv1-lv0)*u,fb=fbk*lv;
let x=w-A;if(x<0)x+=N;let j=x|0,f=x-j,j1=j+1===N?0:j+1;
let rl=0,rr=0;if(A<=wr){rl=L[j]+(L[j1]-L[j])*f;rr=R[j]+(R[j1]-R[j])*f;}
if(xf){
let pl=0,pr=0;if(B<=wr){x=w-B;if(x<0)x+=N;j=x|0;f=x-j;j1=j+1===N?0:j+1;pl=L[j]+(L[j1]-L[j])*f;pr=R[j]+(R[j1]-R[j])*f;}
const ga=ca0+(ca1-ca0)*u,gb=sb0+(sb1-sb0)*u;rl=rl*ga+pl*gb;rr=rr*ga+pr*gb;
}
if(kq){const g=(kq-u)/MK_KQ;rl*=g;rr*=g;}
const il=xL[i],ir=xR[i];
let ul,ur;
if(t===0){ul=il+fb*rl;ur=ir+fb*rr;}else if(t===1){ul=il+fb*rr;ur=ir+fb*rl;}else{ul=.5*(il+ir)+fb*rr;ur=fb*rl;}
if(to>=0){const g=r0+(r1-r0)*u,gn=mkCs(1-g),go=mkCs(g);let vl,vr;
if(to===0){vl=il+fb*rl;vr=ir+fb*rr;}else if(to===1){vl=il+fb*rr;vr=ir+fb*rl;}else{vl=.5*(il+ir)+fb*rr;vr=fb*rl;}
ul=ul*gn+vl*go;ur=ur*gn+vr*go;}
L[w]=ul<1e-15&&ul>-1e-15?0:ul;R[w]=ur<1e-15&&ur>-1e-15?0:ur;
xL[i]=il+lv*rl;xR[i]=ir+lv*rr;
if(++w===N)w=0;if(wr<N)wr++;
}
this.dw=w;this.dWr=kEnd?0:wr;this.lv0=lv1;
if(xf){this.dX+=n;if(this.dX>=MK_DXF){this.dA=this.dB;this.dX=-1;}}
if(to>=0){this.dTX+=n;if(this.dTX>=MK_FXF){this.dOld=-1;if(this.dPend>=0){const p=this.dPend;this.dPend=-1;this.dType_(p);}}}
}
const q=this.q,lgT=Math.max(-12,Math.min(12,this.lg)),hgT=Math.max(-12,Math.min(12,this.hg));
const lfT=this.lfT===this.lf?this.lfL:(this.lfT=this.lf,this.lfL=Math.log(Math.max(40,Math.min(1000,this.lf)))),hfT=this.hfT===this.hf?this.hfL:(this.hfT=this.hf,this.hfL=Math.log(Math.max(1000,Math.min(18000,this.hf))));
if(!this.lOn)this.sLf=lfT;if(!this.hOn)this.sHf=hfT;
for(let c=0;c<n;c+=CR){
this.sLg=mkSme(this.sLg,lgT);this.sHg=mkSme(this.sHg,hgT);this.sLf=mkSme(this.sLf,lfT);this.sHf=mkSme(this.sHf,hfT);
const lOn=lgT!==0||this.sLg!==0,hOn=hgT!==0||this.sHg!==0;
if(lOn&&!this.lOn){q[0]=q[1]=q[2]=q[3]=0;}if(hOn&&!this.hOn){q[4]=q[5]=q[6]=q[7]=0;}
this.lOn=lOn;this.hOn=hOn;
if(lOn){this.shelf(0,this.sLf,this.sLg);this.eqRun(this.lc,0,xL,xR,c,c+CR);}
if(hOn){this.shelf(1,this.sHf,this.sHg);this.eqRun(this.hc,4,xL,xR,c,c+CR);}
}
for(let k=0;k<8;k++)if(q[k]<1e-15&&q[k]>-1e-15)q[k]=0;
let pk=0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=xL[i],b=xR[i];Lo[off+i]+=a;Ro[off+i]+=b;const m=a<0?-a:a,m2=b<0?-b:b;if(m>pk)pk=m;if(m2>pk)pk=m2;}
this.quiet=pk<MK_TQ?Math.min(1e9,this.quiet+n):0;
}
mType(t,xL,xR,oL,oR,dep,ph0,ph1,n){
const W=MKW,wi=this.mw;
if(t===0){
const hub=W.cho.hub*dep/127,fb=Math.max(0,(dep-64)/63)*W.cho.fb,cd=this.cd,cL=this.cL,cR=this.cR;
const l1=(W.cho.d+hub*(.5+.5*SINE[(ph1*SINE_N)|0]))*SR,r1=(W.cho.d+hub*(.5+.5*SINE[(((ph1+.25)%1)*SINE_N)|0]))*SR;
const l0=cd[0]<0?l1:cd[0],r0=cd[1]<0?r1:cd[1];cd[0]=l1;cd[1]=r1;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
const u=(i+1)/n,w=wi+i,yl=mkRdM(cL,w,l0+(l1-l0)*u),yr=mkRdM(cR,w,r0+(r1-r0)*u);
const a=xL[i]+fb*yl,b=xR[i]+fb*yr;cL[w&MK_MDM]=a<1e-15&&a>-1e-15?0:a;cR[w&MK_MDM]=b<1e-15&&b>-1e-15?0:b;
oL[i]=yl;oR[i]=yr;
}
}else if(t===1){
const hs=W.ens.hub*dep/127*SR,b=W.ens.d*SR,e=this.eM,ed=this.ed;
const n0=b+hs*SINE[(ph1*SINE_N)|0],n1=b+hs*SINE[(((ph1+1/3)%1)*SINE_N)|0],n2=b+hs*SINE[(((ph1+2/3)%1)*SINE_N)|0];
const p0=ed[0]<0?n0:ed[3],p1=ed[0]<0?n1:ed[4],p2=ed[0]<0?n2:ed[5];ed[0]=1;ed[3]=n0;ed[4]=n1;ed[5]=n2;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
const u=(i+1)/n,w=wi+i,m=.5*(xL[i]+xR[i]);e[w&MK_MDM]=m;
const a=mkRdM(e,w,p0+(n0-p0)*u),c=mkRdM(e,w,p1+(n1-p1)*u),d=mkRdM(e,w,p2+(n2-p2)*u);
oL[i]=a+.5*c;oR[i]=d+.5*c;
}
}else{
const h=dep/127,fb=h*W.pha.fb,rt=Math.log(W.pha.hi/W.pha.lo),pa=this.pa,ap=this.ap,py=this.py;
const aL1=mkApC(rt*h,ph1),aR1=mkApC(rt*h,(ph1+.25)%1),aL0=pa[0]!==pa[0]?aL1:pa[0],aR0=pa[1]!==pa[1]?aR1:pa[1];pa[0]=aL1;pa[1]=aR1;
for(let c=0;c<2;c++){
const x=c?xR:xL,o=c?oR:oL,a0=c?aR0:aL0,a1=c?aR1:aL1,z=c*6;
let y=py[c],s0=ap[z],s1=ap[z+1],s2=ap[z+2],s3=ap[z+3],s4=ap[z+4],s5=ap[z+5];
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
const a=a0+(a1-a0)*(i+1)/n;let v=x[i]+fb*y,r;
r=a*v+s0;s0=v-a*r;v=r;r=a*v+s1;s1=v-a*r;v=r;r=a*v+s2;s2=v-a*r;v=r;
r=a*v+s3;s3=v-a*r;v=r;r=a*v+s4;s4=v-a*r;v=r;r=a*v+s5;s5=v-a*r;v=r;
y=v;o[i]=v;
}
const fl=mkFl;
py[c]=fl(y);ap[z]=fl(s0);ap[z+1]=fl(s1);ap[z+2]=fl(s2);ap[z+3]=fl(s3);ap[z+4]=fl(s4);ap[z+5]=fl(s5);
}
}
}
shelf(hi,lf,db){
const K=hi?this.hk:this.lk;if(K[0]===lf&&K[1]===db)return;K[0]=lf;K[1]=db;
const c=hi?this.hc:this.lc,A=Math.pow(10,db/40),t=Math.tan(Math.PI*Math.min(Math.exp(lf),.45*SR)*ISR),k=1/MKW.eqQ;
const g=hi?t*Math.sqrt(A):t/Math.sqrt(A),a1=1/(1+g*(g+k)),a2=g*a1,a3=g*a2;
c[0]=a1;c[1]=a2;c[2]=a3;
if(hi){c[3]=A*A;c[4]=k*(1-A)*A;c[5]=1-A*A;}else{c[3]=1;c[4]=k*(A-1);c[5]=A*A-1;}
}
eqRun(c,z,xL,xR,i0,i9){
const q=this.q,a1=c[0],a2=c[1],a3=c[2],m0=c[3],m1=c[4],m2=c[5];
for(let ch=0;ch<2;ch++){
const x=ch?xR:xL,o=z+2*ch;let i1=q[o],i2=q[o+1];

q[o]=i1;q[o+1]=i2;
}
}
}
function mkHash(a,b,c){
let h=(a>>>0)^0x9E3779B9;h=Math.imul(h^(h>>>16),0x85ebca6b)>>>0;h^=(b+0x632be5ab)>>>0;
h=Math.imul(h^(h>>>13),0xc2b2ae35)>>>0;h^=(c*0x27d4eb2f)>>>0;h=Math.imul(h^(h>>>16),0x165667b1)>>>0;

}
function mkRnd(v){let x=v.rng;x^=x<<13;x^=x>>>17;x^=x<<5;v.rng=x>>>0;return(x>>>0)/4294967296;}
function mkEnvC(E,a,d,s,r){
const T=1+MK_RA,ta=Math.max(1e-5,a)*SR/CR,tau=n=>(n>0?Math.exp(-1/n):0);
E.aC=tau(ta/-Math.log(1-.9/T));E.aT=T;
const td=Math.max(1e-5,d)*SR/CR,sp=1-s;
E.dC=sp>1e-6?tau(td/-Math.log((.1*sp+MK_RD)/(sp+MK_RD))):0;E.dT=s-MK_RD;
E.rC=tau(Math.max(1e-5,r)*SR/CR/-Math.log((.1+MK_RD)/(1+MK_RD)));E.rT=-MK_RD;E.s=s;
}
const MK_LHZ=new Float64Array(127*16+2);for(let i=0;i<MK_LHZ.length;i++)MK_LHZ[i]=MKW.lfoHz(i<2032?i/16:127);
function mkLHz(x){x=x>0?(x<127?x:127):0;const y=x*16,i=y|0,a=MK_LHZ[i];return y===i?a:a+(MK_LHZ[i+1]-a)*(y-i);}
let MK_ST=0;
function mkEnvB(E,st,o){
if(st===1){o=E.aT+(o-E.aT)*E.aC;if(o>=1){o=1;st=2;}}
if(st===2){o=E.dT+(o-E.dT)*E.dC;if(o<=E.s){o=E.s;st=3;}}
else if(st===3)o+=(E.s-o)*MK_SM;
else if(st===4){o=E.rT+(o-E.rT)*E.rC;if(o<=0){o=0;st=0;}}
MK_ST=st;return o;
}
function mkLfoV(n,w,ph,sh){
if(w===3)return sh;

}

}

lbEvM(p,x,r){

lbStepM(){

pInit(){const P=this.T.p,A=this.pAct;A.length=0;for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const c=this.pc[k];c.s=Math.max(0,Math.min(7,Math.round(P[k].s)));c.d=Math.max(0,Math.min(7,Math.round(P[k].d)));c.si=P[k].i;if(c.si!==0)A.push(k);}}
pStep(){

for(let k=0;k<4;k++){

}

l2x(x){return MKW.lfoHz(Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.T.l2.f+x)));}
need(n){
const f=currentFrame,q=this.wReq[n];if(q!=null&&f-q<SR)return;this.wReq[n]=f;
try{this.h.port.postMessage({mkwNeed:n});}catch(e){}
}
load(tt){
const t=tt&&typeof tt==='object'?tt:null,T=mkT();
if(t)for(const k in T){const x=t[k];if(x==null)continue;
if(k==='p'){if(Array.isArray(x))for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const q=x[j];if(q&&typeof q==='object')for(const f of['s','d','i'])if(typeof q[f]==='number'&&isFinite(q[f]))T.p[j][f]=q[f];}continue;}
if(k==='seq'){mkSqLoad(T.seq,x);continue;}
if(typeof T[k]==='object'){for(const j in T[k])if(typeof x[j]==='number'&&isFinite(x[j]))T[k][j]=x[j];}else if(typeof x==='number'&&isFinite(x))T[k]=x;}
const sounding=this.V.some(v=>v.on&&!v.ghost),oldSt=this.st,re=MK_REASSIGN,T0=this.T;
const reas=sounding&&(T.asg!==T0.asg||T.prio!==T0.prio||T.trig!==T0.trig);
this.T=T;this.derive();this.smRun=true;this.pRun=true;this.sqDirty=true;
const ns=this.structOf();
if(sounding&&!reas&&(ns.w1!==oldSt.w1||ns.w2!==oldSt.w2||ns.mod!==oldSt.mod||ns.ft!==oldSt.ft||ns.gate!==oldSt.gate))this.structXf(ns);
else this.setStruct(ns);
if(!this.sm)this.smInit();
void re;return reas;
}
set(o){
const T=this.T;let st=false,reas=false,env=false;this.smRun=true;
for(const path in o){
const v=+o[path];if(!isFinite(v))continue;
const q=path.split('.');if(q[0]!=='t'||q[1]!==String(this.ti))continue;
const k1=q[2],k2=q[3];
if(k1==='p'){const P=T.p[+k2],f=q[4];if(P&&(f==='s'||f==='d'||f==='i')){P[f]=v;this.pRun=true;}continue;}
if(k1==='seq'){mkSqSet(T.seq,q.slice(3),v);this.sqDirty=true;continue;}
if(k2!=null){if(!T[k1]||typeof T[k1]!=='object')continue;T[k1][k2]=v;}else{if(!(k1 in T)||typeof T[k1]==='object')continue;T[k1]=v;}
const key=k2!=null?k1+'.'+k2:k1;
if(MK_STRUCT[key])st=true;if(MK_REASSIGN[key])reas=true;if(k1==='e1'||k1==='e2'||k1==='l1'||k1==='l2'||k1==='porta')env=true;
}
if(env||reas)this.derive();
if(st){const ns=this.structOf(),s0=this.st;if(ns.w1!==s0.w1||ns.w2!==s0.w2||ns.mod!==s0.mod||ns.ft!==s0.ft||ns.gate!==s0.gate){if(this.V.some(v=>v.on&&!v.ghost))this.structXf(ns);else this.setStruct(ns);}}
return reas;
}
sounding(){return this.V.some(v=>v.on&&!v.ghost);}
structOf(){const T=this.T,r=x=>Math.round(x);return{w1:r(T.o1.w),w2:r(T.o2.w),mod:r(T.o2.mod),ft:r(T.f.type),gate:r(T.a.gate)};}
setStruct(ns){this.st=ns;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost&&!(this.sxOn&&v.sxo))v.st=ns;}
derive(){
const T=this.T,W=MKW;
mkEnvC(this.E1,W.atk(T.e1.a),W.dec(T.e1.d),W.sus(T.e1.s),W.dec(T.e1.r));
mkEnvC(this.E2,W.atk(T.e2.a),W.dec(T.e2.d),W.sus(T.e2.s),W.dec(T.e2.r));
const pt=W.porta(Math.round(T.porta));
this.gc=pt>0?1-Math.exp(-CR/(pt/Math.LN10*SR)):1;
}
smTarget(o){
const T=this.T;o=o||new MkSm();
o.cut=T.f.cut;o.res=T.f.res;o.eg=T.f.eg;o.fvel=T.f.vel;o.kbd=T.f.kbd;o.c1=T.o1.c1;o.c2=T.o1.c2;o.m1=T.mix.o1;o.m2=T.mix.o2;o.mn=T.mix.n;

}
smInit(){this.sm=this.smTarget();}
smStep(){
if(!this.smRun)return;

s.cut=f(s.cut,t.cut);s.res=f(s.res,t.res);s.eg=f(s.eg,t.eg);s.fvel=f(s.fvel,t.fvel);s.kbd=f(s.kbd,t.kbd);s.c1=f(s.c1,t.c1);s.c2=f(s.c2,t.c2);

}
wheel(o){
if(o.bend!=null&&isFinite(o.bend)){this.bendT=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,+o.bend));this.bendR=o.ret>0?Math.abs(this.bend)/Math.max(1,o.ret*SR/CR):0;}

if(o.mw0!=null&&isFinite(o.mw0)){this.mw0=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,+o.mw0));if(!this.modLive){this.modT=this.mw0;}}
if(o.reset){this.modLive=false;this.modT=this.mw0;}
}
sqPrep(){
this.sqDirty=false;
const S=this.T.seq,r=this.rec,L=mkCl(Math.round(S.last),1,16),ty=r?0:mkCl(Math.round(S.type),0,3),O=this.sqO;let n=0;
for(let s=0;s<L;s++)O[n++]=ty===1?L-1-s:s;
if(ty===2)for(let s=L-2;s>=1;s--)O[n++]=s;else if(ty===3)for(let s=L-1;s>=0;s--)O[n++]=s;
this.sqN=n;this.sqLoop=!!r||S.run>=.5;this.sqKs=mkCl(Math.round(S.ks),0,2);
const on=(S.on>=.5||!!r)&&this.par.mode!==3,wasV=this.sqV;
this.sqV=this.sqKs===2;this.sqEv++;
if(this.sqV!==wasV&&this.sqSig!=null&&this.sounding()){
if(this.sqV){for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){v.sq=v.sq?v.sq.take(this.sq):this.sq.copy();const c=v.sxo;if(c&&c.on)c.sq=c.sq?c.sq.take(this.sq):this.sq.copy();}}
else{let vo=null;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost&&v.sq&&(!vo||v.age>vo.age))vo=v;if(vo)this.sq.take(vo.sq);}
}
const E=[],seen=new Set();let sl=null,sig=on?'on':'off';
for(let k=2;k>=0;k--){
const Q=S.q[k],tg=Math.round(Q.k);if(!(tg>=1&&tg<MK_SQN)||seen.has(tg)||(r&&r.k===k))continue;seen.add(tg);
if(tg===2){sl=Q;continue;}
const R=MKW.sqR(tg),c=new Float64Array(16);for(let s=0;s<16;s++)c[s]=mkCl(+Q.v[s]||0,-R,R);
E.push({k,tg,m:Math.round(Q.m)===0?0:1,R,v:Q.v,c});sig+='|'+k+':'+tg;
}
if(sig!==this.sqSig){
const was=this.sqSig!=null&&this.sqSig.startsWith('on'),snd=this.sounding();
if(snd&&was){this.sqFo=1;this.sqEo=this.sqE;}else{this.sqFo=0;this.sqEo=null;}
this.sqFi=snd&&on&&this.sqSig!=null?0:1;this.sqSig=sig;this.sqGen++;
}
this.sqOn=on||this.sqFo>0;
this.sqPt=false;this.sqEg=0;this.sqL=0;this.sqPm=0;
if(this.sqOn)for(const q of this.sqFo>0&&this.sqEo?E.concat(this.sqEo):E){const tg=q.tg;
if(tg===3)this.sqPt=true;else if(tg>=17&&tg<=20)this.sqEg|=1;else if(tg>=21&&tg<=24)this.sqEg|=2;else if(tg===25)this.sqL|=1;else if(tg===26)this.sqL|=2;else if(tg>=27)this.sqPm|=1<<(tg-27);}
this.sqE=on?E:[];
const res=Math.round(S.res);let C=0;
for(let s=0;s<16;s++)this.sqD[s]=MKW.SRES[mkCl(res+(sl&&this.sqOn?Math.round(mkCl(sl.v[s],-6,6)):0),0,15)]*4;
for(let j=0;j<n;j++)C+=this.sqD[O[j]];
this.sqC=C;
}
sqAdv(H,nb,abs,b){
const O=this.sqO,n=this.sqN,D=this.sqD;
if(H.j>=n){H.j%=n;H.u=0;}
if(abs){
if(!this.sqLoop&&b>=this.sqC){H.j=n-1;H.u=D[O[n-1]];H.done=true;}
else if(!this.sqLoop&&b<0){H.j=0;H.u=0;H.done=false;}
else{let x=((b%this.sqC)+this.sqC)%this.sqC,j=0;while(j<n-1&&x>=D[O[j]]){x-=D[O[j]];j++;}H.j=j;H.u=x;H.done=false;}
if(H.b===H.b&&Math.abs(b-H.b-nb)>1e-3)H.jmp=true;H.b=b;
const cy=Math.floor(b/this.sqC);if(H.st>=0&&(H.j!==H.jj||cy!==H.cy))this.sqHist(H,O[H.j]);if(cy!==H.cy)H.wr++;H.cy=cy;
}else if(!H.done){
H.u+=nb;
while(H.u>=D[O[H.j]]){H.u-=D[O[H.j]];H.j++;if(H.j>=n){if(this.sqLoop){H.j=0;H.wr++;}else{H.j=n-1;H.u=D[O[n-1]];H.done=true;break;}}this.sqHist(H,O[H.j]);}
}
if(H.st<0)this.sqHist(H,O[H.j]);H.jj=H.j;
}
sqHist(H,s){H.st=s;if(H.h.length>=64)H.h.shift();H.h.push(s+1);}
sqVal(H){
if(H.gen!==this.sqGen){
if(this.sqFo>0&&H.gen>=0){H.fo.set(H.o);H.fx=1;}else H.fx=0;
H.o.fill(0);H.gen=this.sqGen;H.a.fill(0);H.f.fill(0);H.g.fill(0);H.r.fill(1);H.d.fill(0);H.yv=0;
}
if(H.fx>0){const fo=H.fo;for(let i=0;i<=MK_SQN;i++)if(fo[i]!==0)H.o[i]=0;}
const O=this.sqO,n=this.sqN,E=this.sqE,s=O[H.j],end=H.done||(!this.sqLoop&&H.j===n-1),fi=this.sqFi;
const s2=O[H.j+1<n?H.j+1:0],fr=H.u/this.sqD[s],fresh=H.fresh,jp=H.jmp||H.ev!==this.sqEv;H.fresh=false;H.jmp=false;H.ev=this.sqEv;
for(let e=0;e<E.length;e++){
const q=E[e],k=q.k,c=q.c,a=c[s];let x;
if(q.m===0){
x=end?a:a+(c[s2]-a)*(fr>1?1:fr);
const bit=1<<k;let dd=H.d[k];
if(fresh||!(H.yv&bit))dd=0;else if(jp)dd=H.a[k]-x;
if(dd!==0){x+=dd;dd*=MK_SQD;if(dd<1e-7&&dd>-1e-7)dd=0;}
H.d[k]=dd;H.yv|=bit;H.g[k]=NaN;
}
else if(fresh){x=a;H.f[k]=a;H.g[k]=a;H.r[k]=1;}
else{
if(a!==H.g[k]){H.f[k]=H.a[k];H.g[k]=a;H.r[k]=0;}
if(H.r[k]<1){H.r[k]+=MK_SQR;if(H.r[k]>1)H.r[k]=1;}
x=H.f[k]+(H.g[k]-H.f[k])*H.r[k];
}
H.a[k]=x;if(fi<1)x*=fi;H.o[q.tg]=x;if(q.tg===5)H.o[31]=q.m===0?x:a;
}
if(H.fx>0){H.fx-=MK_SQF;const w=H.fx>0?H.fx:0,fo=H.fo;for(let i=0;i<=MK_SQN;i++)if(fo[i]!==0){H.o[i]+=fo[i]*w;if(w===0)fo[i]=0;}}
}
sqEnv(v,n,so){
const st=n?v.e2s:v.e1s,b=n?21:17,e=n?this.T.e2:this.T.e1,da=so[b],dd=so[b+1],ds=so[b+2],dr=so[b+3];
let Ev=n?v.sqE2:v.sqE1,on=n?v.sqO2:v.sqO1;const ps=n?v.sqS2:v.sqS1;
if(st!==ps||(st===3&&ds!==(n?v.sqU2:v.sqU1))){
const any=da||dd||ds||dr,W=MKW;
if(st===ps&&on&&any){const sv=W.sus(mkCl(e.s+ds,0,127));Ev.s=sv;Ev.dT=sv-MK_RD;}
else if(any){if(!Ev)Ev={};mkEnvC(Ev,W.atk(mkCl(e.a+da,0,127)),W.dec(mkCl(e.d+dd,0,127)),W.sus(mkCl(e.s+ds,0,127)),W.dec(mkCl(e.r+dr,0,127)));on=true;}
else on=false;
if(n){v.sqE2=Ev;v.sqS2=st;v.sqU2=ds;v.sqO2=on;}else{v.sqE1=Ev;v.sqS1=st;v.sqU1=ds;v.sqO1=on;}
}
return on?Ev:n?this.E2:this.E1;
}
sqPar(tg){const p=MK_SQP[tg],T=this.T;if(!p)return 0;return p[0]==='p'?T.p[+p[1]].i:p.length===1?T[p[0]]:T[p[0]][p[1]];}
sqHold(){if(!this.sqV)return this.sq;let vo=null;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost&&v.sq&&(!vo||v.age>vo.age))vo=v;return vo?vo.sq:this.sq;}
sqTick(frame,nb){
if(this.sqDirty)this.sqPrep();
if(this.sqFi<1){this.sqFi+=MK_SQF;if(this.sqFi>1)this.sqFi=1;}
if(this.sqFo>0){this.sqFo-=MK_SQF;if(this.sqFo<=0){this.sqFo=0;this.sqEo=null;this.sqDirty=true;}}
const h=this.h,abs=this.sqKs===0&&h.songStart>=0&&frame>=0;

this.sqAdv(this.sq,nb,abs,b);

}
recMsg(m){
if(!m||m.cancel){if(this.rec){this.rec=null;this.sqDirty=true;}return;}
const tg=m.tg|0;if(!(tg>=1&&tg<MK_SQN)||tg===2||this.par.mode===3)return;

}
recStep(nb){

}

}
sqInfo(){if(this.sqDirty)this.sqPrep();const H=this.sqHold();return{on:this.sqOn&&!this.rec?1:0,run:this.busy()||this.rec||(this.sqKs===0&&this.h.songStart>=0)?1:0,step:this.sqO[H.j<this.sqN?H.j:0]+1,done:H.done,rem:H.done?1e9:this.sqD[this.sqO[H.j<this.sqN?H.j:0]]-H.u,nx:(H.done?this.sqO[H.j<this.sqN?H.j:0]:H.j+1<this.sqN?this.sqO[H.j+1]:this.sqLoop?this.sqO[0]:this.sqO[H.j<this.sqN?H.j:0])+1,rec:this.rec?{k:this.rec.k,tg:this.rec.tg,step:this.rec.cur+1}:null,vals:Array.from(H.a)};}
busy(){if(this.keys.length)return true;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)return true;return false;}
count(){let n=0;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost)n++;return n;}
maxV(){return this.cv;}
uniN(){return this.cu;}
free(){
for(const v of this.V)if(!v.on&&!v.sxo)return v;
for(const v of this.V)if(!v.on)return v;
let b=null;for(const v of this.V)if(v.fade>0&&(!b||v.fadeG<b.fadeG))b=v;

this.kill(b);return b;
}
kill(v){v.on=false;const id=v.id;v.id=-1;this.endCheck(id);}
endCheck(id){this.par.endCheck(id);}
holds(id){if(this.keys.some(k=>k.id===id))return true;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.id===id)return true;return false;}
fadeOut(v,n){if(v.fade>0&&v.fade<=n)return;v.fade=n;v.fadeN=n/CR*(v.fadeG>0?1/v.fadeG:1)*CR;}
steal(v){this.fadeOut(v,MK_STEAL);v.gate=false;}
start(v,k,from,retrig){

if(fresh){
const sq=v.sq;Object.assign(v,new MkVoice(v.i));v.on=true;

}else if(retrig){

}

if(k.lk||this.lbN||v.lkS)this.lkStartM(v,k.lk||null,from);
this.lastPitch=k.tp;
}
keyReset(){if(this.T.l1.ks===1)this.g1=0;if(this.T.l2.ks===1)this.g2=0;
if(Math.round(this.T.seq.ks)!==0){if(this.V.some(v=>v.on))this.sq.reset();else this.sq.init();}}
rank(a,b){const pr=Math.round(this.T.prio);return pr===1?a.note-b.note:pr===2?b.note-a.note:b.ord-a.ord;}
noteOn(id,note,vel,lk){
if(!this.par.arp&&!this.keys.length&&!this.V.some(v=>v.on&&v.gate&&!v.ghost))this.keyReset();

}
gated(){return this.V.filter(v=>v.on&&v.gate&&!v.ghost&&v.fade===0);}
polyOn(k,finN){
const cap=this.maxV(),G=this.gated(),pr=Math.round(this.T.prio);
if(G.length>=cap){
let victim=null;
if(pr===0&&this.par.lim_>=2){for(const v of G)if(!victim||v.e2<victim.e2)victim=v;}
else if(pr===0){for(const v of G)if(!victim||v.ord<victim.ord)victim=v;}
else if(pr===1){for(const v of G)if(!victim||v.note>victim.note)victim=v;if(victim&&k.note>=victim.note)victim=null;}
else{for(const v of G)if(!victim||v.note<victim.note)victim=v;if(victim&&k.note<=victim.note)victim=null;}
if(!victim)return null;
this.steal(victim);
}
let v=null;for(const x of this.V)if(x.on&&!x.ghost&&x.fade===0&&!x.gate&&x.note===k.note)v=x;
if(v){const from=(this.gc<1||this.sqPt)&&this.lastPitch>=0?this.lastPitch:-1;this.start(v,k,from===k.tp?-1:from,true);v.fin=finN;v.finN=finN||1;return v;}
const live=this.V.filter(x=>x.on&&!x.ghost&&x.fade===0);
if(live.length>=cap){let q=null;for(const x of live)if(!x.gate&&(!q||x.e2<q.e2))q=x;if(q)this.steal(q);}

}
line(){return this.V.filter(v=>v.on&&!v.ghost&&v.fade===0&&v.isLine);}
monoSel(isNew){
const T=this.T,L=this.line();
if(!this.keys.length){for(const v of L)this.release(v);return;}
const best=this.keys.slice().sort((a,b)=>this.rank(a,b))[0],gatedLine=L.length&&L[0].gate;
if(gatedLine&&L[0].id===best.id)return;
const multi=Math.round(T.trig)===1,oldId=L.length?L[0].id:-1;
if(gatedLine){
const retrig=multi&&isNew&&best.ord===this.ord;
for(const v of L){v.id=best.id;v.note=best.note;v.vel=retrig?best.vel:v.vel;v.ord=best.ord;v.target=best.tp;v.gc=this.gc;v.gl=true;v.sqPv=-1;if(best.lk||v.lkS)this.lkStartM(v,best.lk||null,0);
if(retrig){if(T.e1.rst>=.5)v.e1=0;if(T.e2.rst>=.5){v.e2=0;v.aA=-1;}v.e1s=1;v.e2s=1;if(T.l1.ks===2)v.l1=0;if(T.l2.ks===2)v.l2=0;if(v.sq)v.sq.reset();}}
this.lastPitch=best.tp;this.endCheck(oldId);return;
}
const from=multi&&(this.gc<1||this.sqPt)&&this.lastPitch>=0&&this.lastPitch!==best.tp?this.lastPitch:-1;
const n=this.ea===2?this.uniN():1,off=MKW.uni(T.udet,n);
if(L.length===n){for(let u=0;u<n;u++){this.start(L[u],best,from,true);L[u].isLine=true;L[u].udet=off[u];}}
else{
for(const v of L)this.steal(v);
for(let u=0;u<n;u++){const v=this.free();this.start(v,best,from,false);v.isLine=true;v.udet=off[u];v.ug=n>1? .5:1;}
}
this.endCheck(oldId);
}
release(v){v.gate=false;if(v.e1s)v.e1s=4;if(v.e2s)v.e2s=4;}
noteOff(id){
const i=this.keys.findIndex(k=>k.id===id);
if(i>=0)this.keys.splice(i,1);
if(this.ea!==1){this.monoSel(false);this.endCheck(id);return;}
let had=false;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost&&v.id===id&&v.gate){this.release(v);had=true;}
if(!had){this.endCheck(id);return;}
const G=this.gated();if(G.length>=this.maxV())return;
const wait=this.keys.filter(k=>!G.some(v=>v.id===k.id)).sort((a,b)=>this.rank(a,b));
if(wait.length)this.polyOn(wait[0],0);
}
fastOff(id,tau){for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.id===id)this.fadeOut(v,Math.max(64,Math.round(tau*5*SR)));const i=this.keys.findIndex(k=>k.id===id);if(i>=0){this.keys.splice(i,1);}this.endCheck(id);}
killId(id){const i=this.keys.findIndex(k=>k.id===id);if(i>=0)this.keys.splice(i,1);for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.id===id)this.fadeOut(v,96);this.endCheck(id);}
retune(id,note,tau){
const k=this.keys.find(x=>x.id===id),tp=this.par.tuned(note);if(k){k.note=note;k.tp=tp;}
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost&&v.id===id){v.note=note;v.target=tp;v.gc=tau>0?1-Math.exp(-CR/(tau*SR)):1;}
}
rescale(){for(const k of this.keys)k.tp=this.par.tuned(k.note);for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost){const tp=this.par.tuned(v.note);v.pitch+=tp-v.target;v.target=tp;}if(this.lastPitch>=0){const k=this.keys.find(x=>x.ord===this.ord);if(k)this.lastPitch=k.tp;}}
allOff(){const ids=this.keys.map(k=>k.id);this.keys.length=0;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.gate)this.release(v);for(const id of ids)this.endCheck(id);}
panic(){const ids=this.keys.map(k=>k.id);this.keys.length=0;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)this.fadeOut(v,64);for(const id of ids)this.endCheck(id);}
handOver(n){
const held=this.keys.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.ord-b.ord).map(k=>({id:k.id,note:k.note,vel:k.vel}));
this.keys.length=0;
const hs=new Set(held.map(x=>x.id));
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){v.ghost=true;v.gate=false;v.xfo=true;v.noEnd=hs.has(v.id);this.fadeOut(v,n);}
return held;
}
adopt(held,n){
for(const h of held)this.noteOn(h.id,h.note,h.vel);
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost){v.fin=n;v.finN=n;}
}
reassign(list,keep,from){
const old=this.V.filter(v=>v.on&&!v.ghost),byId=new Map();
const att=MKW.atk(this.T.e2.a),n=Math.round(Math.max(.04,Math.min(.5,att))*SR);
const kp=keep&&this.ea===1,hid=kp?new Set(this.keys.map(k=>k.id)):null,hn=kp?new Set((list||this.keys).map(k=>k.note)):null;

if(list){const had=new Map(keys.map(k=>[k.id,k]));keys=list.map(g=>{const k=had.get(g.id)||{id:g.id,note:g.note,vel:g.vel,tp:this.par.tuned(g.note)};k.ord=++this.ord;return k;});}

for(const k of keys){
this.keys.push(k);
if(this.ea===1){const v=this.polyOn(k,MK_EXF);if(v&&byId.has(k.id))this.inherit(v,byId.get(k.id));else if(v&&from&&from.has(k.id))this.inheritPh(v,from.get(k.id));}
}
if(this.ea!==1&&this.keys.length){
this.monoSel(false);
for(const v of this.line()){const c=byId.get(v.id);if(c)this.inherit(v,c);else if(from&&from.has(v.id))this.inheritPh(v,from.get(v.id));v.fin=MK_EXF;v.finN=MK_EXF;}
}
}
inheritPh(v,c){v.t1=c.t1;v.t2=c.t2;v.sp=c.sp;v.l1=c.l1;v.l2=c.l2;if(c.wn>=0){v.tw=c.tw;v.wn=c.wn;v.wx=0;}}
inherit(v,c){
for(const k of['t1','t2','sp','e1','e1s','e2','e2s','gt','l1','l2','sh1','sh2','s1','s2','s3','s4','pitch','dx','dF','rng','os','o1p','rsc',
'tw','wn','vz1','vz2','rn','ns1','ns2','xL2'])v[k]=c[k];
v.dz.set(c.dz);if(c.sq)v.sq=c.sq.copy();

}
structXf(ns){
if(this.sxOn){this.sxPend=ns;return;}
this.sxkN++;
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost){
const c=mkClone(v);c.st=v.st;v.sxo=c;v.sxk=this.sxkN;c.sxk=-2;

if(ns.w2!==v.st.w2&&!(ns.mod&2)){const d=wavePhi(ns.w2,.5)-wavePhi(v.st.w2,.5);let t=v.t2+d;t-=Math.floor(t);v.t2=t;}
v.st=ns;
}
this.st=ns;this.sxOn=true;this.sxT=0;this.sxN=0;
}
sxMix(c,v,L,R,off){
const oL=this.oL,oR=this.oR,nL=this.nL,nR=this.nR,E=c.sxE,n=CR;
let oo=0,nn=0,on=0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=oL[i],b=oR[i],p=nL[i],d=nR[i];oo+=a*a+b*b;nn+=p*p+d*d;on+=a*p+b*d;}

if(v&&this.sxN===SX_M&&E[2]<0){v.pol=-v.pol;E[2]=-E[2];}
const t=this.sxT,u=1-t,Po=E[0],Pn=E[1],den=u*u*Po+t*t*Pn+2*u*t*E[2],tg_=u*Po+t*Pn;
const sc=tg_>1e-24?(den>tg_/(SX_MAX*SX_MAX)?Math.sqrt(tg_/den):SX_MAX):1;
const a1=u*sc,b1=t*sc,a0=c.sxA,b0=c.sxB,da=(a1-a0)/n,db=(b1-b0)/n;
let ga=a0,gb=b0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ga+=da;gb+=db;L[off+i]+=oL[i]*ga+nL[i]*gb;R[off+i]+=oR[i]*ga+nR[i]*gb;}
c.sxA=a1;c.sxB=b1;
}
idle(n,fr){const T=this.T;this.g1=(this.g1+this.lfoRate(T.l1,T.l1.sync>=.5)*n*ISR)%1;this.g2=(this.g2+this.lfoRate(T.l2,T.l2.sync>=.5)*n*ISR)%1;

lfoRate(L,sync){const spb=60/this.h.bpm;return sync?1/(MKW.LSYNC[Math.max(0,Math.min(14,Math.round(L.note)))]*4*spb):MKW.lfoHz(L.f);}
lfoRateC(n,L){
const K=this.lfK,o=n*5,sync=L.sync>=.5,b=this.h.bpm;
if(K[o]!==L.f||K[o+1]!==+sync||K[o+2]!==L.note||K[o+3]!==b){K[o]=L.f;K[o+1]=+sync;K[o+2]=L.note;K[o+3]=b;K[o+4]=this.lfoRate(L,sync);}

}
block(Lo,Ro,off,frame){

}
sqPreAll(sx,sk){

sqPre(v){

voice(v,Lo,Ro,off){

osc1x(v,N,dt1,m0,m,w1,ob,wr){

ob[n]=lp*G;
}
if(s1<1e-15&&s1>-1e-15)s1=0;if(s2<1e-15&&s2>-1e-15)s2=0;
v.ns1=s1;v.ns2=s2;v.rn=x>>>0;v.t1=t;return;
}
const C=v.wn>0?MKWT[v.wn]:null,O=v.wx>0&&v.wo>0?MKWT[v.wo]:null,a=v.wa,j=v.wj<0?0:v.wj;
const dtw=dt1/(C?C.r:O?O.r:1),dww=(v.wwB-v.wwA)/N,dwo=(v.woB-v.woA)/N;
let tw=v.tw,wx=v.wx,ww=v.wwA,wo=v.woA;
const nc=C&&C.o?v.ntC:null,ntx=C&&C.o&&v.ntA<1;
const S0=C&&wx<=0&&!ntx?(C.o?(nc?nc.L:null):C.s[0]):null;
if(S0){
const S=S0,A=S[a],B=S[a+1],na=A.length-1,nb=B.length-1,idt=1/dt1;
if(!this.wrUse){
let x=tw*na;const dx=dtw*na;
if(dww===0&&ww<=0)for(let n=0;n<N;n++){x+=dx;if(x>=na)x-=na;const i=x|0;ob[n]=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*(x-i);}
else if(na===nb){
if(dww===0)for(let n=0;n<N;n++){x+=dx;if(x>=na)x-=na;const i=x|0,f=x-i,ya=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*f;ob[n]=ya+(B[i]+(B[i+1]-B[i])*f-ya)*ww;}
else for(let n=0;n<N;n++){x+=dx;if(x>=na)x-=na;ww+=dww;const i=x|0,f=x-i,ya=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*f;ob[n]=ww>0?ya+(B[i]+(B[i+1]-B[i])*f-ya)*ww:ya;}
}else{
const hb=nb/na;
if(dww===0)for(let n=0;n<N;n++){x+=dx;if(x>=na)x-=na;const i=x|0,xb=x*hb,ib=xb|0,ya=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*(x-i);ob[n]=ya+(B[ib]+(B[ib+1]-B[ib])*(xb-ib)-ya)*ww;}
else for(let n=0;n<N;n++){x+=dx;if(x>=na)x-=na;ww+=dww;const i=x|0,ya=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*(x-i);
if(ww>0){const xb=x*hb,ib=xb|0;ob[n]=ya+(B[ib]+(B[ib+1]-B[ib])*(xb-ib)-ya)*ww;}else ob[n]=ya;}
}
t+=N*dt1;t-=Math.floor(t);v.tw=x/na;v.t1=t;return;
}
if(ww<=0&&dww===0)for(let n=0;n<N;n++){t+=dt1;let wrap=-1;if(t>=1){t-=1;wrap=t*idt;}wr[n]=wrap;tw+=dtw;if(tw>=1)tw-=1;const x=tw*na,i=x|0;ob[n]=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*(x-i);}
else if(na===nb)for(let n=0;n<N;n++){
t+=dt1;let wrap=-1;if(t>=1){t-=1;wrap=t*idt;}wr[n]=wrap;tw+=dtw;if(tw>=1)tw-=1;ww+=dww;
const x=tw*na,i=x|0,f=x-i,ya=A[i]+(A[i+1]-A[i])*f;ob[n]=ya+(B[i]+(B[i+1]-B[i])*f-ya)*ww;
}
else for(let n=0;n<N;n++){t+=dt1;let wrap=-1;if(t>=1){t-=1;wrap=t*idt;}wr[n]=wrap;tw+=dtw;if(tw>=1)tw-=1;ww+=dww;ob[n]=mkwLv(S,a,ww,tw);}
v.tw=tw;v.t1=t;return;
}
this.osc1g(v,N,dt1,ob,wr,C,O,nc,ntx,a,j,dtw,dww,dwo);
}
osc1g(v,N,dt1,ob,wr,C,O,nc,ntx,a,j,dtw,dww,dwo){
let t=v.t1,tw=v.tw,wx=v.wx,ww=v.wwA,wo=v.woA;
const so=v.ntO,sw=v.ntW,du=(v.ntB-v.ntA)/N;let nu=v.ntA;
for(let n=0;n<N;n++){
t+=dt1;let wrap=-1;if(t>=1){t-=1;wrap=t/dt1;}wr[n]=wrap;
tw+=dtw;if(tw>=1)tw-=1;ww+=dww;wo+=dwo;
let y=nc?mkwLv(nc.L,a,ww,tw):C?mkwRd(C,a,ww,j,wo,tw):mkSaw(t,dt1);
if(ntx){nu+=du;const z=so?mkwLv(so.L,a,ww,tw):mkwRd(C,a,ww,j,wo,tw);y=z+(y-z)*nu;}
if(wx>0){wx--;const u=1-wx/MK_WXF,z=u*u*(3-2*u)*1.5707963267948966,yo=O?(sw?mkwLv(sw.L,a,ww,tw):mkwRd(O,a,ww,j,wo,tw)):v.wo===0?mkSaw(t,dt1):0;y=y*Math.sin(z)+yo*Math.cos(z);}
ob[n]=y;
}
if(wx<=0){v.wo=-1;v.ntW=null;}
v.tw=tw;v.wx=wx;v.t1=t;
}
osc2(v,N,dt2,w2,sync,o2b,wr,hs,hq){
let t2=v.t2;
if(!sync){
for(let n=0;n<N;n++){
t2+=dt2;if(t2>=1)t2-=1;let y=0;
if(w2===0){y=2*t2-1;if(t2<dt2){const x=t2/dt2;y-=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t2>1-dt2){const x=(t2-1)/dt2;y-=x*x+x+x+1;}}
else if(w2===1){y=t2<.5?1:-1;
if(t2<dt2){const x=t2/dt2;y+=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t2>1-dt2){const x=(t2-1)/dt2;y+=x*x+x+x+1;}
let tp=t2-.5;if(tp<0)tp+=1;if(tp<dt2){const x=tp/dt2;y-=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(tp>1-dt2){const x=(tp-1)/dt2;y-=x*x+x+x+1;}}
else{const c=8*dt2/6;y=t2<.5?4*t2-1:3-4*t2;
if(t2<dt2){const d=1-t2/dt2;y+=c*d*d*d;}else if(t2>1-dt2){const d=1-(1-t2)/dt2;y+=c*d*d*d;}
const e=t2-.5,ae=e<0?-e:e;if(ae<dt2){const d=1-ae/dt2;y-=c*d*d*d;}}
o2b[n]=y;
}
}else{
const dt=dt2,jw=w2===0?-2:w2===1?2:0,sq=w2===1;
let prev=v.sp;
for(let n=0;n<N;n++){
const t0=t2,xm=wr[n];let cP=0,cN=0,xs=0;
if(xm>=0){
let tb=t0+(1-xm)*dt;MK_XP=0;MK_XQ=0;
if(tb>=1){const e1=xm+(tb-1)/dt;
if(sq&&t0<.5){xs=e1+.5/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);if(hs)mkXt(xm,xs,-2);}
cP+=.5*jw*e1*e1;cN-=.5*jw*(1-e1)*(1-e1);tb-=1;if(hs)mkXt(xm,e1,jw);
if(sq&&tb>=.5){xs=xm+(tb-.5)/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);if(hs)mkXt(xm,xs,-2);}
}else if(sq&&t0<.5&&tb>=.5){xs=xm+(tb-.5)/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);if(hs)mkXt(xm,xs,-2);}
const hh=mkW(w2,0)-mkW(w2,tb);cP+=.5*hh*xm*xm;cN-=.5*hh*(1-xm)*(1-xm);
t2=xm*dt;
if(sq&&t2>=.5){xs=(t2-.5)/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);if(hs)mkXt(xm,xs,-2);}
if(hs){mkXt(xm,xm,hh);hs[n]=MK_XP;hq[n]=MK_XQ;}
}else if(t2+dt<1&&!(sq&&t2<.5&&t2+dt>=.5))t2+=dt;
else{
t2+=dt;
if(t2>=1){const e=(t2-1)/dt;
if(sq&&t0<.5){xs=e+.5/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);}
cP+=.5*jw*e*e;cN-=.5*jw*(1-e)*(1-e);t2-=1;
if(sq&&t2>=.5){xs=(t2-.5)/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);}
}else if(sq&&t0<.5&&t2>=.5){xs=(t2-.5)/dt;cP-=xs*xs;cN+=(1-xs)*(1-xs);}
}
const y=mkW(w2,t2)+cN;prev+=cP;o2b[n]=prev;prev=y;
}
v.sp=prev;
}
v.t2=t2;
}
osc2x(v,dt1,dt2,m0,m,w1,w2,mod,g1,g2){
const N=CR+CR,a=this.ob2,s=this.o2b2,wr=this.wr2,hs=this.hs2,hq=this.hq2,ob=this.ob;
this.osc1(v,N,dt1*.5,m0,m,w1,a,wr);
this.osc2(v,N,dt2*.5,w2,true,s,wr,hs,hq);
if(mod&1){
const dO=w1===0?-2:w1===1?2:0;let po=v.o1p,cc=v.rsc;
for(let n=0;n<N;n++){
const o=a[n],xm=wr[n];let p=po*s[n]+cc;cc=0;
if(xm>=0&&dO!==0){p+=dO*hs[n];cc=-dO*hq[n];}
s[n]=p;po=o;
}
v.o1p=po;v.rsc=cc;
}else{v.o1p=a[N-1];v.rsc=0;}
const z=v.dz,c0=MK_HB[0],c1=MK_HB[1],c2=MK_HB[2],c3=MK_HB[3];
let x0=z[0],x1=z[1],x2=z[2],x3=z[3],y0=z[4],y1=z[5],y2=z[6],y3=z[7];
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
const i=n+n;let e=a[i]*g1+s[i]*g2,l=a[i+1]*g1+s[i+1]*g2,t;
t=l;l=(l-y0)*c0+x0;x0=t;y0=l;t=l;l=(l-y2)*c2+x2;x2=t;y2=l;
t=e;e=(e-y1)*c1+x1;x1=t;y1=e;t=e;e=(e-y3)*c3+x3;x3=t;y3=e;
ob[n]=.5*(l+e);
}
z[0]=x0;z[1]=x1;z[2]=x2;z[3]=x3;z[4]=y0;z[5]=y1;z[6]=y2;z[7]=y3;
}
vEnd(v){if(!v.on)return;const id=v.id,ne=v.noEnd;v.on=false;v.id=-1;v.ghost=false;v.isLine=false;v.noEnd=false;v.xfo=false;if(!ne)this.endCheck(id);}
scope(){
const out=[],th=this.sq.h.splice(0);
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost)out.push({note:v.note,t:v.age,eg1:v.e1,eg2:v.st&&v.st.gate?v.gt:v.e2,lfo1:v.lv1||0,lfo2:v.lv2||0,cutHz:v.cutHz,cutMax:v.cutMax,pitch:v.pitch,gate:v.gate,udet:v.udet,vel:v.vel,
seqStep:this.sqV&&v.sq?v.sq.h.splice(0):th.slice()});
for(const v of this.V)v.cutMax=0;
return out;
}
}
const MK_VN=16,MK_VXF=Math.max(1,Math.round(.03*SR/CR));
const MK_VRN=MKW.VR.map(f=>69+12*Math.log2(f/440));
const MK_VGW=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(.003*SR/CR));
const MK_HK=[2*Math.cos(Math.PI/8),2*Math.cos(3*Math.PI/8)];
const MK_VQ=.0001;
function mkVg(pos){const i=Math.min(22,Math.max(0,Math.floor(pos))),t=Math.min(1,pos-i),n=MK_VRN[i]+(MK_VRN[i+1]-MK_VRN[i])*t;return lut(GTAN,n);}
const MK_VHB=[0.10910604180338343,0.36163258249296687,0.6334772201668326,0.8774434190209677],MK_VH=CR>>1,MK_VD=3;
const MK_VNH=69+12*Math.log2(.21*SR/440);let MK_VHK=1;
function mkVgH(pos){
const i=Math.min(22,Math.max(0,Math.floor(pos))),n=MK_VRN[i]+(MK_VRN[i+1]-MK_VRN[i])*Math.min(1,pos-i),nh=Math.min(n,MK_VNH);
const t=lut(GTAN,n),u=lut(GTAN,nh),g=2*u/(1-u*u);
MK_VHK=(4*Math.PI*lut(FREQ,nh)/SR*(1+g*g)/(2*g))/(2*Math.PI*Math.min(lut(FREQ,n),.45*SR)/SR*(1+t*t)/(2*t));return g;
}
function mkAp(c,st,i,x){const y=c*(x-st[i+1])+st[i];st[i]=x;st[i+1]=y;return y;}
function mkHbDown(src,io,dst,st){
const C=MK_VHB;for(let m=0,n=io;m<MK_VH;m++,n+=2)dst[m]=.5*(mkAp(C[2],st,2,mkAp(C[0],st,0,src?src[n+1]:0))+mkAp(C[3],st,6,mkAp(C[1],st,4,src?src[n]:0)));
for(let i=0;i<8;i++)st[i]=mkFl(st[i]);
}
function mkHbUp(h,st,out,off,wa,wb){
const C=MK_VHB,dw=(wb-wa)/CR;let w=wa;
for(let m=0,n=off;m<MK_VH;m++,n+=2){const x=h[m];w+=dw;out[n]+=mkAp(C[2],st,2,mkAp(C[0],st,0,x))*w;w+=dw;out[n+1]+=mkAp(C[3],st,6,mkAp(C[1],st,4,x))*w;}
for(let i=0;i<8;i++)st[i]=mkFl(st[i]);
}
function mkB4(x,xo,len,z,o,g,dg,a1,da,k,y){
let s1=z[o],s2=z[o+1],s3=z[o+2],s4=z[o+3];
for(let n=0;n<len;n++){
g+=dg;a1+=da;const a2=g*a1,a3=g*a2;let v3=x[xo+n]-s2,v1=a1*s1+a2*v3,v2=s2+a2*s1+a3*v3;s1=2*v1-s1;s2=2*v2-s2;
v3=k*v1-s4;v1=a1*s3+a2*v3;v2=s4+a2*s3+a3*v3;s3=2*v1-s3;s4=2*v2-s4;y[n]=k*v1;
}
z[o]=s1=mkFl(s1);z[o+1]=s2=mkFl(s2);z[o+2]=s3=mkFl(s3);z[o+3]=s4=mkFl(s4);
return Math.max(s1<0?-s1:s1,s2<0?-s2:s2,s3<0?-s3:s3,s4<0?-s4:s4);
}
class MkVoc{
constructor(core){
const N=MK_VN;this.c=core;
this.car=new Float64Array(CR);this.carR=new Float64Array(CR);this.gb=new Float64Array(CR);
this.P={gs:0,th:0,hpl:0,hpg:1,ins:0,fs:0,cut:0,res:0,msrc:1,mint:0,efs:64,lvl:127,dir:0,dist:0,vel:0,kbd:0};
this.chl=new Float64Array(N).fill(127);this.chp=new Float64Array(N);
this.hold=null;this.hq=null;
this.ak=1/MKW.vocQA;this.ag=new Float64Array(N);this.aa=new Float64Array(N);
for(let b=0;b<N;b++){const g=mkVg(b+4);this.ag[b]=g;this.aa[b]=1/(1+g*(g+this.ak));}
this.agH=new Float64Array(N);this.aaH=new Float64Array(N);this.akH=new Float64Array(N);
for(let b=0;b<N;b++){const g=mkVgH(b+4),k=this.ak*MK_VHK;this.agH[b]=g;this.akH[b]=k;this.aaH[b]=1/(1+g*(g+k));}
this.az=new Float64Array(8*N);this.sz=new Float64Array(8*N);
this.hE=new Float64Array(MK_VH);this.hC=new Float64Array(MK_VH);this.hL=new Float64Array(MK_VH);this.hR=new Float64Array(MK_VH);this.yb=new Float64Array(MK_VH);this.yf=new Float64Array(CR);
this.cD=new Float64Array(CR);this.cq=new Float64Array(MK_VD);this.dE=new Float64Array(8);this.dC=new Float64Array(8);this.uL=new Float64Array(8);this.uR=new Float64Array(8);
this.skH=new Float64Array(N);this.gHA=new Float64Array(N);this.gHB=new Float64Array(N);this.aHA=new Float64Array(N);this.aHB=new Float64Array(N);
this.env=new Float64Array(N);this.eL=new Float64Array(N);this.eR=new Float64Array(N);
this.gA=new Float64Array(N);this.gB=new Float64Array(N);this.aA=new Float64Array(N);this.aB=new Float64Array(N);this.sk=1/3;this.coefOk=false;this.hOk=false;
this.cL=new Float64Array(N);this.cR=new Float64Array(N);this.cRun=true;
this.sSh=0;this.sRes=0;this.sIns=0;this.sDir=0;this.sHpl=0;this.sLvl=127;this.sVel=0;this.sKbd=0;this.lvG=MKW.ampG(127);this.shK=NaN;this.resK=NaN;
this.src=0;this.efsK=NaN;this.fA=0;this.fR=0;this.fA2=0;this.fR2=0;this.fRb=1;
this.hw=0;this.gg=1;this.gw=new Float64Array(MK_VGW);this.gwi=0;this.thK=NaN;this.thL=0;this.gsK=NaN;this.gC=1;
this.hg=0;this.hz=new Float64Array(4);this.hpG=Math.tan(Math.PI*Math.min(MKW.vocHpf,.45*SR)*ISR);
this.p4=true;this.p2=false;this.w4=1;this.wv=0;this.warm=0;
this.stail=0;this.htail=0;this.lvOn=false;this.lvPk=[0,0];
this.first=true;
}
load(vc){
const o=vc&&typeof vc==='object'?vc:{};
for(const k in this.P)if(typeof o[k]==='number'&&isFinite(o[k]))this.P[k]=o[k];
for(let b=0;b<MK_VN;b++){if(Array.isArray(o.chl)&&isFinite(o.chl[b]))this.chl[b]=o.chl[b];if(Array.isArray(o.chp)&&isFinite(o.chp[b]))this.chp[b]=o.chp[b];}
this.setHold(Array.isArray(o.hold)?o.hold:null);this.cRun=true;
if(this.first)this.settle();
}
set(k,j,v){
if(k==='chl'||k==='chp'){const b=+j;if(b>=0&&b<MK_VN){this[k][b]=v;this.cRun=true;}return;}
if(k in this.P)this.P[k]=v;
}
setHold(h){
if(!h){this.hold=null;this.hq=null;return;}
const q=[];for(let b=0;b<MK_VN;b++)q.push(Math.max(0,Math.min(127,Math.round(+h[b]||0))));
if(this.hold&&this.hq&&this.hq.every((x,b)=>x===q[b]))return;
this.hq=q;this.hold=Float64Array.from(q,x=>MKW.holdA(x));this.env.set(this.hold);
}
holding(){return!!this.hold||this.P.efs>=126.5;}
capture(){if(!this.hold){this.hold=Float64Array.from(this.env);this.hq=Array.from(this.hold,a=>MKW.holdQ(a));}return this.hq.slice();}
settle(){
const P=this.P;this.sSh=P.fs+2*P.cut/63;this.sRes=P.res;this.sIns=P.ins;this.sDir=P.dir;this.sHpl=P.hpl;this.sLvl=P.lvl;this.sVel=P.vel;this.sKbd=P.kbd;
this.chTarget(true);this.hw=this.holding()?1:0;this.first=false;
}
chTarget(jump){
let mv=false;
for(let b=0;b<MK_VN;b++){
const g=MKW.vocChl(Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.chl[b]))),x=(Math.max(-63,Math.min(63,this.chp[b]))+63)/126*1.5707963267948966,tl=g*Math.cos(x),tr=g*Math.sin(x);
if(jump){this.cL[b]=tl;this.cR[b]=tr;continue;}
const dl=tl-this.cL[b],dr=tr-this.cR[b];
if(dl>1e-7||dl<-1e-7){this.cL[b]+=dl*MK_SM;mv=true;}else this.cL[b]=tl;
if(dr>1e-7||dr<-1e-7){this.cR[b]+=dr*MK_SM;mv=true;}else this.cR[b]=tr;
}
this.cRun=mv;
}
hot(){return!!(this.c.inE1||this.c.inE2)||this.stail>0||this.htail>0;}
block(Lo,Ro,off){
const c=this.c,P=this.P,W=MKW,N=MK_VN,E1=c.inE1,E2=c.inE2,io=c.io,car=this.car,T1=c.tm[0],TT=T1.T,Sm=T1.sm;
const sm=(a,b)=>{const d=b-a;return d<1e-6&&d>-1e-6?b:a+d*MK_SM;};
{const s=Math.round(P.msrc);let v=null;
for(const x of T1.V)if(x.on&&!x.ghost&&x.vm&&(!v||(x.gate&&!v.gate)||(x.gate===v.gate&&x.age>v.age)))v=x;
if(s===2)this.src=mkLfoV(1,Math.round(TT.l1.w),T1.g1,T1.gsh1);
else if(s===3)this.src=mkLfoV(2,Math.round(TT.l2.w),T1.g2,T1.gsh2);
else if(s===6)this.src=T1.bend;
else if(s===7)this.src=T1.modw;
else if(v)this.src=s===0?v.e1:s===1?v.e2:s===4?v.vel:(v.pitch+(Sm?Sm.trans:0)+T1.bend*TT.bend-60)/60;}
this.sSh=sm(this.sSh,P.fs+2*(P.cut+P.mint*this.src)/63);this.sRes=sm(this.sRes,P.res);
this.sIns=sm(this.sIns,P.ins);this.sDir=sm(this.sDir,P.dir);this.sHpl=sm(this.sHpl,P.hpl);this.sLvl=sm(this.sLvl,P.lvl);this.sVel=sm(this.sVel,P.vel);this.sKbd=sm(this.sKbd,P.kbd);
this.lvG=W.ampG(Math.max(0,Math.min(127,this.sLvl)));

const MK_SOLOS=CR/(.005*SR);
class MkCore{
constructor(host,init){

get T(){return this.tm[0].T;}

top(pm){
const r=(x,lo,hi,d)=>(typeof x==='number'&&isFinite(x)?Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,Math.round(x))):d);
const m0=this.mode,s0=this.split,t0=this.tv;
if(pm.arp&&typeof pm.arp==='object')this.arp=pm.arp.on>=.5?1:0;

if(sk!==k0){let on=false;for(let pc=0;pc<12;pc++){const c=MKW.sclCt(this.scl,this.skey,this.usc,pc)/100;this.sc[pc]=c;if(c!==0)on=true;}this.scOn=on;}
return[this.mode!==m0||(this.mode===2&&this.split!==s0),sk!==k0,this.tv!==t0];
}
tuned(note){return this.scOn?note+this.sc[((Math.round(note)%12)+12)%12]:note;}
dual(){return this.mode===1||this.mode===2;}
recap(){
const A=this.tm[0],B=this.tm[1],L=this.lim_,LS=this.limS_|0,ch=[false,false];
const set=(t,k,cv,cu,ea)=>{if(t.cv!==cv||t.cu!==cu||t.ea!==ea)ch[k]=true;t.cv=cv;t.cu=cu;t.ea=ea;};
const as=t=>Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Math.round(t.T.asg)));
if(!this.dual()){set(A,0,L>=2?2:4,LS>=1?2:4,as(A));set(B,1,L>=2?2:4,LS>=1?2:4,as(B));return ch;}
const a=as(A),b=as(B);let na,nb;
if(a===0||b===0){na=a===0?1:a===1?3:2;nb=b===0?1:b===1?3:2;}
else{na=[1,2,3][this.tv];nb=4-na;}

return ch;
}
routes(note,k,d){if(d&&this.dual())return((d>>k)&1)===1;return this.mode===1?true:this.mode===2?(note<this.split?k===0:k===1):k===0;}
keysEv(ty){if(ty===9){this.tm[0].keyReset();this.tm[1].keyReset();}}
after(route,reas,ch){

const l=ks.filter(x=>this.routes(x.note,k,x.d)),same=!reas[k]&&!ch[k]&&l.length===R[k].keys.length&&l.every(x=>R[k].keys.some(y=>y.id===x.id));
if(same)continue;

}
return;
}
for(let k=0;k<2;k++)if((reas[k]||ch[k])&&R[k].sounding())R[k].reassign();
}
load(pm){

}
set(o){

else if(path.startsWith('t.1.'))(b||(b={}))[path]=o[path];

else if(q[0]==='usc'){const u=this.usc?this.usc.slice():new Array(12).fill(0),j=+q[1];if(j>=0&&j<12){u[j]=v;tp.usc=u;}}
else if(q[0]==='arp'){if(q[1]==='on')this.arp=v>=.5?1:0;}
else if(q.length===1&&(q[0]==='mode'||q[0]==='tv'||q[0]==='split'||q[0]==='scale'||q[0]==='skey'))tp[q[0]]=v;}
}
const[route,scl]=this.top(tp),ra=a?this.tm[0].set(a):false,rb=b?this.tm[1].set(b):false;
if(scl){this.tm[0].rescale();this.tm[1].rescale();}
const ch=this.recap();if((m0===3)!==(this.mode===3)){ch[0]=true;this.sqMode();}this.after(route,[ra,rb],ch);
if(fx)this.fxSettle(fa);
}
wheel(o){this.tm[0].wheel(o);this.tm[1].wheel(o);}
sqMode(){for(const t of this.tm){if(this.mode===3&&t.rec)t.rec=null;t.sqDirty=true;}}
recMsg(m){const t=this.tm[m&&m.ti===1?1:0];t.recMsg(m);}
sqScope(){return[this.tm[0].sqInfo(),this.tm[1].sqInfo()];}
busy(){return this.keys.length>0||this.tm[0].busy()||this.tm[1].busy();}
fxHot(){return this.fx.hot();}
inSet(inp){const n=inp?inp.length:0;this.inE1=n>0?inp[0]:null;this.inE2=n>1?inp[1]:null;}
vocHot(){return this.mode===3&&this.h.P.eng===3&&this.vc.hot();}
vmOn(){for(const v of this.tm[0].V)if(v.on&&v.vm)return true;return false;}
fxEnd(L,R){if(this.fx.pend){this.fx.run(L,R);if(!this.fx.hot()&&!this.busy())this.fx.settle();}}
fxSettle(fa){if(this.busy())return;const F=this.fx;if(!fa)F.quiet=1e9;else if(F.hot())return;F.settle();}
count(){return this.tm[0].count()+this.tm[1].count();}
has(id){for(const t of this.tm){if(t.keys.some(k=>k.id===id))return true;for(const v of t.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost&&v.id===id)return true;}return false;}
endCheck(id){if(id<0)return;for(const t of this.tm)if(t.holds(id))return;this.phm.delete(id);this.h.ended.push(id);}
notePh(ti,id,v){
let e=this.phm.get(id);if(!e){if(this.phm.size>256)this.phm.clear();e={a:[],j:[0,0]};this.phm.set(id,e);}
const j=e.j[ti]++;if(j<e.a.length){v.t1=e.a[j][0];v.t2=e.a[j][1];}else e.a.push([v.t1,v.t2]);
}
noteOn(id,note,vel,d,e){
if(!this.seed)this.seed=((Math.random()*4294967295)>>>0)||1;
d=d>0&&d<4?d:0;this.keys.push(d?{id,note,vel,ord:++this.ord,d}:{id,note,vel,ord:++this.ord});
for(let k=0;k<2;k++)if(this.routes(note,k,d))this.tm[k].noteOn(id,note,vel,e||null);
}
lbEv(p,x,r){p=p|0;if(!(p>=0&&p<PV_M)||typeof x!=='number'){this.lkBad=(this.lkBad|0)+1;return;}this.tm[PVMT[p]].lbEvM(p,x,r|0);}
lbPrep(){}
lkBadN(){return this.lkBad|0;}
lkRep(){return this.tm[0].lkRepM().concat(this.tm[1].lkRepM());}
dropKey(id){const i=this.keys.findIndex(k=>k.id===id);if(i>=0)this.keys.splice(i,1);}
noteOff(id){this.dropKey(id);let any=false;for(const t of this.tm)if(t.holds(id)){t.noteOff(id);any=true;}if(!any)this.endCheck(id);}
retune(id,note,tau){const k=this.keys.find(x=>x.id===id);if(k)k.note=note;for(const t of this.tm)t.retune(id,note,tau);}
fastOff(id,tau){this.dropKey(id);for(const t of this.tm)t.fastOff(id,tau);}
killId(id){this.dropKey(id);for(const t of this.tm)t.killId(id);}
allOff(){this.keys.length=0;for(const t of this.tm)t.allOff();}
panic(){this.keys.length=0;this.phm.clear();for(const t of this.tm)t.panic();this.fx.kill();}
handOver(n){
const held=this.keys.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.ord-b.ord).map(k=>({id:k.id,note:k.note,vel:k.vel}));
this.keys.length=0;for(const t of this.tm)t.handOver(n);
return held;
}
adopt(held,n){
for(const h of held)this.noteOn(h.id,h.note,h.vel);
for(const t of this.tm)for(const v of t.V)if(v.on&&!v.ghost){v.fin=n;v.finN=n;}
}
idle(n,fr){this.fx.idle(n);this.tm[0].idle(n,fr);this.tm[1].idle(n,fr);this.soloStep();}
soloStep(){const s=this.solo_,g=this.sg;let mv=false;for(let k=0;k<2;k++){const tg=s===0||s===k+1?1:0;if(g[k]!==tg&&!this.tm[k].busy()){g[k]=tg;continue;}if(g[k]!==tg){g[k]=tg>g[k]?Math.min(tg,g[k]+MK_SOLOS):Math.max(tg,g[k]-MK_SOLOS);mv=true;}}return mv;}
block(Lo,Ro,off,frame){
if(MKNT_Q.length)mkntWork();
const g0=this.sg[0],g1=this.sg[1];this.soloStep();
const FX=this.fx,fxa=(!this.tap_||this.tap_==='fx')&&FX.active(),VL=fxa?FX.bL:Lo,VR=fxa?FX.bR:Ro,vo=off;
if(fxa){if(FX.qf!==frame-off){FX.qf=frame-off;VL.fill(0);VR.fill(0);}FX.pend=true;}
const voc=this.mode===3||this.vmOn();
if(voc){this.vc.car.fill(0);this.vc.carR.fill(0);}this.io=off;
for(let k=0;k<2;k++){
const t=this.tm[k],a=k?g1:g0,b=this.sg[k];
if(!(t.busy()||(k===0&&this.tap_==='lfo'))){t.idle(CR,frame);continue;}
if(a===1&&b===1){t.block(VL,VR,vo,frame);continue;}
const L=this.bL,R=this.bR;L.fill(0);R.fill(0);t.block(L,R,0,frame);
if(a===0&&b===0)continue;
const d=(b-a)/CR;let g=a;for(let i=0;i<CR;i++){g+=d;VL[vo+i]+=L[i]*g;VR[vo+i]+=R[i]*g;}
}
if(voc)this.vc.block(VL,VR,vo);
if(!fxa)FX.idle(CR);
}
scope(){const o=this.tm[0].scope();for(const x of this.tm[1].scope()){x.tb=2;o.push(x);}return o;}
}
class AnalogCore extends AudioWorkletProcessor{

ord:new Int8Array(6),fk:new Int8Array(6),ms:new Int8Array(36),mn:new Uint8Array(6),ca:new Uint8Array(6),tw:new Uint8Array(6)};

onMsg(m){
this.kf2Msg(m);
if(m.set&&typeof m.set.eng==='number')for(const k of KF12_Z)if(!(k in m.set))m.set[k]=0;
if(m.set&&typeof m.set.eng==='number')for(const k in KS4_N)if(!(k in m.set))m.set[k]=KS4_N[k];
if(m.ev){for(const e of m.ev)this.enqueue(e);}
if(m.kfKey)this.kf1Key(m.kfKey,m.kfT);
if(m.mk)this.mk.load(m.mk);
if(m.mkSet)this.mk.set(m.mkSet);
if(m.mkWheel)this.mk.wheel(m.mkWheel);
if(m.mkSeed!=null)this.mk.seed=(m.mkSeed>>>0)||1;
if(m.mkScope!=null){this.mk.scopeOn=!!m.mkScope;this.mk.scopeNext=0;}
if(m.mkTap!=null)this.mk.tap=String(m.mkTap);

if(m.mkFxKill)this.mk.fx.kill();
if(m.mkFxFrz!=null)this.mk.fx.frz=m.mkFxFrz>=0?m.mkFxFrz%1:-1;

if(m.set&&(this.srOn||this.mo))this.srSetIn(m.set,!!m.xf);
if(m.srProbe!=null){this.srInit();this.srPrb=!!m.srProbe;}
if(m.moProbe!=null){this.srInit();this.moPrb=!!m.moProbe;}
if(m.n8q)this.port.postMessage({n8q:{lfMiss:this.lfMiss||0,lf:[!!this.lf1,!!this.lf2],srOn:!!this.srOn,xs:this.srXs||0,x:this.srX||0,mo:!!this.mo,defer:this.srDefer||0,n:this.srN||0,keys:this.srTab?this.srTab.map(e=>e.k):[],P:Object.assign({},this.P)}});
if(m.set)this.setReq(m.set,!!m.xf,m.fm6||null);
if(m.mods)this.setMods(m.mods);
if(m.fm6&&!m.set)this.fmMsg(m.fm6);
if(m.fmxLv!=null){this.lvOn=!!m.fmxLv;this.lvNext=0;}
if(m.tempo){
