if(e.at!=null&&isFinite(e.at)){this.tnext.push(e);this.tnext.sort((a,b)=>a.at-b.at);}
else{this.bpm=e.bpm;this.songStart=e.start;this.tnext.length=0;}
}
if(m.scope!=null){this.scopeOn=!!m.scope;this.scopeNext=0;}
if(m.scopeDst!=null){this.scopeDst=!!m.scopeDst;this.scD.fill(0);}
if(m.dispose)this.alive=false;
if(m.lkMd){this.lkMd=this.lkMd||{};for(const k in m.lkMd)this.lkMd[k]=m.lkMd[k];}
if(m.lkProbe!=null){this.lkTap=!!m.lkProbe;this.lkRep=null;this.lkRepI=typeof m.lkProbe==='number'&&m.lkProbe>=16?m.lkProbe|0:0;this.lkRepT=0;}
if(m.lkInfo)this.port.postMessage({lkInfo:this.lkInfoOf()});
if(m.k1Dump)this.port.postMessage({k1Dump:{P:Object.assign({},this.P),mods:Array.from(this.msrc,(s,i)=>[s,this.mdst[i],this.mamt[i]]).filter(x=>x[0]),mk:this.mk.tm.map(t=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.T))),mkTop:{mode:this.mk.mode,tv:this.mk.tv,split:this.mk.split,scl:this.mk.scl,skey:this.mk.skey,cap:this.mk.tm.map(t=>[t.cv,t.cu])},voc:Object.assign({},this.mk.vc.P),fmx:this.gx?{on:Array.from(this.gx.on),fmS:Array.from(this.gx.fmS),am:Array.from(this.gx.am),amG:Array.from(this.gx.amG),pit:this.gx.pit.map(x=>!!x),pt:this.gx.pe.slice(6).map(x=>!!x&&Array.from(x.v).some(y=>Math.abs(y)>1e-9))}:null,peg:!this.gx&&!!(this.fm&&this.fm.peg&&!this.fm.peg.l.every(x=>x===50))}});
}
enqueue(e){
const q=this.q;
if(e[1]===4){
for(let i=q.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(q[i][2]===e[2]&&q[i][1]!==5&&q[i][1]!==6&&q[i][1]<11)q.splice(i,1);
this.mk.killId(e[2]);
const v=this.findId(e[2]);if(v)this.fadeOut(v,96);this.dropStack(e[2]);this.heldDrop(e[2]);
return;
}
if(e[1]===8){for(let i=q.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(q[i][2]===e[2]&&q[i][1]===3)q.splice(i,1);return;}
const f=Math.round(e[0]*SR);
if(e[1]===12){const c=e[3]|0;for(let i=q.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(q[i][1]===11&&q[i][0]>=f&&(c>=0?q[i][3]===c||q[i][3]<0:q[i][3]===c))q.splice(i,1);return;}
let ev;
if(e.length>7){ev=e.slice();ev[0]=f;if(e[1]===1)this.lkPrep(ev);else if(e[1]===11)this.lbPrep(e[3]);}
else ev=e.length>6?[f,e[1],e[2],e[3],e[4],e[5],e[6]]:[f,e[1],e[2],e[3],e[4],e[5]];
let i=q.length;while(i>0&&q[i-1][0]>f)i--;
q.splice(i,0,ev);
}
setReq(o,force,fm){
if(typeof o.eng==='number'&&o.eng!==this.P.eng&&(o.eng===3||this.P.eng===3)){this.mkEng(o,fm);return;}
let active=false;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){active=true;break;}
if(active&&typeof o.eng==='number'&&o.eng!==this.P.eng){
const m=this.pend?Object.assign({},this.pend,o):o;this.pend=null;this.engXf(m);if(fm)this.fmMsg(fm);return;
}
if(this.xfState===3&&!this.sxfOn){this.setParams(o);if(fm)this.fmMsg(fm);return;}
if(this.pend){Object.assign(this.pend,o);if(fm)this.pendFm=fm;return;}
if(this.pendS){Object.assign(this.pendS.o,o);if(fm)this.pendS.fm=fm;return;}
if(active){
let xf=force;const P=this.P;
if(!xf)for(const k of XF_KEYS)if(k in o&&typeof o[k]==='number'&&o[k]!==P[k]){xf=true;break;}
if(!xf&&fm&&P.eng===1&&this.fmStruct(fm))xf=true;
if(xf){
if((P.xfLite>=1||('mode'in o&&(o.mode>0)!==(P.mode>0)))&&!this.sxfOn){this.pend=Object.assign({},o);if(fm)this.pendFm=fm;this.xfState=1;return;}
this.structReq(o,fm);return;
}
}
this.setParams(o);if(fm)this.fmMsg(fm);
}
structReq(o,fm){
if(this.sxfOn||this.xfState){const q=this.pendS||(this.pendS={o:{},fm:null});if(o)Object.assign(q.o,o);if(fm)q.fm=fm;return;}
this.structXf(o,fm);
}
structXf(o,fm){
const g=this.mkGhost();g.V=[];
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){const c=cloneVoice(v);c.sxo=null;c.sxE.fill(0);c.sxA=1;c.sxB=0;c.sxBlk=-1;v.sxo=c;g.V.push(c);}
g.noSpare=true;g.xfN=SXF_N;
const ow=Array.from(this.oW);
if(o&&Object.keys(o).length)this.setParams(o);
if(this.P.eng!==1)for(let n=0;n<NOSC;n++)if(this.oW[n]!==ow[n])for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){
const d=wavePhi(this.oW[n],v.pw[n])-wavePhi(ow[n],v.pw[n]);
for(let u=0;u<MAXU;u++){let t=v.ph[n*MAXU+u]+d;t-=Math.floor(t);v.ph[n*MAXU+u]=t;}
}
if(fm)this.setFm(fm,true);
this.sxkN++;
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)v.sxk=this.sxkN;
this.sxfG=g;g.xg=g.xg1=1;this.sxfFin=false;this.sxt=0;this.sxBlk=0;
this.sxfOn=true;this.xfState=3;this.xfT=0;this.xfLen=SXF_N+SXM_N;this.xg=this.xg1=0;
}
sxMix(c,v,L,R,off){
const oL=this.oL,oR=this.oR,nL=this.nL,nR=this.nR,E=c.sxE,n=CR;
let oo=0,nn=0,on=0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=oL[i],b=oR[i],p=nL[i],d=nR[i];oo+=a*a+b*b;nn+=p*p+d*d;on+=a*p+b*d;}
E[0]=E[0]*SX_EMA+oo;E[1]=E[1]*SX_EMA+nn;E[2]=E[2]*SX_EMA+on;
if(v&&this.xfT===SXM_N&&E[2]<0){v.pol=-v.pol;v.pl0=-v.pl0;v.pr0=-v.pr0;E[2]=-E[2];}
const t=this.sxt,u=1-t,Po=E[0],Pn=E[1],den=u*u*Po+t*t*Pn+2*u*t*E[2],tg=u*Po+t*Pn;
const sc=tg>1e-24?(den>tg/(SX_MAX*SX_MAX)?Math.sqrt(tg/den):SX_MAX):1;
const a1=u*sc,b1=t*sc,a0=c.sxA,b0=c.sxB,da=(a1-a0)/n,db=(b1-b0)/n;
let ga=a0,gb=b0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ga+=da;gb+=db;L[off+i]+=oL[i]*ga+nL[i]*gb;R[off+i]+=oR[i]*ga+nR[i]*gb;}
c.sxA=a1;c.sxB=b1;
}
sxfEnd(){
this.sxfOn=false;const q=this.pendS;this.pendS=null;if(!q)return;
let active=false;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){active=true;break;}
if(active)this.structXf(q.o,q.fm);else{if(Object.keys(q.o).length)this.setParams(q.o);if(q.fm)this.setFm(q.fm,true);}
}
mkGhost(){
const g=Object.setPrototypeOf({...this},AnalogCore.prototype);
for(const k of Object.keys(g)){const x=g[k];if(ArrayBuffer.isView(x))g[k]=x.slice();}
if(this.fr){g.fr={};for(const k of Object.keys(this.fr))g.fr[k]=this.fr[k].slice();}
g.P=Object.assign({},this.P);g.A=Object.assign({},this.A);g.F=Object.assign({},this.F);g.M=Object.assign({},this.M);
g.Dc=this.Dc.copyFor(g);
g.q=[];g.stack=[];g.ended=[];g.tnext=this.tnext.slice();g.ghosts=null;g.spare=null;
g.pend=null;g.pendFm=null;g.pendS=null;g.sxfOn=false;g.scopeOn=false;g.scopeDst=false;g.lvOn=false;g.noSpare=false;
g.xfState=4;g.xfT=0;g.xfA=this.xg1;g.xg=g.xg1=this.xg1;
if(this.ghosts.length>=3)this.dropGhost(this.ghosts.shift());
this.ghosts.push(g);
return g;
}
engXf(o){
const held=this.xfHeld?this.xfHeld.slice():[];
this.xfHeld=null;this.xfZC=null;this.xfNeedFm=false;
if(held.length){}else if(this.P.mode>0){
const v=this.monoVoice();
if(v&&v.gate)for(const s of this.stack)held.push({id:s.id,note:s.id===v.id?v.target:s.note,vel:s.vel});
}else{
const hv=this.V.filter(v=>v.on&&v.gate&&v.fade===0).sort((a,b)=>a.age-b.age);
for(const v of hv)held.push({id:v.id,note:v.target,vel:v.vel});
}
const keep=new Set(held.map(h=>h.id));
if(this.sxfOn){
const sg=this.sxfG,a=Math.cos(this.sxt*Math.PI/2);if(sg&&sg.xfState===4){sg.xfA=a;sg.xfT=0;sg.xg=sg.xg1=a;}
this.sxfG=null;this.sxfFin=false;this.xg=this.xg1=Math.sin(this.sxt*Math.PI/2);
}
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&!keep.has(v.id))this.ended.push(v.id);
for(const s of this.stack)if(!keep.has(s.id)&&!this.findId(s.id))this.ended.push(s.id);
const g=this.mkGhost();this.sxfOn=false;this.pendS=null;this.xfLen=EXF_N;
const V=this.spare.length?this.spare.pop():Array.from({length:POOL},(_,i)=>new Voice(i));
for(const v of V){v.on=false;v.id=-1;v.fade=0;v.gate=false;}
this.V=V;this.stack=[];
const fmWas=this.pendFm;this.setParams(o);this.fmPendApply();
if(this.P.eng===3){
this.xfState=0;this.xg=this.xg1=1;g.xfN=Math.round(clamp(MKW.atk(this.mk.T.e2.a),EXF_N/SR,.5)*SR);this.mk.adopt(held,EXF_N);return;
}
this.xfState=3;this.xfT=0;this.xg=this.xg1=0;
const att=this.P.eng===1&&this.P.fmAmp<1?0:this.P.aA;
g.xfN=Math.round(clamp(att||0,EXF_N/SR,.5)*SR);
const one=this.P.mode>0?held.length>0:held.length===1;
const gv=one?(g.P.mode>0?g.monoVoice():g.findId(held[0].id)):null;
this.xfHeld=held;this.xfWait=2;this.xfNeedFm=this.P.eng===1&&!fmWas;
this.xfZC=this.P.eng===1&&gv?{g,v:gv,prev:0,left:Math.round(SR/25),ok:false}:null;
this.xfTry();
}
mkEng(o,fm){
const m=this.pend?Object.assign({},this.pend,o):o;this.pend=null;
if(o.eng===3){
let active=false;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){active=true;break;}
if(active||this.xfHeld){this.engXf(m);return;}
this.setParams(m);if(fm)this.fmMsg(fm);return;
}
const held=this.mk.busy()?this.mk.handOver(EXF_N):[];
if(this.sxfOn||this.pendS){this.sxfOn=false;this.pendS=null;this.sxfG=null;}
this.setParams(m);if(fm)this.fmMsg(fm);
if(!held.length){this.xfState=0;this.xg=this.xg1=1;return;}
this.xfState=3;this.xfT=0;this.xg=this.xg1=0;this.xfLen=EXF_N;
this.xfHeld=held;this.xfWait=2;this.xfNeedFm=this.P.eng===1&&!fm;this.xfZC=null;
this.xfTry();
}
xfTry(){
if(this.xfHeld&&!this.xfNeedFm&&(!this.xfZC||this.xfZC.ok)){this.xfZC=null;this.engStart(this.xfHeld);}
}
engStart(held){
this.xfHeld=null;this.xfZC=null;this.xfNeedFm=false;this.xfT=0;
if(this.P.mode>0){
if(held.length){const t=held[held.length-1];this.stack=held.map(h=>({id:h.id,note:h.note,vel:h.vel}));this.startVoice(this.freeSlot(),t.id,t.note,t.vel,-1);}
}else for(const h of held){this.stealIfFull();this.startVoice(this.freeSlot(),h.id,h.note,h.vel,-1);}
}
heldDrop(id){
const H=this.xfHeld;if(!H)return false;
const k=H.findIndex(h=>h.id===id);if(k<0)return false;
H.splice(k,1);this.ended.push(id);if(!H.length){this.xfZC=null;this.xfNeedFm=false;this.engStart(H);}return true;
}
heldAllEnd(){const H=this.xfHeld;if(H){for(const h of H)this.ended.push(h.id);this.xfHeld=null;this.xfZC=null;this.xfNeedFm=false;}}
dropGhost(g){for(const v of g.V){v.on=false;v.id=-1;v.fade=0;}if(!g.noSpare&&this.spare.length<2)this.spare.push(g.V);}
xfBlock(){
if(this.sxfFin){this.sxfFin=false;const g=this.sxfG;this.sxfG=null;if(g)g.xfState=5;this.sxfEnd();}
this.xg=this.xg1;
if(this.xfState>=3){
this.xfT+=CR;const x=Math.min(1,this.xfT/(this.xfState===4?this.xfN||EXF_N:this.xfLen||EXF_N));
if(this.xfState===3&&this.sxfOn){
this.sxt=Math.max(0,(this.xfT-SXM_N)/SXF_N);this.xg1=1;if(x>=1){this.sxt=1;this.xfState=0;this.sxfFin=true;}return;
}
if(this.xfState===3){if(this.xfHeld){this.xfT=0;this.xg1=0;return;}this.xg1=Math.sin(x*Math.PI/2);if(x>=1){this.xg1=1;this.xfState=0;this.sxfEnd();}}
else{this.xg1=x>=1?0:this.xfA*Math.cos(x*Math.PI/2);if(x>=1)this.xfState=5;}
return;
}
if(this.xfState===1){this.xg1=Math.max(0,this.xg-XF_D);if(this.xg===0){const o=this.pend;this.pend=null;if(o)this.setParams(o);this.fmPendApply();this.xfState=2;this.xg1=Math.min(1,XF_D);}}
else if(this.xfState===2){this.xg1=Math.min(1,this.xg+XF_D);if(this.xg1>=1){this.xfState=0;if(this.pendS)this.sxfEnd();}}
}
setParams(o){
const P=this.P,mono0=P.mode>0,eng0=P.eng;
for(const k in o)if(k in P&&typeof o[k]==='number'&&isFinite(o[k]))P[k]=o[k];
if('mkLim'in o)this.mk.lim=P.mkLim|0;
if('mkLimS'in o)this.mk.limS=P.mkLimS|0;
if(P.eng!==eng0){
for(const s of this.stack)if(!this.findId(s.id))this.ended.push(s.id);
this.stack.length=0;
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)this.endVoice(v);
}else if(P.eng===1&&('fmAtk'in o||'fmDec'in o||'fmRel'in o||'fmBite'in o)){
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)for(let k=0;k<6;k++)if(v.fix[k]<4)this.fmAdvance(v,k,v.fix[k]);
}
if((P.mode>0)!==mono0){
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.gate)this.releaseV(v);
for(const s of this.stack)if(!this.findId(s.id))this.ended.push(s.id);
this.stack.length=0;
}
this.derive();
}
setMods(list){
this.msrc.fill(0);this.mdst.fill(0);this.mamt.fill(0);
for(let i=0;i<Math.min(list.length,NSLOT);i++){
const s=list[i];this.msrc[i]=s.src|0;this.mdst[i]=s.dst|0;this.mamt[i]=clamp(+s.amt||0,-1,1);
}
if(this.mamB){this.mamB=null;this.lkModsApply();}
this.uniMod=false;for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++)if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mdst[i]===D_UNIDET&&this.mamt[i]!==0)this.uniMod=true;
this.fmRoutesFilt=false;
for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++){const d=this.mdst[i];if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mamt[i]!==0&&(d===D_CUT||d===D_RES||d===D_DRIVE||d===D_FENV))this.fmRoutesFilt=true;}
this.fmBypassCalc();
}
derive(){
const Dc=this.Dc;
if(this.lbP){const PR=this.PR;Object.assign(PR,this.P);for(let q=0;q<PV_AL;q++)if(this.lbPs[q])PR[PVA[q]]=this.lb[q];}
this.deriveInto(Dc,this.lbP?this.PR:this.P);
this.cutN0=Dc.cutN0;this.drv0=Dc.drv0;this.outG0=Dc.outG0;this.stereo=Dc.stereo;this.sync=Dc.sync;this.mix=Dc.mix;
this.fmBypassCalc();
if(this.lkOn)this.lkAll(false);
}
deriveInto(D,P){
envCoefs(D.A,P.aA,P.aD,P.aS,P.aR,P.aCurve);
envCoefs(D.F,P.fA,P.fD,P.fS,P.fR,P.fCurve);
envCoefs(D.M,P.mA,P.mD,P.mS,P.mR,P.mCurve);
this.ks4Der(D,P);
const det=P.uniDet,semis=(0.25*det+0.75*det*det*det*det)*1.8;
const side=-0.73764*P.uniMix*P.uniMix+1.2841*P.uniMix+0.044372,mid=-0.55366*P.uniMix+0.99785;
let anyUni=false;
for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){
const on='o'+(o+1);
D.oW[o]=clamp(P[on+'w']|0,0,4);D.oSemi[o]=P[on+'oct']*12+P[on+'semi']+P[on+'fine']/100;
D.oRat[o]=Math.pow(2,D.oSemi[o]/12);
D.oLvl[o]=P[on+'lvl'];D.oPw[o]=P[on+'pw'];
const n=clamp(P[on+'uni']|0,1,MAXU),pos=UNI[n];D.uniN[o]=n;if(n>1)anyUni=true;
let norm=0;for(let u=0;u<n;u++){const g=n===1?1:(pos[u]===0?mid:side);norm+=g*g;}
norm=1/Math.sqrt(norm);
for(let u=0;u<n;u++){
const k=o*MAXU+u,g=(n===1?1:(pos[u]===0?mid:side))*norm*(n>1?1.4:1);
const pan=n===1?0:(u/(n-1)*2-1)*P.uniWidth;
D.uniG[2*k]=g*Math.sqrt(1-pan);D.uniG[2*k+1]=g*Math.sqrt(1+pan);
D.uniR[k]=Math.pow(2,pos[u]*semis/12);
}
}
D.stereo=anyUni&&P.uniWidth>.01;
D.sync=P.o2sync>=.5?1:0;
D.mix=(P.ftype|0)===6?KF2_VMIX:FMIX[clamp(P.ftype|0,0,5)];
D.cutN0=69+12*Math.log2(clamp(P.cutoff,10,22000)/440);
D.drv0=.6*Math.pow(2,clamp(P.drive,0,1)*4);D.outG0=1/(1+clamp(P.drive,0,1)*1.3);
if(P.eng===1)D.stereo=false;
}
fmBypassCalc(){
const P=this.P;
this.fmBypass=P.eng===1&&P.ftype<=1&&P.cutoff>=18000&&P.res<=.02&&P.fenv===0&&P.drive<=.01&&!this.fmRoutesFilt;
}
lkPrep(ev){
if(ev.length<10)return;
let fm=false;const n=ev[8]|0;
for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const i=ev[9+2*j],p=i<0?-1-i:i;if(p>=0&&p<PV_A&&PVK[p]>=2&&PVK[p]<=7)fm=true;}
if(this.P.eng!==3)this.lkViews(fm);
}
lkViews(fm){
for(const v of this.V){
if(!v.pv){v.pv=new Float32Array(PV_A).fill(NaN);v.pvR=new Uint8Array(PV_A);v.lkF=new Float64Array(PV_A);}
if(!v.VP){v.VP=Object.assign(defaults(),this.P);v.Dv=new LkD(newEnv(),newEnv(),newEnv());}
if(fm&&!v.fmPv){v.fmPv=fmCopy(null,this.fm);v.gxv={fbS:new Float64Array(6),ph0:new Float64Array(6),phm:new Uint8Array(6),am:new Float64Array(6),amG:new Float64Array(6),fmS:new Float64Array(6)};}
}
}
lbPrep(c){
if(c===-2){this.mk.lbPrep();return;}
if(!this.lb){this.lb=new Float32Array(PV_AL).fill(NaN);this.lbT=new Float32Array(PV_AL).fill(NaN);this.lbS=new Float32Array(PV_AL);this.lbR=new Int32Array(PV_AL);this.lbRL=new Int16Array(PV_AL);this.lbPs=new Uint8Array(PV_AL);this.PR=Object.assign(defaults(),this.P);}
if(this.P.eng!==3)this.lkViews(this.P.eng===1);
}
lkStart(v,e){
if(v.lkN||v.D)this.lkClear(v);
if(e&&e.length>9&&v.pv){
const n=Math.min(16,e[8]|0);
for(let j=0;j<n;j++){
const i=e[9+2*j],x=e[10+2*j],p=i<0?-1-i:i;
if(i<=-9000&&i>-9018)continue;if(!(x===x)||typeof x!=='number'||!(p>=0&&p<PV_A)||i!==Math.floor(i)){this.lkBad++;continue;}
if(v.pv[p]!==v.pv[p])v.lkN++;
v.pv[p]=x;v.pvR[p]=i<0?1:0;
}
}
if(this.lbN&&v.pv)for(let p=0;p<PV_A;p++){const y=this.lb[p];if(y===y&&!PVb[p]&&v.pv[p]!==v.pv[p]){v.pv[p]=y;v.lkN++;}}
if(v.lkN||this.lbX){this.lkOn=true;this.lkView(v);}
}
lkClear(v){if(v.pv){v.pv.fill(NaN);v.pvR.fill(0);}v.lkN=0;v.D=null;v.fmP=null;v.gxOn=false;v.lkRm=0;}
lkVal(v,p){const x=v.pv?v.pv[p]:NaN;if(x===x)return x;return this.lb?this.lb[p]:NaN;}
lkView(v){
if(!v.VP)this.lkViews(false);
const P=this.lbP?this.PR:this.P,VP=v.VP;Object.assign(VP,P);
let fm=false,gx=false;
for(let p=0;p<PV_A;p++){
const x=this.lkVal(v,p);if(!(x===x))continue;
const k=PVK[p];
if(k===1)VP[PVA[p]]=x;else if(k===6)gx=true;else fm=true;
}
if(v.ksm&&v.ksm.t)this.ksmVP(v,VP);this.deriveInto(v.Dv,VP);v.D=v.Dv;
if(fm||gx){
if(!v.fmPv)this.lkViews(true);
if(fm){fmCopy(v.fmPv,this.fm);const F=v.fmPv;
for(let p=0;p<PV_A;p++){const k=PVK[p];if(k<2||k>7||k===6)continue;const x=this.lkVal(v,p);if(!(x===x))continue;
if((k===2&&!this.gx)||(k===5&&this.gx)){const op=F.ops[PVO[p]];if(!op)continue;if(PVJ[p]>=0){if(op[PVF[p]])op[PVF[p]][PVJ[p]]=x;}else op[PVF[p]]=x;}
else if((k===3&&!this.gx)||(k===7&&this.gx))F.trans=x;
else if(k===4&&!this.gx&&F.peg)F.peg[PVF[p]][PVJ[p]]=x;}
v.fmP=F;}else v.fmP=null;
if(gx&&this.gx){const X=this.gx,G=v.gxv;G.fbS.set(X.fbS);G.ph0.set(X.ph0);G.phm.set(X.phm);G.am.set(X.am);G.amG.set(X.amG);G.fmS.set(X.fmS);
for(let p=0;p<PV_A;p++){if(PVK[p]!==6)continue;const x=this.lkVal(v,p);if(!(x===x))continue;const o=PVO[p],f=PVF[p];
if(f==='fbAmt')G.fbS[o]=X.fbK[o]>=0&&x>0?Math.pow(2,Math.min(7,x)-8):0;
else if(f==='ph')G.ph0[o]=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,x|0))*33554432;
else if(f==='phm')G.phm[o]=Math.max(0,Math.min(2,Math.round(x)));
else if(f==='lfoFm')G.fmS[o]=LFOFM_HT[Math.max(0,Math.min(127,x|0))]*(X.pmK==null?1:X.pmK);
else if(f==='lfoAm'){const lA=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,x|0)),amK=X.amK==null?-1:X.amK;
if(amK>=0&&X.car[o]){G.am[o]=0;G.amG[o]=amK*.5*(lA<=42?lA/42*.3:lA<=85? .3+(lA-42)/43*.3:.6+(lA-85)/42*.4);}
else G.am[o]=lA*LFOAM_DB*(amK>=0?amK:1);}}
v.gxOn=true;}else v.gxOn=false;
}else{v.fmP=null;v.gxOn=false;}
}
lkAll(fmOnly){
if(this.lb&&this.lbP){Object.assign(this.PR,this.P);for(let q=PV_A;q<PV_AL;q++)if(this.lb[q]===this.lb[q]&&PVK[q]===1)this.PR[PVA[q]]=this.lb[q];}
for(const v of this.V){if(!v.on||!v.D)continue;if(fmOnly&&!v.fmP&&!v.gxOn)continue;this.lkView(v);}
}
lkLegato(v,e){
if(!(e&&e.length>9)&&!v.lkN&&!this.lbN)return;
const F=v.lkF,cur=v.D?v.VP:this.lbP?this.PR:this.P;if(!F)return;
for(let p=0;p<PV_A;p++)if(PVb[p]&&PVK[p]===1)F[p]=cur[PVA[p]];
this.lkStart(v,e);
if(!v.D){this.lkOn=true;this.lkView(v);}
v.lkRm=LK_RM;this.lkRamp(v);
}
lkRamp(v){
const n=--v.lkRm,a=1-n/LK_RM,F=v.lkF,VP=v.VP;if(!VP||!F){v.lkRm=0;return;}
const P=this.lbP?this.PR:this.P;
for(let p=0;p<PV_A;p++){if(!PVb[p]||PVK[p]!==1)continue;const x=this.lkVal(v,p),to=x===x?x:P[PVA[p]];VP[PVA[p]]=F[p]+(to-F[p])*a;}
if(v.ksm&&v.ksm.t)this.ksmVP(v,VP);this.deriveInto(v.Dv,VP);v.D=v.Dv;
if(!n&&!v.lkN&&!this.lbX)v.D=null;
}
n1Hold(v,e){
if(!(e.length>9)||!v.pv||!v.VP||v.fade)return;
const n=Math.min(16,e[8]|0),cur=v.D?v.VP:this.lbP?this.PR:this.P;
let any=false;
for(let j=0;j<n;j++){
const p=e[9+2*j];if(p!==0&&p!==1&&p!==2)continue;
const x=e[10+2*j],c=cur[PVA[p]];
if(!(x===x)||x===c)continue;
if(p===0)v.n1w0=x;else if(p===1)v.n1w1=x;else v.n1w2=x;
v.n1m=(v.n1m|0)|(1<<p);e[10+2*j]=c;any=true;
}
if(any){if(!(v.n1d>0&&v.n1d<=6))v.n1d=1;v.n1id=v.id;}
}
n1Blk(v){
if(v.id!==v.n1id||!v.on){v.n1d=0;v.n1m=0;return;}
const d=v.n1d;
if(d<=6){v.vgain*=1-d/6;v.n1d=d+1;return;}
if(d===7&&v.n1m&&v.VP&&v.Dv){
const m=v.n1m;v.n1m=0;
for(let p=0;p<3;p++)if(m&(1<<p)){const x=p===0?v.n1w0:p===1?v.n1w1:v.n1w2;if(v.pv[p]!==v.pv[p])v.lkN++;v.pv[p]=x;v.VP[PVA[p]]=x;}
this.deriveInto(v.Dv,v.VP);v.D=v.Dv;this.lkOn=true;
}
const u=d-6;v.vgain*=u/6;v.n1d=u>=6?0:d+1;
}
lbEv(p,x,r){
p=p|0;if(!(p>=0&&p<PV_AL)||typeof x!=='number'){this.lkBad++;return;}
if(!this.lb)this.lbPrep(0);
this.lkOn=true;
const was=this.lb[p],ramp=r>0&&PVb[p];
if(p<PV_A)for(const v of this.V)if(v.pvR&&v.pvR[p]){if(ramp&&x===x)v.pvR[p]=2;else{v.pvR[p]=0;v.pv[p]=NaN;v.lkN--;}}
const fresh=!(this.lbR[p]>0);
if(x===x){
if(ramp){const a=was===was?was:this.lbBase(p);if(was!==was)this.lbCnt(p,1);if(fresh){this.lbRm++;this.lbRL[this.lbRc++]=p;}this.lbS[p]=(x-a)/r;this.lb[p]=a-this.lbS[p]*CR;this.lbT[p]=x;this.lbR[p]=r+CR;}
else{if(was!==was)this.lbCnt(p,1);if(!fresh){this.lbR[p]=0;this.lbRm--;}this.lb[p]=x;}
}else if(was===was){
if(ramp){const b=this.lbBase(p);this.lbT[p]=NaN;if(fresh){this.lbRm++;this.lbRL[this.lbRc++]=p;}this.lbS[p]=(b-was)/r;this.lb[p]=was-this.lbS[p]*CR;this.lbR[p]=r+CR;}
else{this.lb[p]=NaN;this.lbCnt(p,-1);if(!fresh){this.lbR[p]=0;this.lbRm--;}}
}else return;
this.lbChanged(p,1);
}
lbBase(p){
const k=PVK[p];
if(k===1)return+this.P[PVA[p]]||0;
if(k===9){const m=this.lkMd&&this.lkMd[p];if(!m)return 0;for(let j=0;j<NSLOT;j++)if(this.msrc[j]===m[0]&&this.mdst[j]===m[1])return this.mamB?this.mamB[j]:this.mamt[j];return 0;}
if(k===2||k===5){const op=this.fm.ops[PVO[p]];if(!op)return 0;const y=PVJ[p]>=0?(op[PVF[p]]||[])[PVJ[p]]:op[PVF[p]];return+y||0;}
if(k===3||k===7)return this.fm.trans|0;
if(k===6){const X=this.gx;return X&&PVF[p]==='fbAmt'&&X.fbS[PVO[p]]>0?Math.log2(X.fbS[PVO[p]])+8:0;}
return 0;
}
lbCnt(p,d){this.lbN+=d;if(PVK[p]!==1&&PVK[p]!==9)this.lbX+=d;}
lbStep(){
const lb=this.lb,L=this.lbCh||(this.lbCh=new Int16Array(PV_AL)),RL=this.lbRL;let n=0,w=0;
for(let j=0;j<this.lbRc;j++){
const p=RL[j],r=this.lbR[p];if(!(r>0))continue;
if(r<=CR){this.lbR[p]=0;this.lbRm--;const t=this.lbT[p];if(t===t)lb[p]=t;else{lb[p]=NaN;this.lbCnt(p,-1);}
if(p<PV_A)for(const v of this.V)if(v.pvR&&v.pvR[p]===2){v.pvR[p]=0;v.pv[p]=NaN;v.lkN--;}}
else{this.lbR[p]=r-CR;lb[p]+=this.lbS[p]*CR;RL[w++]=p;}
L[n++]=p;
}
this.lbRc=w;
if(n)this.lbChanged(-1,n);
}
lbChanged(p,n){
const L=this.lbCh,one=p>=0,cnt=one?1:n,at=j=>(one?p:L[j]);
let mods=false,own=false,core=0;
for(let j=0;j<cnt;j++){const q=at(j),k=PVK[q];
if(k===9)mods=true;
else if(k===1){if(q>=PV_A||PVb[q])core|=this.lkApplyR(q)|1;}
else if(PVb[q])own=true;}
if(mods){let flags=false;for(let j=0;j<cnt;j++){const q=at(j);if(PVK[q]===9&&this.lkModOne(q))flags=true;}if(flags)this.lkModFlags();}
if(core&496){const Dc=this.Dc;this.deriveB(Dc,this.lbP?this.PR:this.P,core);this.cutN0=Dc.cutN0;this.drv0=Dc.drv0;this.outG0=Dc.outG0;}
if(!core&&!own){this.lkOnChk();return;}
const fm=this.P.eng===1;
for(const v of this.V){
if(!v.on)continue;
if(!v.D){if(own){this.lkView(v);if(fm)this.fmVoicePatch(v,true);}continue;}
let need=0;for(let j=0;j<cnt;j++){const q=at(j);if(q>=PV_A||!PVb[q]||PVK[q]===9)continue;need|=this.lkApply(v,q);}
if(need&2){this.lkView(v);if(fm)this.fmVoicePatch(v,true);continue;}
if(need&496)this.deriveB(v.Dv,v.VP,need);
if(fm&&(need&4))this.fmVoicePatch(v,true);
}
this.lkOnChk();
}
lkOnChk(){if(this.lbN)return;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.lkN)return;this.lkOn=false;}
lkApplyR(q){
const x=this.lb[q],k=PVA[q],on=x===x?1:0;
if(!this.PR)this.PR=Object.assign(defaults(),this.P);
this.PR[k]=on?x:this.P[k];
if(this.lbPs[q]!==on){this.lbPs[q]=on;this.lbPn+=on?1:-1;this.lbP=this.lbPn>0;}
if(q>=PV_A)for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.D)v.VP[k]=this.PR[k];
return q<PV_A?PVD[q]:0;
}
lkApply(v,q){
const k=PVK[q],x=this.lkVal(v,q),y=x===x;
if(k===1){if(q>=PV_A)return 0;const P=this.lbP?this.PR:this.P,nm=PVA[q];v.VP[nm]=y?x:P[nm];return PVD[q];}
if(k===9)return 0;
const X=this.gx;
if(k===6){if(!v.gxOn||!X)return 2;const G=v.gxv,o=PVO[q],f=PVF[q];
if(f==='fbAmt')G.fbS[o]=y?(X.fbK[o]>=0&&x>0?Math.pow(2,Math.min(7,x)-8):0):X.fbS[o];
else if(f==='lfoFm')G.fmS[o]=y?LFOFM_HT[Math.max(0,Math.min(127,x|0))]*(X.pmK==null?1:X.pmK):X.fmS[o];
else if(f==='lfoAm'){if(!y){G.am[o]=X.am[o];G.amG[o]=X.amG[o];}else{const lA=Math.max(0,Math.min(127,x|0)),amK=X.amK==null?-1:X.amK;
if(amK>=0&&X.car[o]){G.am[o]=0;G.amG[o]=amK*.5*(lA<=42?lA/42*.3:lA<=85? .3+(lA-42)/43*.3:.6+(lA-85)/42*.4);}else G.am[o]=lA*LFOAM_DB*(amK>=0?amK:1);}}
return 8;}
if(!v.fmP)return y?2:0;
const F=v.fmP,B=this.fm;
if((k===2&&!X)||(k===5&&X)){const op=F.ops[PVO[q]],b0=B.ops[PVO[q]];if(!op||!b0)return 0;const f=PVF[q];
if(PVJ[q]>=0){if(op[f]&&b0[f])op[f][PVJ[q]]=y?x:b0[f][PVJ[q]];}else op[f]=y?x:b0[f];return 4;}
if((k===3&&!X)||(k===7&&X)){F.trans=y?x:B.trans;return 4;}
if(k===4&&!X&&F.peg&&B.peg){F.peg[PVF[q]][PVJ[q]]=y?x:B.peg[PVF[q]][PVJ[q]];return 4;}
return 0;
}
deriveB(D,P,m){
if(m&256)for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){const K=PV_OK[o];D.oLvl[o]=P[K[3]];D.oPw[o]=P[K[4]];}
if(m&192){const det=P.uniDet,semis=(0.25*det+0.75*det*det*det*det)*1.8;
for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){
if(m&64){const K=PV_OK[o];D.oSemi[o]=P[K[0]]*12+P[K[1]]+P[K[2]]/100;D.oRat[o]=Math.pow(2,D.oSemi[o]/12);}
if(m&128){const n=D.uniN[o],pos=UNI[n];for(let u=0;u<n;u++)D.uniR[o*MAXU+u]=Math.pow(2,pos[u]*semis/12);}
}}
if(m&16)D.cutN0=69+12*Math.log2(clamp(P.cutoff,10,22000)/440);
if(m&32){D.drv0=.6*Math.pow(2,clamp(P.drive,0,1)*4);D.outG0=1/(1+clamp(P.drive,0,1)*1.3);}
}
lkModOne(p){
const M=this.lkMd,m=M&&M[p];if(!m)return false;
const x=this.lb[p];let j=-1,fl=false;
for(let s=0;s<NSLOT;s++)if(this.msrc[s]===m[0]&&this.mdst[s]===m[1]){j=s;break;}
if(x===x){
if(j<0){for(let s=0;s<NSLOT;s++)if(!this.msrc[s]){j=s;break;}if(j<0)return false;this.msrc[j]=m[0];this.mdst[j]=m[1];this.mamt[j]=0;fl=true;}
if(!this.mamB)this.mamB=Float64Array.from(this.mamt);
const y=clamp(x,-1,1);if((y===0)!==(this.mamt[j]===0))fl=true;this.mamt[j]=y;
}else if(j>=0&&this.mamB){const y=this.mamB[j];if((y===0)!==(this.mamt[j]===0))fl=true;this.mamt[j]=y;}
return fl;
}
lkModFlags(){
this.uniMod=false;for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++)if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mdst[i]===D_UNIDET&&this.mamt[i]!==0)this.uniMod=true;
this.fmRoutesFilt=false;
for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++){const d=this.mdst[i];if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mamt[i]!==0&&(d===D_CUT||d===D_RES||d===D_DRIVE||d===D_FENV))this.fmRoutesFilt=true;}
this.fmBypassCalc();
}
lkModsApply(){
const lb=this.lb,M=this.lkMd;if(!lb||!M)return;
for(const k in M){
const p=+k,m=M[k],x=lb[p];let j=-1;
for(let s=0;s<NSLOT;s++)if(this.msrc[s]===m[0]&&this.mdst[s]===m[1]){j=s;break;}
if(x===x){
if(j<0){for(let s=0;s<NSLOT;s++)if(!this.msrc[s]){j=s;break;}if(j<0)continue;this.msrc[j]=m[0];this.mdst[j]=m[1];this.mamt[j]=0;}
if(!this.mamB){this.mamB=Float64Array.from(this.mamt);}
this.mamt[j]=clamp(x,-1,1);
}else if(j>=0&&this.mamB)this.mamt[j]=this.mamB[j];
}
this.uniMod=false;for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++)if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mdst[i]===D_UNIDET&&this.mamt[i]!==0)this.uniMod=true;
this.fmRoutesFilt=false;
for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++){const d=this.mdst[i];if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mamt[i]!==0&&(d===D_CUT||d===D_RES||d===D_DRIVE||d===D_FENV))this.fmRoutesFilt=true;}
this.fmBypassCalc();
}
lkProbeRep(){
const fr=this.curF;if(fr<(this.lkRepT||0))return;this.lkRepT=fr+(this.lkRepI||Math.round(SR/30));
const out=[];
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){const D=v.D||this.Dc,VP=v.D?v.VP:this.P,F=v.fmP||this.fm;
out.push({id:v.id,note:v.note,own:!!v.D,cut:440*Math.pow(2,(D.cutN0-69)/12),res:VP.res,level:VP.level,aD:VP.aD,o1w:D.oW[0],ftype:VP.ftype,uni:D.uniN[0],
ol:F.ops?F.ops.map(o=>o.ol):null,fol:Array.from(v.foutLvl),fmBright:VP.fmBright,n:v.lkN});}
this.port.postMessage({lkRep:out,mk:this.P.eng===3?this.mk.lkRep():null,f:fr});
}
lkInfoOf(){let n=0,vs=0;for(const v of this.V){if(v.pv)n++;if(v.on&&v.lkN)vs++;}return{bad:this.lkBad+this.mk.lkBadN(),tables:n,voices:vs,lanes:this.lbN,on:this.lkOn,bytes:n*(PV_A*5),mods:Array.from(this.mamt),lb:this.lb?Array.from(this.lb,x=>(x===x?x:null)):null};}
findId(id){for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.id===id)return v;return null;}
freeSlot(){
for(const v of this.V)if(!v.on)return v;
let best=null;for(const v of this.V)if(v.fade>0&&(!best||v.fade<best.fade))best=v;
if(best){this.endVoice(best);return best;}
best=this.V[0];for(const v of this.V)if(v.age<best.age)best=v;
this.endVoice(best);return best;
}
stealIfFull(){
const gx_=this.P.eng===1?this.gx:null,lim2=gx_&&this.P.xfLite>=1&&(gx_.nE>FMX_EDGE_CAP||gx_.anyLoop);
const capS=gx_&&gx_.nE>FMX_EDGE_CAP?6:8,poly=clamp(this.P.poly|0,1,lim2?4:capS);
const held=this.P.polyH>0?clamp(this.P.polyH|0,poly,capS):poly;
let n=0,ng=0;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.fade===0){n++;if(v.gate)ng++;}
while(n>=poly){
let victim=null;
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.fade===0&&!v.gate&&(!victim||v.aOut<victim.aOut))victim=v;
if(!victim&&ng>=held)for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.fade===0&&(!victim||v.age<victim.age))victim=v;
if(!victim)break;
if(victim.gate)ng--;
this.fadeOut(victim,STEAL_N);n--;
}
}
fadeOut(v,n){v.fade=n;v.fadeStep=v.fadeG/n;}
endVoice(v){if(v.on){v.on=false;v.fade=0;this.ended.push(v.id);v.id=-1;if(v.lkN||v.D)this.lkClear(v);}}
startVoice(v,id,note,vel,from){
if(this.lkOn||this.lkEv)this.lkStart(v,this.lkEv);this.ksmV(v,this.lkEv);
const P=v.D?v.VP:this.P;
v.on=true;v.id=id;v.gate=true;v.age=++this.ageCtr;v.vel=vel;v.note=note;v.target=note;
v.pitch=from>=0&&P.glide>0?from:note;
v.gcoef=P.glide>0?1-Math.exp(-CR/(P.glide/3*SR)):1;
v.aSt=1;v.aOut=0;v.fSt=1;v.fOut=0;v.mSt=1;v.mOut=0;v.fade=0;v.fadeG=1;v.t0=0;
v.s1=v.s2=v.s3=v.s4=v.r1=v.r2=v.r3=v.r4=0;
const rnd=()=>{let x=v.rng;x^=x<<13;x^=x>>>17;x^=x<<5;v.rng=x>>>0;return(x>>>0)/4294967296;};
for(let k=0;k<NOSC*MAXU;k++)v.ph[k]=(P.reset&&k%MAXU===0)?0:rnd();
v.subPh=P.reset?0:rnd();v.sp.fill(0);
v.dP=(rnd()*2-1)*P.analog*.08;v.dC=(rnd()*2-1)*P.analog*1.5;v.rnd=rnd()*2-1;
for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){v.dO[o]=(rnd()*2-1)*P.analog*.05;v.dR[o]=Math.pow(2,v.dO[o]/12);}
v.pan0=(v.idx%2?1:-1)*P.spread*(0.5+0.5*rnd());v.panL0=Math.sqrt(1-v.pan0);v.panR0=Math.sqrt(1+v.pan0);
v.l1=P.l1retrig?0:this.gl1;v.l2=P.l2retrig?0:this.gl2;v.sh1=rnd()*2-1;v.sh2=rnd()*2-1;
v.gA=v.gB=-1;
v.pl0=v.pr0=-1;v.lvOk=false;v.sxk=-1;v.pol=1;
this.kf2Start(v);
if(P.eng===1)this.fmNoteOn(v);
}
runEvents(endFrame){
const q=this.q;
while(q.length&&q[0][0]<endFrame){
const e=q.shift(),type=e[1],id=e[2];
if(type===11){if(e[3]===-2)this.mk.lbEv(e[4],e[5],e[6]);else this.lbEv(e[4],e[5],e[6]);continue;}
if(type===1&&this.P.eng===3){this.mk.noteOn(id,e[3],e[4],e[6]|0,e.length>8?e:null);continue;}
if(type===9||type===10){if(this.P.eng===3)this.mk.keysEv(type);continue;}
if((type===2||type===3||type===7)&&this.mkHas(id)){if(type===2)this.mk.noteOff(id);else if(type===3)this.mk.retune(id,e[3],e[4]);else this.mk.fastOff(id,e[3]);continue;}
if(type===5)this.mk.allOff();else if(type===6)this.mk.panic();
if(type===1){this.lkEv=e.length>8?e:null;this.noteOn(id,e[3],e[4],e[5]==null?-1:e[5]);this.lkEv=null;}
else if(type===2)this.noteOff(id);
else if(type===3)this.retune(id,e[3],e[4]);
else if(type===7){const v=this.P.mode>0?null:this.findId(id);if(v)this.fadeOut(v,Math.max(64,Math.round(e[3]*5*SR)));else this.noteOff(id);}
else if(type===5){for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.gate)this.releaseV(v);this.stack.length=0;this.heldAllEnd();}
else if(type===6){for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)this.fadeOut(v,64);this.stack.length=0;this.heldAllEnd();for(const g of this.ghosts)for(const v of g.V)if(v.on)g.fadeOut(v,64);}
}
}
mkHas(id){return this.mk.has(id);}
noteOn(id,note,vel,from){
if(this.P.mode>0)return this.monoOn(id,note,vel,from);
this.stealIfFull();
this.startVoice(this.freeSlot(),id,note,vel,from);
}
releaseV(v){v.gate=false;if(v.aSt)v.aSt=4;if(v.fSt)v.fSt=4;if(v.mSt)v.mSt=4;}
noteOff(id){
if(this.xfHeld&&this.heldDrop(id))return;
if(this.P.mode>0&&this.stack.some(s=>s.id===id))return this.monoOff(id);
const v=this.findId(id);
if(v){if(v.gate)this.releaseV(v);return;}
const k=this.stack.findIndex(s=>s.id===id);if(k>=0){this.stack.splice(k,1);this.ended.push(id);}
}
retune(id,note,tau){
if(this.P.mode>0){
const e=this.stack.find(s=>s.id===id);if(e)e.note=note;
const top=this.stack[this.stack.length-1];if(!top||top.id!==id)return;
}
const v=this.P.mode>0?this.monoVoice():this.findId(id);if(!v)return;
v.target=note;v.note=note;v.gcoef=tau>0?1-Math.exp(-CR/(tau*SR)):1;
}
monoVoice(){for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.fade===0)return v;return null;}
dropStack(id){const i=this.stack.findIndex(s=>s.id===id);if(i>=0)this.stack.splice(i,1);}
monoOn(id,note,vel,from){
const P=this.P,v=this.monoVoice();
this.stack.push({id,note,vel});
if(!v){this.startVoice(this.freeSlot(),id,note,vel,from);return;}
const wasGated=v.gate;
if(!wasGated)this.ended.push(v.id);
v.id=id;v.note=note;v.target=note;v.vel=vel;v.gate=true;
if(this.lkEv&&v.aOut>1e-3)this.n1Hold(v,this.lkEv);
if(this.lkOn||this.lkEv)this.lkLegato(v,this.lkEv);
v.gcoef=P.glide>0&&(P.mode===1||wasGated)?1-Math.exp(-CR/(P.glide/3*SR)):1;
if(P.mode===1||!wasGated){v.aSt=1;v.fSt=1;v.mSt=1;if(P.eng===1)this.fmRetrig(v);this.kf2Re(v);}
}
monoOff(id){
const st=this.stack,i=st.findIndex(s=>s.id===id);if(i<0)return;
st.splice(i,1);
const v=this.monoVoice();
if(!v||v.id!==id){this.ended.push(id);return;}
const nx=st[st.length-1];
if(nx){this.ended.push(id);v.id=nx.id;v.note=nx.note;v.target=nx.note;}
else this.releaseV(v);
}
lfoVal(shape,ph,sh){
switch(shape){
case 0:return SINE[(ph*SINE_N)|0];
case 1:return ph<.5?4*ph-1:3-4*ph;
case 2:return 2*ph-1;
case 3:return ph<.5?1:-1;
default:return sh;
}
}
control(frame){
KF_F=frame;
if(this.lkOn&&this.lbRm)this.lbStep();
if(this.lkTap)this.lkProbeRep();
const P=this.lbP?this.PR:this.P,src=this.src,dst=this.dst,step=CR*ISR;
this.tempoAt(frame);
if(this.ks4T&&this.ks4B!==this.bpm)this.derive();
const songT=frame*ISR-this.songStart,spb=60/this.bpm;
const r1=P.l1sync>0?1/(P.l1sync*spb):P.l1rate,r2=P.l2sync>0?1/(P.l2sync*spb):P.l2rate;
const og1=this.gl1,og2=this.gl2;
if(P.l1sync>0&&this.songStart>=0)this.gl1=((songT/spb/P.l1sync)%1+1)%1;else this.gl1=(this.gl1+r1*step)%1;
if(P.l2sync>0&&this.songStart>=0)this.gl2=((songT/spb/P.l2sync)%1+1)%1;else this.gl2=(this.gl2+r2*step)%1;
if(this.gl1<og1)this.gsh1=Math.random()*2-1;
if(this.gl2<og2)this.gsh2=Math.random()*2-1;
if((P.l1shape|0)===5){const q=this.lfSongPh(frame,P.l1sync);this.g51=q>=0?q:this.gl1;}
if((P.l2shape|0)===5){const q=this.lfSongPh(frame,P.l2sync);this.g52=q>=0?q:this.gl2;}
let fv=null;
if(this.scopeOn)for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.fade===0&&(!fv||(v.gate&&!fv.gate)||(v.gate===fv.gate&&v.age>fv.age)))fv=v;
for(const v of this.V){
if(!v.on)continue;
v.t0+=step;
const D=v.D||this.Dc,VP=v.D?v.VP:P;
if(v.lkRm)this.lkRamp(v);
v.fOut=envBlock(D.F,v.fSt,v.fOut);v.fSt=ENV_ST;
v.mOut=envBlock(D.M,v.mSt,v.mOut);v.mSt=ENV_ST;
let p1,p2;
if(P.l1retrig){const o=v.l1;v.l1=(v.l1+r1*step)%1;if(v.l1<o)v.sh1=Math.random()*2-1;p1=v.l1;}else p1=this.gl1;
if(P.l2retrig){const o=v.l2;v.l2=(v.l2+r2*step)%1;if(v.l2<o)v.sh2=Math.random()*2-1;p2=v.l2;}else p2=this.gl2;
const fade1=P.l1fade>0?Math.min(1,v.t0/P.l1fade):1,fade2=P.l2fade>0?Math.min(1,v.t0/P.l2fade):1;
const sh1_=P.l1shape|0,sh2_=P.l2shape|0;
src[S_LFO1]=(sh1_===5?this.lf5(1,P.l1retrig?p1:this.g51,P.l1sync):this.lfoVal(sh1_,p1,P.l1retrig?v.sh1:this.gsh1))*fade1;
src[S_LFO2]=(sh2_===5?this.lf5(2,P.l2retrig?p2:this.g52,P.l2sync):this.lfoVal(sh2_,p2,P.l2retrig?v.sh2:this.gsh2))*fade2;
src[S_FENV]=v.fOut;src[S_MENV]=v.mOut;src[S_VEL]=v.vel;src[S_KEY]=(v.pitch-60)/24;
src[S_MW]=P.mw;src[S_RND]=v.rnd;src[S_AENV]=v.aOut;
if(this.ks4L||v.ksm)this.ksmLfo(v,P,src,step,r1,r2);
if(v===fv){this.sc.set(src);this.scHas=true;}
dst.fill(0);
for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++)if(this.msrc[i]&&this.mdst[i])dst[this.mdst[i]]+=this.mamt[i]*src[this.msrc[i]];
if(P.kfSh1||P.kfSh2)this.kf1ShQ(v,P,src,dst);
if(this.ks4P||v.ksm)this.ksmCtl(v,P,src,dst,step);
if(v===fv&&this.scopeDst)this.scD.set(dst);
if(this.uniMod){
const det=clamp(VP.uniDet+dst[D_UNIDET],0,1),semis=(0.25*det+0.75*det*det*det*det)*1.8;
for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){const nu=D.uniN[o],pos=UNI[nu];for(let u=0;u<nu;u++)v.ur[o*MAXU+u]=Math.pow(2,pos[u]*semis/12);}
}
v.pitch+=(v.target-v.pitch)*v.gcoef;
if(P.eng===1)this.fmControl(v,dst);
else{
const fb=lut(FREQ,v.pitch+v.dP+dst[D_PITCH]*24)*ISR;
for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){
let inc0=fb*D.oRat[o]*v.dR[o];const nu=D.uniN[o];
if(o===1&&dst[D_O2PITCH]!==0)inc0*=lut(FREQ,69+dst[D_O2PITCH]*24)/440;
const UR=this.uniMod?v.ur:D.uniR;
for(let u=0;u<nu;u++)v.inc[o*MAXU+u]=Math.min(inc0*UR[o*MAXU+u],.45);
const lt=clamp(D.oLvl[o]+dst[D_O1+o],0,1.5)*.5,pt=clamp(D.oPw[o]+dst[D_PW]*.45,.03,.97);
if(v.lvOk){v.lvl[o]+=(lt-v.lvl[o])*.3;v.pw[o]+=(pt-v.pw[o])*.3;if(Math.abs(v.lvl[o]-lt)<1e-5)v.lvl[o]=lt;}else{v.lvl[o]=lt;v.pw[o]=pt;}
}
v.subInc=Math.min(v.inc[0]/(VP.subOct>=2?4:2),.45);
const sT=VP.subLvl*.5,nT=clamp(VP.noise+dst[D_NOISE],0,1)*.4;
if(v.lvOk){v.subL+=(sT-v.subL)*.3;v.noiseL+=(nT-v.noiseL)*.3;if(v.subL<1e-6&&sT===0)v.subL=0;if(v.noiseL<1e-6&&nT===0)v.noiseL=0;}else{v.subL=sT;v.noiseL=nT;}
if(VP.kfRm>0||v.kfRL>0)this.kf1RingLvl(v,VP);
v.lvOk=true;
}
const velF=1-VP.fvel+VP.fvel*v.vel;
const cutN=D.cutN0+VP.kt*(v.pitch-60)+v.dC
+clamp(VP.fenv+dst[D_FENV],-1,1)*72*v.fOut*velF+dst[D_CUT]*120;
const g=lut(GTAN,cutN<0?0:cutN);
if(v===fv){const q=this.ksV||(this.ksV=new Float64Array(5));q[0]=440*Math.pow(2,((cutN>0?cutN:0)-69)/12);q[1]=v.aOut*Math.max(0,1+dst[D_AMP]);q[2]=v.pitch+dst[D_PITCH]*24;q[3]=v.pitch;q[4]=v.t0;}
const k=clamp(VP.res+dst[D_RES],0,1.15)*4,fresh=v.gB<0;
v.gA=fresh?g:v.gB;v.gB=g;v.kA=fresh?k:v.kB;v.kB=k;
if(dst[D_DRIVE]!==0){const drive=clamp(VP.drive+dst[D_DRIVE],0,1);v.drv=.6*lut(FREQ,69+drive*48)/440;v.outG=1/(1+drive*1.3);}
else{v.drv=D.drv0;v.outG=D.outG0;}
v.comp=1+VP.bass*k;
const vg=1-VP.ampVel+VP.ampVel*v.vel;
v.vgain=VP.level*vg*Math.max(0,1+dst[D_AMP]);
if(v.n1d)this.n1Blk(v);
if(dst[D_PAN]!==0){const pan=clamp(v.pan0+dst[D_PAN],-1,1);v.panL=Math.sqrt(1-pan);v.panR=Math.sqrt(1+pan);}
else{v.panL=v.panL0;v.panR=v.panR0;}
}
}
renderOsc(v,o,st){
const D=v.D||this.Dc,bl=this.bl,br=this.br,nu=D.uniN[o],w=D.oW[o],lv=v.lvl[o],pw=v.pw[o];
const rec=o===0&&D.sync===1,wr=v.wr;
if(lv<=0){if(rec)this.masterPhase(v);return;}
for(let u=0;u<nu;u++){
const k=o*MAXU+u,dt=v.inc[k],gl=D.uniG[2*k]*lv,gr=D.uniG[2*k+1]*lv,wb=u*CR;
if(rec)wr.fill(-1,wb,wb+CR);
let t=v.ph[k];
if(w===W_SAW){
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
t+=dt;if(t>=1){t-=1;if(rec)wr[wb+n]=t/dt;}
let y=2*t-1;
if(t<dt){const x=t/dt;y-=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t>1-dt){const x=(t-1)/dt;y-=x*x+x+x+1;}
bl[n]+=y*gl;if(st)br[n]+=y*gr;
}
}else if(w===W_PULSE){
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
t+=dt;if(t>=1){t-=1;if(rec)wr[wb+n]=t/dt;}
let y=t<pw?1:-1;
if(t<dt){const x=t/dt;y+=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t>1-dt){const x=(t-1)/dt;y+=x*x+x+x+1;}
let t2=t-pw;if(t2<0)t2+=1;
if(t2<dt){const x=t2/dt;y-=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t2>1-dt){const x=(t2-1)/dt;y-=x*x+x+x+1;}
y-=2*pw-1;
bl[n]+=y*gl;if(st)br[n]+=y*gr;
}
}else if(w===W_TRI){
const c=8*dt/6;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
t+=dt;if(t>=1){t-=1;if(rec)wr[wb+n]=t/dt;}
let y=t<.5?4*t-1:3-4*t;
if(t<dt){const d=1-t/dt;y+=c*d*d*d;}else if(t>1-dt){const d=1-(1-t)/dt;y+=c*d*d*d;}
const e=t-.5,ae=e<0?-e:e;
if(ae<dt){const d=1-ae/dt;y-=c*d*d*d;}
bl[n]+=y*gl;if(st)br[n]+=y*gr;
}
}else if(w===4)t=this.kf2Osc(v,k,t,dt,gl,gr,st,rec,wb);
else{
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
t+=dt;if(t>=1){t-=1;if(rec)wr[wb+n]=t/dt;}
const x=t*SINE_N,i=x|0,y=SINE[i]+(SINE[i+1]-SINE[i])*(x-i);
bl[n]+=y*gl;if(st)br[n]+=y*gr;
}
}
v.ph[k]=t;
}
}
masterPhase(v){
const nu=(v.D||this.Dc).uniN[0],wr=v.wr;
for(let u=0;u<nu;u++){
const k=u,dt=v.inc[k],wb=u*CR;let t=v.ph[k];wr.fill(-1,wb,wb+CR);
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){t+=dt;if(t>=1){t-=1;wr[wb+n]=t/dt;}}
v.ph[k]=t;
}
}
renderSlave(v,st){
const D=v.D||this.Dc,bl=this.bl,br=this.br,nu=D.uniN[1],nm=D.uniN[0],w=D.oW[1],lv=v.lvl[1],pw=v.pw[1],wr=v.wr;
if(lv<=0)return;
if(w===4){this.renderOsc(v,1,st);return;}
const W=w===W_SAW?(t=>2*t-1):w===W_PULSE?(t=>(t<pw?1:-1)):w===W_TRI?(t=>(t<.5?4*t-1:3-4*t))
:(t=>{const x=t*SINE_N,i=x|0;return SINE[i]+(SINE[i+1]-SINE[i])*(x-i);});
const dc=w===W_PULSE?2*pw-1:0;
for(let u=0;u<nu;u++){
const k=MAXU+u,dt=v.inc[k],gl=D.uniG[2*k]*lv,gr=D.uniG[2*k+1]*lv,wb=(u%nm)*CR;
let t=v.ph[k],prev=v.sp[u],cPrev=0,cNow=0;
const step=(xs,h)=>{cPrev+=.5*h*xs*xs;cNow-=.5*h*(1-xs)*(1-xs);};
const seg=w===W_PULSE?(a,b,e)=>{if(a<pw&&b>=pw)step(e+(b-pw)/dt,-2);}:()=>{};
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
const t0=t,xm=wr[wb+n];
cPrev=0;cNow=0;
if(xm>=0){
let tb=t0+(1-xm)*dt;
if(tb>=1){seg(t0,1,xm+(tb-1)/dt);step(xm+(tb-1)/dt,w===W_SAW?-2:w===W_PULSE?2:0);tb-=1;seg(0,tb,xm);}
else seg(t0,tb,xm);
step(xm,W(0)-W(tb));
t=xm*dt;seg(0,t,0);
}else if(t+dt<1&&!(w===W_PULSE&&t<pw&&t+dt>=pw)){
t+=dt;
}else{
t+=dt;
if(t>=1){const e=(t-1)/dt;seg(t0,1,e);step(e,w===W_SAW?-2:w===W_PULSE?2:0);t-=1;seg(0,t,0);}
else seg(t0,t,0);
}
const y=W(t)+cNow;prev+=cPrev;
const o=prev-dc;bl[n]+=o*gl;if(st)br[n]+=o*gr;
prev=y;
}
v.ph[k]=t;v.sp[u]=prev;
}
}
renderVoice(v,L,R,off){
if(this.P.eng===1)return this.fmRenderVoice(v,L,R,off);
const D=v.D||this.Dc,bl=this.bl,br=this.br,st=D.stereo;
bl.fill(0);if(st)br.fill(0);
const kfR=v.kfRL>0&&this.kf1Pre(v,D);
if(D.sync){this.renderOsc(v,0,st);this.renderSlave(v,st);this.renderOsc(v,2,st);}
else for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++)this.renderOsc(v,o,st);
if(kfR)this.kf1Ring(v,D,st);
if(v.subL>0){
let t=v.subPh;const dt=v.subInc,g=v.subL,sine=(v.D?v.VP:this.P).subW===1;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
t+=dt;if(t>=1)t-=1;
let y;
if(sine){const x=t*SINE_N,i=x|0;y=SINE[i]+(SINE[i+1]-SINE[i])*(x-i);}
else{
y=t<.5?1:-1;
if(t<dt){const x=t/dt;y+=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t>1-dt){const x=(t-1)/dt;y+=x*x+x+x+1;}
let t2=t-.5;if(t2<0)t2+=1;
if(t2<dt){const x=t2/dt;y-=x+x-x*x-1;}else if(t2>1-dt){const x=(t2-1)/dt;y-=x*x+x+x+1;}
}
bl[n]+=y*g;if(st)br[n]+=y*g;
}
v.subPh=t;
}
const kfNc=v.noiseL>0?(v.D?v.VP:this.lbP?this.PR:this.P).kfNc|0:0;
if(kfNc)this.kf1Noise(v,kfNc,st);
else if(v.noiseL>0){
let x=v.rng;const g=v.noiseL;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){x^=x<<13;x^=x>>>17;x^=x<<5;const y=(x|0)*4.656612873e-10*g;bl[n]+=y;if(st)br[n]+=y;}
v.rng=x>>>0;
}
const ks4F=this.ks4F&&this.ks4Pre(v,st);
if(this.P.lq>=1){this.ladder(v,bl,false);if(st)this.ladder(v,br,true);}
else{this.ladderEco(v,bl,false);if(st)this.ladderEco(v,br,true);}
if(ks4F)this.ks4Post(v,st);
const A=D.A,gain=v.vgain*v.outG;
let s=v.aSt,e=v.aOut,fg=v.fadeG;
const fs=v.fade>0?v.fadeStep:0;
const xg=(this.sxfOn?1:this.xg1)*v.pol;
const pl1=gain*v.panL*xg,pr1=gain*v.panR*xg;
let pl=v.pl0<0?pl1:v.pl0,pr=v.pr0<0?pr1:v.pr0;
const dpl=(pl1-pl)/CR,dpr=(pr1-pr)/CR;
v.pl0=pl1;v.pr0=pr1;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
pl+=dpl;pr+=dpr;
if(s===1){e=A.aT+(e-A.aT)*A.aC;if(e>=1){e=1;s=2;}}
else if(s===2){e=A.dT+(e-A.dT)*A.dC;if(e<=A.s){e=A.s;s=3;}}
else if(s===3)e+=(A.s-e)*.002;
else if(s===4){e=A.rT+(e-A.rT)*A.rC;if(e<=0){e=0;s=0;}}
if(fs){fg-=fs;if(fg<0)fg=0;}
const a=e*fg;
L[off+n]+=bl[n]*a*pl;
R[off+n]+=(st?br[n]:bl[n])*a*pr;
}
v.aSt=s;v.aOut=e;v.fadeG=fg;
if(v.fade>0){v.fade-=CR;if(v.fade<=0||fg<=0)this.endVoice(v);}
else if(s===0||(s===3&&A.s<=0&&e<1e-4))this.endVoice(v);
}
fmMsg(P){
if(!P||!Array.isArray(P.ops)||P.ops.length<6)return;
if(this.pend){this.pendFm=P;return;}
if(this.xfState===3&&!this.sxfOn){
if(this.xfHeld){this.setFm(P,false);this.xfNeedFm=false;this.xfTry();}
else this.setFm(P,true);
return;
}
if(this.pendS){this.pendS.fm=P;return;}
let active=false;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){active=true;break;}
if(active&&this.P.eng===1&&this.fmStruct(P)){
if(this.P.xfLite>=1&&!this.sxfOn){this.pendFm=P;this.pend={};this.xfState=1;return;}
this.structReq(null,P);return;
}
this.setFm(P,true);
}
fmPendApply(){const f=this.pendFm;if(f){this.pendFm=null;this.setFm(f,true);}}
fmStruct(P){return fmxSig(P)!==(this.gx?this.gx.sig:'a'+(this.fm.alg|0));}
setFm(P,live){
if(!P||!Array.isArray(P.ops)||P.ops.length<6)return;
const c=P.x?fmxCompile(P.x):null;
if(c){this.fm=c.fm;this.alg=c.alg;this.gx=c.gx;this.fbShift=0;}
else{
this.gx=null;
this.fm=P;this.alg=ALGS[(P.alg|0)-1]||ALGS[0];
this.fbShift=P.fb>0?Math.pow(2,(P.fb|0)-8):0;
}
if(live&&this.gx&&this.gx.anyFree)for(let s=0;s<12;s++){const E=this.gx.pe[s];if(E&&E.free&&this.fr.pM[s]===2)this.ptRebase(this.fr,s,E,s<6?-192:0,null);}
if(this.lkOn)this.lkAll(true);
if(live&&this.P.eng===1)for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)this.fmVoicePatch(v,true);
}
fmNoteOn(v){
const P=v.fmP||this.fm,note=v.note+(P.trans|0),vel127=Math.max(1,Math.min(127,Math.round(v.vel*127)));
v.fdone=0;v.ffb0=v.ffb1=0;v.frel=0;v.frelN=0;v.fgate=0;v.falive=true;
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){v.fph[k]=0;v.flvl[k]=0;v.fg0[k]=0;v.fg1[k]=0;v.fix[k]=4;v.xny[k]=-1;}
this.fmVoicePatch(v,false,note,vel127);
for(let k=0;k<6;k++)this.fmAdvance(v,k,0);
const G=(v.fmP||this.fm).peg;v.pegL=G?PEG_TAB[G.l[3]]/32:0;this.pegAdvance(v,G?0:4);
if(this.gx)this.fmxNoteOn(v);
}
fmxNoteOn(v){
const X=this.gx,F=this.curF,G=v.gxOn?v.gxv:X;
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
const m=G.phm[k];
if(m===1){let x=v.rng;x^=x<<13;x^=x>>>17;x^=x<<5;v.rng=x>>>0;v.fph[k]=v.rng;}
else if(m===2){if(this.frF[k]>=0)v.fph[k]=(this.frPh[k]+this.frInc[k]*(F-this.frF[k]))%4294967296;else v.fph[k]=0;}
else v.fph[k]=G.ph0[k];
v.xmu[k]=X.on[k];v.olN[k]=0;v.frN[k]=0;v.xf0[k]=v.xf1[k]=0;v.fyl[k]=v.fyp[k]=0;v.xy[k]=v.xyp[k]=0;v.xwa[k]=-1;
const E=X.pit[k];v.xpL[k]=E?PEG_TAB[E.l[3]]/32:0;this.xpAdvance(v,k,E?0:4);
}
if(X.anyPt)for(let s=0;s<12;s++)this.ptStart(v,s,X.pe[s]);else v.pM.fill(0);
v.xnew=1;
}
xpAdvance(v,k,ix){
v.xpIx[k]=ix;const E=this.gx&&this.gx.pit[k];if(!E||ix>3)return;
v.xpT[k]=PEG_TAB[E.l[ix]]/32;v.xpRise[k]=v.xpT[k]>v.xpL[k]?1:0;v.xpR[k]=PEG_RATE[E.r[ix]]/21.3*CR/SR;
}
xpStep(v,k){
const ix=v.xpIx[k];if(!(ix<3||(ix<4&&!v.gate)))return;
if(v.xpRise[k]){v.xpL[k]+=v.xpR[k];if(v.xpL[k]>=v.xpT[k]){v.xpL[k]=v.xpT[k];this.xpAdvance(v,k,ix+1);}}
else{v.xpL[k]-=v.xpR[k];if(v.xpL[k]<=v.xpT[k]){v.xpL[k]=v.xpT[k];this.xpAdvance(v,k,ix+1);}}
}
ptOfs(v,s){return s<6?v.foutLvl[s]-4256:0;}
ptTgt(E,j,ofs){const y=E.v[j];return E.pit?y:Math.max(16,((scaleOL(y|0)>>1)<<6)+ofs);}
ptTf(E,s,j,v){
if(E.pit)return 1;
const P=v&&v.D?v.VP:this.P,fo=(v&&v.fmP||this.fm).ops[s];
return ptDxF(E,j,this.alg.car[s],v?v.frs[s]:0,(scaleOL(fo?fo.ol|0:99)<<5)-4256,+P.fmAtk||0,+P.fmDec||0,+P.fmRel||0,+P.fmBite||0,v?v.foutLvl[s]-4256:null);
}
ptEnter(st,s,E,j,cur,ofs,tf){
st.pS[s]=j;st.pH[s]=0;st.pX[s]=0;
const b=this.ptTgt(E,j,ofs);let a=cur;
if(!E.pit&&!E.lin&&b>1716&&a<1716)a=1716;
st.pA[s]=a;st.pB[s]=b;st.pG[s]=!E.pit&&!E.lin&&b>a?riseG(a,b):0;
st.pN[s]=Math.max(1e-6,E.tm[j]*tf*SR/(1000*CR));
}
ptReach(st,s,E,j,gate,ofs,v){
if(E.la>=0&&j===E.lb&&(E.free||!E.pre||gate)){
if(E.adj){const tf=this.ptTf(E,s%6,E.lb,v);this.ptEnter(st,s,E,E.la,st.pV[s],ofs,tf);st.pN[s]=Math.max(1e-6,E.tm[E.lb]*tf*SR/(1000*CR));return;}
const n=E.la+1;this.ptEnter(st,s,E,n,st.pV[s],ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,n,v));return;}
if(j===E.sus&&gate&&!E.span){st.pH[s]=1;return;}
if(j>=E.k){st.pH[s]=2;return;}
this.ptEnter(st,s,E,j+1,st.pV[s],ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,j+1,v));
}
ptRun(st,s,E,dt,gate,ofs,v,guard){
let g=guard||64,wrap=0;
while(dt>1e-9&&g-- >0){
if(st.pH[s])return;
const rem=st.pN[s]-st.pX[s];
if(dt<rem){st.pX[s]+=dt;st.pV[s]=ptVal(E,st.pA[s],st.pB[s],st.pX[s]/st.pN[s],st.pG[s]);return;}
dt-=rem;st.pX[s]=st.pN[s];st.pV[s]=st.pB[s];
const j0=st.pS[s];
this.ptReach(st,s,E,j0,gate,ofs,v);
if(E.la>=0&&j0===E.lb&&st.pS[s]===(E.adj?E.la:E.la+1)&&++wrap===2&&dt>0){
let P=0;for(let j=E.la+1;j<=E.lb;j++)P+=Math.max(1e-6,E.tm[j]*this.ptTf(E,s%6,j,v)*SR/(1000*CR));
if(E.adj)P*=2;
if(P>0&&dt>P)dt-=Math.floor(dt/P)*P;
}
}
}
ptRelease(v,s,E){
if(E.sus<0)return;
const held=v.pH[s]===1;
if(held||(E.skip&&v.pH[s]===0&&v.pS[s]<=E.sus)){
if(E.sus<E.k){const ofs=this.ptOfs(v,s),j=E.sus+1;this.ptEnter(v,s,E,j,v.pV[s],ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,j,v));
if(!held)v.pN[s]=Math.max(1e-6,v.pN[s]*ptRelScale(E,this.ptTgt(E,E.sus,ofs),v.pV[s],v.pB[s]));}
else v.pH[s]=2;
}
}
ptStart(v,s,E){
if(!E){v.pM[s]=0;return;}
if(E.free){v.pM[s]=2;const F=this.fr;if(F.pM[s]!==2||F.pH[s]===2)this.ptFreeStart(s,E);return;}
const ofs=this.ptOfs(v,s);v.pM[s]=1;v.pV[s]=this.ptTgt(E,0,ofs);this.ptEnter(v,s,E,1,v.pV[s],ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,1,v));
}
ptFreeStart(s,E){const F=this.fr,ofs=s<6?-192:0;F.pM[s]=2;F.pV[s]=this.ptTgt(E,0,ofs);this.ptEnter(F,s,E,1,F.pV[s],ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,1,null));F.pT[s]=this.curF-CR;}
ptFreeStep(){
const F=this.fr,X=this.gx;
for(let s=0;s<12;s++){const E=X.pe[s];if(!E||!E.free||F.pM[s]!==2)continue;
const dt=(this.curF-F.pT[s])/CR;F.pT[s]=this.curF;if(dt>0)this.ptRun(F,s,E,dt,true,s<6?-192:0,null,4096);}
}
ptRebase(st,s,E,ofs,v){
const cur=st.pV[s],GL=Math.max(1,.02*SR/CR);
if(st.pH[s]===2||(st.pH[s]===1&&E.sus<0)){if(st.pH[s]===1&&st.pS[s]<E.k){this.ptEnter(st,s,E,Math.min(E.k,st.pS[s]+1),cur,ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,st.pS[s]+1,v));return;}
if(Math.abs(this.ptTgt(E,E.k,ofs)-cur)>1e-9){this.ptEnter(st,s,E,E.k,cur,ofs,1);st.pN[s]=GL;}else{st.pS[s]=E.k;st.pH[s]=2;}return;}
if(st.pH[s]===1){const j=E.sus;if(Math.abs(this.ptTgt(E,j,ofs)-cur)>1e-9){this.ptEnter(st,s,E,j,cur,ofs,1);st.pN[s]=GL;}else st.pS[s]=j;return;}
const j=Math.max(1,Math.min(E.k,st.pS[s])),x=st.pN[s]>0?Math.min(1,st.pX[s]/st.pN[s]):1;
this.ptEnter(st,s,E,j,cur,ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,j,v));st.pN[s]=Math.max(1e-6,st.pN[s]*(1-x));
}
ptJoin(v,s,E,ix,cur){
const ofs=this.ptOfs(v,s),GL=Math.max(1,.02*SR/CR);v.pM[s]=1;v.pV[s]=cur;
if(ix>=4){this.ptEnter(v,s,E,E.k,cur,ofs,1);v.pN[s]=GL;return;}
if(ix===3&&v.gate&&E.sus>=0){this.ptEnter(v,s,E,E.sus,cur,ofs,1);v.pN[s]=GL;return;}
const j=Math.min(E.k,ix+1);this.ptEnter(v,s,E,j,cur,ofs,this.ptTf(E,s%6,j,v));
}
ptLive(v,k){
const X=this.gx,F=this.fr;
for(const s of[k,6+k]){
const E=X.pe[s],m=v.pM[s],amp=s<6;
if(!E){
if(!m)continue;
const ix=v.pH[s]===2?4:v.pH[s]===1?3:Math.max(0,Math.min(3,v.pS[s]-1));v.pM[s]=0;
if(m===2){if(amp)v.flvl[k]=Math.max(0,F.pV[s]+v.foutLvl[k]-4064);else v.xpL[k]=F.pV[s]/12;}
if(amp)v.fix[k]=ix;else{v.xpIx[k]=ix;if(X.pit[k])this.xpAdvance(v,k,ix===3&&v.gate?2:ix);else{v.xpL[k]=0;v.xpIx[k]=4;}}
continue;
}
const ofs=this.ptOfs(v,s);
if(E.free){
if(m===2)continue;
if(F.pM[s]!==2||F.pH[s]===2){
for(const f of['pS','pH','pN','pX','pG'])F[f][s]=v[f][s];
const d=amp?-192-ofs:0;F.pV[s]=(m?v.pV[s]:amp?v.flvl[k]:v.xpL[k]*12)+d;F.pA[s]=v.pA[s]+d;F.pB[s]=v.pB[s]+d;F.pM[s]=2;F.pT[s]=this.curF;
if(!m)this.ptRebase(F,s,E,amp?-192:0,null);
}
v.pM[s]=2;continue;
}
if(m===2){
const d=amp?ofs+192:0;
for(const f of['pS','pH','pN','pX','pG'])v[f][s]=F[f][s];
v.pV[s]=F.pV[s]+d;v.pA[s]=F.pA[s]+d;v.pB[s]=F.pB[s]+d;v.pM[s]=1;this.ptRebase(v,s,E,ofs,v);continue;
}
if(m===1){this.ptRebase(v,s,E,ofs,v);continue;}
this.ptJoin(v,s,E,amp?v.fix[k]:v.xpIx[k],amp?v.flvl[k]:v.xpL[k]*12);
}
}
// V0d (A.4b 3): pitch EG like msfa (start at L4, L1 -> L2 -> L3, hold L3; key off -> L4), linear in octaves
pegAdvance(v,ix){
v.pegIx=ix;const G=(v.fmP||this.fm).peg;if(!G||ix>3)return;
v.pegT=PEG_TAB[G.l[ix]]/32;v.pegRise=v.pegT>v.pegL?1:0;v.pegR=PEG_RATE[G.r[ix]]/21.3*CR/SR;
}
pegStep(v){
const ix=v.pegIx;if(!(ix<3||(ix<4&&!v.gate)))return;
if(v.pegRise){v.pegL+=v.pegR;if(v.pegL>=v.pegT){v.pegL=v.pegT;this.pegAdvance(v,ix+1);}}
else{v.pegL-=v.pegR;if(v.pegL<=v.pegT){v.pegL=v.pegT;this.pegAdvance(v,ix+1);}}
}
fmRetrig(v){
const P=v.fmP||this.fm,note=v.note+(P.trans|0),vel127=Math.max(1,Math.min(127,Math.round(v.vel*127)));
v.frel=0;v.frelN=0;v.fdone=0;
this.fmVoicePatch(v,false,note,vel127);
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){v.fix[k]=4;this.fmAdvance(v,k,0);}
if(P.peg)this.pegAdvance(v,0);else{v.pegL=0;v.pegIx=4;}
if(this.gx)for(let k=0;k<6;k++){if(this.gx.pit[k])this.xpAdvance(v,k,0);else if(!this.gx.pe[6+k]){v.xpL[k]=0;v.xpIx[k]=4;}}
if(this.gx&&this.gx.anyPt)for(let s=0;s<12;s++){const E=this.gx.pe[s];
if(E&&!E.free&&v.pM[s]===1)this.ptEnter(v,s,E,1,v.pV[s],this.ptOfs(v,s),this.ptTf(E,s,1,v));}
}
frNow(v,k){const t=v.fratio[k];return t*Math.pow(v.frA[k]/t,v.frN[k]/(FRX_N+1));}
fmVoicePatch(v,live,note,vel127){
if(note==null)note=v.fnote;if(vel127==null)vel127=v.fvel;
v.fnote=note;v.fvel=vel127;
const P=v.fmP||this.fm,lf=Math.log2(440*Math.pow(2,(note-69)/12)),X=this.gx;
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
const op=P.ops[k];
v.fon[k]=X?1:op.on!==0&&op.ol>0?1:0;
let ol=scaleOL(op.ol|0)+scaleLevel(note,op.ks);
const vs=op.vs|0;
ol=Math.max(0,(Math.min(127,ol)<<5)+(vs<0?scaleVel(128-vel127,-vs):scaleVel(vel127,vs)));
if(live&&X&&v.foutLvl[k]!==ol&&v.fg1[k]>0){v.olG[k]=v.fg1[k];v.olN[k]=OLX_N;}
v.foutLvl[k]=ol;v.frs[k]=scaleRate(note,op.rs|0);
if(op.fixed){v.ffixed[k]=Math.pow(10,(op.coarse&3)+op.fine/100)*Math.pow(2,(op.det>0?op.det:0)*13457/16777216);v.fratio[k]=0;}
else{
const det=(op.det||0)*0.0209*Math.exp(-0.396*lf)/7*lf,fr0=v.fratio[k];
v.fratio[k]=(op.coarse?op.coarse:0.5)*(1+(op.fine||0)/100)*Math.pow(2,det);v.ffixed[k]=0;
if(live&&X&&fr0>0&&fr0!==v.fratio[k]&&v.fg1[k]>0){v.frA[k]=v.frN[k]>0?this.frNow(v,k):fr0;v.frN[k]=FRX_N;}
}
if(live&&X)this.ptLive(v,k);
if(live&&v.fix[k]<4&&!(X&&X.pe[k]))this.fmAdvance(v,k,v.fix[k]===3&&v.gate?2:v.fix[k]);
}
}
fmAdvance(v,k,ix){
const same=v.fix[k]===ix;
v.fix[k]=ix;
if(!same){v.fholdE[k]=0;v.fholdOn[k]=0;}
v.fhold[k]=0;
if(ix>3)return;
const op=(v.fmP||this.fm).ops[k],P=v.D?v.VP:this.P,car=this.alg.car[k];
const off=ix===0?P.fmAtk:ix===3?-P.fmRel:car?-P.fmDec:0,r=Math.max(0,Math.min(99,op.r[ix]+Math.round(off||0)));
const t=Math.max(16,((scaleOL(op.l[ix])>>1)<<6)+v.foutLvl[k]-4256);
const bite=!car&&(ix===1||ix===2)?+P.fmBite||0:0,bf=bite?Math.pow(2,bite/10):1;
const att0=ix===0&&op.l[0]===0;
if(t===v.flvl[k]||att0){
const h=egHoldSec(r+v.frs[k],att0)*SR/bf;
v.fhold[k]=same&&v.fholdOn[k]?Math.max(0,h-v.fholdE[k]):h;v.fholdOn[k]=1;
if(v.fhold[k]<=0)v.fhold[k]=1e-9;
}
v.ftgt[k]=t;v.frise[k]=t>v.flvl[k]?1:0;
let rate=LVL_PER_SEC(Math.min(63,((r*41)>>6)+v.frs[k]))*FENV_BLK/SR;
if(bf!==1){const q=rate*bf;
if(bf>1&&!v.frise[k]&&v.flvl[k]-t>=510)rate=Math.min(q,Math.max(rate,LVL_PER_SEC(Math.min(63,BITE_LIMQ+v.frs[k]))*FENV_BLK/SR));
else rate=q;}
v.frate[k]=rate;
}
nyGuard(v,k,f){
const t=f<SR*0.45?1:0;let n=v.xny[k];
if(n<0)n=t;else if(n!==t)n=t>n?Math.min(1,n+NY_STEP):Math.max(0,n-NY_STEP);
v.xny[k]=n;return n;
}
fmEnvStep(v,k){
const ix=v.fix[k];
if(!(ix<3||(ix<4&&!v.gate)))return;
if(v.fhold[k]>0){v.fhold[k]-=FENV_BLK;v.fholdE[k]+=FENV_BLK;if(v.fhold[k]<=0){v.fhold[k]=0;this.fmAdvance(v,k,ix+1);}return;}
let l=v.flvl[k];
if(v.frise[k]){
if(l<1716)l=1716;
l+=Math.floor(17-l/256)*v.frate[k];
if(l>=v.ftgt[k]){v.flvl[k]=v.ftgt[k];this.fmAdvance(v,k,ix+1);return;}
}else{
l-=v.frate[k];
if(l<=v.ftgt[k]){v.flvl[k]=v.ftgt[k];this.fmAdvance(v,k,ix+1);return;}
}
v.flvl[k]=l;
}
fmControl(v,dst){
if(this.gx){this.fmxControl(v,dst);return;}
const P=v.D?v.VP:this.P,fm=v.fmP||this.fm,alg=this.alg;
if(!v.gate&&!v.frel){v.frel=1;for(let k=0;k<6;k++){if(v.fix[k]!==3)this.fmAdvance(v,k,3);else{v.fix[k]=2;this.fmAdvance(v,k,3);}}if(fm.peg)this.pegAdvance(v,3);}
if(fm.peg)this.pegStep(v);
const f0=lut(FREQ,v.pitch+v.dP+(fm.trans|0)+dst[D_PITCH]*24+v.pegL*12);
const br=P.fmBright+dst[D_FMBRIGHT]*24;
let alive=false,car=0;
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
this.fmEnvStep(v,k);
v.fg0[k]=v.fg1[k];
const db=(alg.car[k]?0:br)+dst[D_OP1+k]*99*.75;
let g=v.fon[k]?Math.pow(2,v.flvl[k]/256-14)*(db?Math.pow(10,db/20):1):0;
const f=v.ffixed[k]?v.ffixed[k]:f0*v.fratio[k];
const ny=this.nyGuard(v,k,f);if(ny<1)g*=ny;
v.fg1[k]=g;
v.finc[k]=Math.round(Math.min(f,SR*0.45)/SR*4294967296);
if(alg.car[k]){if(g>GAIN_THRESH||v.fg0[k]>GAIN_THRESH)alive=true;if(g>car)car=g;}
}
v.falive=alive;
if(!(P.fmAmp>=1))v.aOut=Math.min(1,car*.5);
}
fmRender(v){
const alg=this.alg,out=this.bl,n=CR,outScale=0.5/Math.sqrt(alg.nCar);
out.fill(0);
for(let k=5;k>=0;k--){
const b=v.fbuf[k],ga=v.fg0[k],gb=v.fg1[k];
if(ga<GAIN_THRESH&&gb<GAIN_THRESH){b.fill(0);if(k===alg.fbDst)v.ffb0=v.ffb1=0;continue;}
const dg=(gb-ga)/n,inc=v.finc[k],mods=alg.mods[k];
let ph=v.fph[k],g=ga,mi=null;
if(mods.length===1)mi=v.fbuf[mods[0]];
else if(mods.length>1){mi=v.fmodIn;const a=v.fbuf[mods[0]];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)mi[i]=a[i];
for(let j=1;j<mods.length;j++){const c=v.fbuf[mods[j]];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)mi[i]+=c[i];}}
if(k===alg.fbDst&&this.fbShift>0){
const fs=this.fbShift*0.5*4294967296;let f0=v.ffb0,f1=v.ffb1;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const pp=(ph+((f0+f1)*fs|0)+(mi?(mi[i]*4294967296|0):0))>>>0;
const x=pp>>>20,y=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((pp&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;
b[i]=y;f0=f1;f1=y;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
v.ffb0=f0;v.ffb1=f1;
}else if(mi){
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const pp=(ph+(mi[i]*4294967296|0))>>>0;
const x=pp>>>20;b[i]=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((pp&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
}else{
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const x=ph>>>20;
b[i]=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((ph&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
}
v.fph[k]=ph;
if(alg.car[k])for(let i=0;i<n;i++)out[i]+=b[i]*outScale;
}
}
fmxControl(v,dst){
const P=v.D?v.VP:this.P,X=this.gx,alg=this.alg,G=v.gxOn?v.gxv:X;
const PE=X.pe;
if(!v.gate&&!v.frel){v.frel=1;for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
if(PE[k]){if(!PE[k].free)this.ptRelease(v,k,PE[k]);}else if(v.fix[k]!==3)this.fmAdvance(v,k,3);else{v.fix[k]=2;this.fmAdvance(v,k,3);}
if(PE[6+k]){if(!PE[6+k].free)this.ptRelease(v,6+k,PE[6+k]);}else if(X.pit[k])this.xpAdvance(v,k,3);}}
if(X.anyFree&&this.frStamp!==this.curF){this.frStamp=this.curF;this.ptFreeStep();}
const base=v.pitch+v.dP+((v.fmP||this.fm).trans|0)+dst[D_PITCH]*24;
const br=P.fmBright+dst[D_FMBRIGHT]*24,l1=this.src[S_LFO1];
let alive=false,car=0,sL=NaN,fL=0;
for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
const Ea=PE[k],Ep=PE[6+k];
if(Ep){if(Ep.free)v.xpL[k]=this.fr.pV[6+k]/12;else{this.ptRun(v,6+k,Ep,1,v.gate,0,v);v.xpL[k]=v.pV[6+k]/12;}}
else if(X.pit[k])this.xpStep(v,k);
if(Ea){if(Ea.free)v.flvl[k]=Math.max(0,this.fr.pV[k]+v.foutLvl[k]-4064);else{this.ptRun(v,k,Ea,1,v.gate,v.foutLvl[k]-4256,v);v.flvl[k]=v.pV[k];}}
else this.fmEnvStep(v,k);
v.fg0[k]=v.fg1[k];
const mt=X.on[k];let mu=v.xmu[k];
if(mu!==mt){mu=mt>mu?Math.min(mt,mu+MUTE_STEP):Math.max(mt,mu-MUTE_STEP);v.xmu[k]=mu;}
let db=(alg.car[k]?0:br)+dst[D_OP1+k]*99*.75;
if(G.am[k])db-=G.am[k]*(1-l1)*.5;
let g=v.fon[k]&&mu>0?Math.pow(2,v.flvl[k]/256-14)*(db?Math.pow(10,db/20):1)*mu:0;
if(G.amG[k])g*=Math.max(0,1-G.amG[k]*l1);
if(v.olN[k]>0){g+=(v.olG[k]-g)*(v.olN[k]/(OLX_N+1));v.olN[k]--;}
const fmv=G.fmS[k]?G.fmS[k]*l1:0;
let f;
if(v.ffixed[k]){const pv=Ep?v.xpL[k]*12+fmv:fmv;f=pv?v.ffixed[k]*Math.pow(2,pv/12):v.ffixed[k];}
else{const sm=base+v.xpL[k]*12+fmv;if(sm!==sL){sL=sm;fL=lut(FREQ,sm);}let rt=v.fratio[k];if(v.frN[k]>0){rt=this.frNow(v,k);v.frN[k]--;}f=fL*rt;}
const ny=this.nyGuard(v,k,f);if(ny<1)g*=ny;
v.fg1[k]=g;
v.finc[k]=Math.round(Math.min(f,SR*0.45)/SR*4294967296);
if(X.wave[k]){
const q=Math.log2(f)+WV_Q;let a=0,w=0;
if(q>0){a=Math.ceil(q);w=q-a+1;if(a>8){a=8;w=1;}}
v.xwb[k]=a;v.xwt[k]=w;
}
if(alg.car[k]){if(g>GAIN_THRESH||v.fg0[k]>GAIN_THRESH)alive=true;if(g>car)car=g;}
}
if(v.xnew){v.xnew=0;for(let k=0;k<6;k++)if(G.phm[k]===2){this.frPh[k]=v.fph[k];this.frInc[k]=v.finc[k];this.frF[k]=this.curF;}}
v.falive=alive;
if(!(P.fmAmp>=1))v.aOut=Math.min(1,car*.5);
}
fmxRender(v){
const X=this.gx,out=this.bl,n=CR,outScale=X.outScale;
out.fill(0);
if(X.ps){this.fmxRenderPS(v);return;}
for(let j=0;j<6;j++){
const k=X.ord[j],b=v.fbuf[k],ga=v.fg0[k],gb=v.fg1[k];
v.fyp[k]=v.fyl[k];
if(ga<GAIN_THRESH&&gb<GAIN_THRESH){b.fill(0);v.xf0[k]=v.xf1[k]=0;v.fyl[k]=0;continue;}
const dg=(gb-ga)/n,inc=v.finc[k],mods=X.srcs[k];
let ph=v.fph[k],g=ga,mi=null;
if(mods.length===1)mi=v.fbuf[mods[0]];
else if(mods.length>1){mi=v.fmodIn;const a=v.fbuf[mods[0]];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)mi[i]=a[i];
for(let m=1;m<mods.length;m++){const c=v.fbuf[mods[m]];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)mi[i]+=c[i];}}
const fk=X.fbK[k],fsh=(v.gxOn?v.gxv:X).fbS[k],w=X.wave[k];
if(w===0){
if(fk===k&&fsh>0){
const fs=fsh*0.5*4294967296;let f0=v.xf0[k],f1=v.xf1[k];
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const pp=(ph+((f0+f1)*fs|0)+(mi?(mi[i]*4294967296|0):0))>>>0;
const x=pp>>>20,y=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((pp&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;
b[i]=y;f0=f1;f1=y;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
v.xf0[k]=f0;v.xf1[k]=f1;
}else if(fk>=0&&fsh>0){
const fs=fsh*4294967296,sb=v.fbuf[fk];let yp=v.fyp[fk];
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const pp=(ph+(yp*fs|0)+(mi?(mi[i]*4294967296|0):0))>>>0;
const x=pp>>>20;b[i]=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((pp&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;yp=sb[i];ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
}else if(mi){
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const pp=(ph+(mi[i]*4294967296|0))>>>0;
const x=pp>>>20;b[i]=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((pp&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
}else{
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g+=dg;const x=ph>>>20;
b[i]=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((ph&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;}
}
}else{
const T=waveTab(w);let w0,w1;
if(v.xwa[k]!==v.xwb[k]){v.xwa[k]=v.xwb[k];w0=w1=v.xwt[k];}else{w0=v.xww[k];w1=v.xwt[k];}
v.xww[k]=w1;
const TA=T[v.xwa[k]],TB=T[v.xwa[k]+1],dw=(w1-w0)/n;let wv=w0;
const self=fk===k&&fsh>0,fsS=fsh*0.5*4294967296,fsF=fsh*4294967296,sb=fk>=0&&!self&&fsh>0?v.fbuf[fk]:null;
let f0=v.xf0[k],f1=v.xf1[k],yp=sb?v.fyp[fk]:0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
g+=dg;wv+=dw;
let pp=ph+(mi?(mi[i]*4294967296|0):0);
if(self)pp+=(f0+f1)*fsS|0;else if(sb){pp+=yp*fsF|0;yp=sb[i];}
pp>>>=0;
const x=pp>>>23,fr=(pp&8388607)*WV_FR,ya=TA[x]+(TA[x+1]-TA[x])*fr,y=(ya+(TB[x]+(TB[x+1]-TB[x])*fr-ya)*wv)*g;
b[i]=y;f0=f1;f1=y;ph=(ph+inc)>>>0;
}
if(self){v.xf0[k]=f0;v.xf1[k]=f1;}
}
v.fph[k]=ph;v.fyl[k]=b[n-1];
if(X.car[k])for(let i=0;i<n;i++)out[i]+=b[i]*outScale;
}
}
fmxRenderPS(v){
const X=this.gx,out=this.bl,n=CR,os=X.outScale,S=this.ps;
const y=v.xy,yp=v.xyp,G=S.g,DG=S.dg,PH=S.ph,IN=S.inc,W=S.w,DW=S.dw,TA=S.ta,TB=S.tb,FS=S.fs;
const ord=S.ord,FK=S.fk,MS=S.ms,MN=S.mn,CA=S.ca,TW=S.tw;let na=0;
for(let j=0;j<6;j++){
const k=X.ord[j],ga=v.fg0[k],gb=v.fg1[k];
if(ga<GAIN_THRESH&&gb<GAIN_THRESH){v.xf0[k]=v.xf1[k]=0;y[k]=0;continue;}
ord[na++]=k;G[k]=ga;DG[k]=(gb-ga)/n;PH[k]=v.fph[k];IN[k]=v.finc[k];
const fk=X.fbK[k],fbs=(v.gxOn?v.gxv:X).fbS[k];FK[k]=fbs>0?fk:-1;FS[k]=fbs>0?fbs*(fk===k?0.5:1)*4294967296:0;
const sr=X.srcs[k];MN[k]=sr.length;for(let q=0;q<sr.length;q++)MS[k*6+q]=sr[q];
CA[k]=X.car[k];const w=X.wave[k];TW[k]=w?1:0;
if(w){const T=waveTab(w);let w0,w1;
if(v.xwa[k]!==v.xwb[k]){v.xwa[k]=v.xwb[k];w0=w1=v.xwt[k];}else{w0=v.xww[k];w1=v.xwt[k];}
v.xww[k]=w1;TA[k]=T[v.xwa[k]];TB[k]=T[v.xwa[k]+1];W[k]=w0;DW[k]=(w1-w0)/n;}
}
const F0=v.xf0,F1=v.xf1;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
let o=0;
for(let j=0;j<na;j++){
const k=ord[j],g=(G[k]+=DG[k]),mn=MN[k];let m=0;
for(let q=0,b0=k*6;q<mn;q++)m+=y[MS[b0+q]];
let pp=PH[k]+(m*4294967296|0);
const fk=FK[k];
if(fk>=0)pp+=(fk===k?(F0[k]+F1[k])*FS[k]:yp[fk]*FS[k])|0;
pp>>>=0;
let s;
if(TW[k]){const x=pp>>>23,fr=(pp&8388607)*WV_FR,A=TA[k],B=TB[k],ya=A[x]+(A[x+1]-A[x])*fr,wv=(W[k]+=DW[k]);
s=(ya+(B[x]+(B[x+1]-B[x])*fr-ya)*wv)*g;}
else{const x=pp>>>20;s=(SIN[x]+(SIN[x+1]-SIN[x])*((pp&1048575)*9.5367431640625e-7))*g;}
y[k]=s;F0[k]=F1[k];F1[k]=s;
PH[k]=(PH[k]+IN[k])>>>0;
if(CA[k])o+=s;
}
yp[0]=y[0];yp[1]=y[1];yp[2]=y[2];yp[3]=y[3];yp[4]=y[4];yp[5]=y[5];
out[i]=o*os;
}
for(let j=0;j<na;j++){const k=ord[j];v.fph[k]=PH[k];}
}
fmRenderVoice(v,L,R,off){
const bl=this.bl,P=this.P,byp=this.fmBypass;
if(this.gx)this.fmxRender(v);else this.fmRender(v);
if(!byp){if(P.lq>=1)this.ladder(v,bl,false);else this.ladderEco(v,bl,false);}
const gain=v.vgain*(byp?1:v.outG);
const xg=(this.sxfOn?1:this.xg1)*v.pol;
const pl1=gain*v.panL*xg,pr1=gain*v.panR*xg;
let pl=v.pl0<0?pl1:v.pl0,pr=v.pr0<0?pr1:v.pr0;
const dpl=(pl1-pl)/CR,dpr=(pr1-pr)/CR;
v.pl0=pl1;v.pr0=pr1;
let fg=v.fadeG;const fs=v.fade>0?v.fadeStep:0;
if(P.fmAmp>=1){
const A=(v.D||this.Dc).A;let s=v.aSt,e=v.aOut;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
pl+=dpl;pr+=dpr;
if(s===1){e=A.aT+(e-A.aT)*A.aC;if(e>=1){e=1;s=2;}}
else if(s===2){e=A.dT+(e-A.dT)*A.dC;if(e<=A.s){e=A.s;s=3;}}
else if(s===3)e+=(A.s-e)*.002;
else if(s===4){e=A.rT+(e-A.rT)*A.rC;if(e<=0){e=0;s=0;}}
if(fs){fg-=fs;if(fg<0)fg=0;}
const x=bl[n]*e*fg;
L[off+n]+=x*pl;R[off+n]+=x*pr;
}
v.aSt=s;v.aOut=e;v.fadeG=fg;
if(s===0||(s===3&&A.s<=0&&e<1e-4)){this.endVoice(v);return;}
}else{
let e=v.fgate;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
pl+=dpl;pr+=dpr;
if(e<1){e=1-(1-e)*FM_GATE_C;if(e>.99)e=1;}
if(fs){fg-=fs;if(fg<0)fg=0;}
const x=bl[n]*e*fg;
L[off+n]+=x*pl;R[off+n]+=x*pr;
}
v.fgate=e;v.fadeG=fg;
}
if(v.fade>0){v.fade-=CR;if(v.fade<=0||fg<=0){this.endVoice(v);return;}}
if(!v.gate){
v.frelN+=CR;
if(v.falive)v.fdone=0;else v.fdone+=CR;
if(v.fdone>256)this.endVoice(v);
else if(v.frelN>FM_MAXREL-FM_CAPF&&!(v.fade>0))this.fadeOut(v,FM_CAPF);
}
}
ladder(v,buf,right){
if((v.D||this.Dc).mix===KF2_VMIX)return this.kf2Vow(v,buf,right);
const mx=(v.D||this.Dc).mix,c0=mx[0],c1=mx[1],c2=mx[2],c3=mx[3],c4=mx[4];
let s1,s2,s3,s4;
if(right){s1=v.r1;s2=v.r2;s3=v.r3;s4=v.r4;}else{s1=v.s1;s2=v.s2;s3=v.s3;s4=v.s4;}
let g=v.gA,k=v.kA;const dg=(v.gB-v.gA)/CR,dk=(v.kB-v.kA)/CR,drv=v.drv,comp=v.comp,idrv=1/Math.sqrt(drv);
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
g+=dg;k+=dk;
const xin=buf[n]*drv*comp+1e-20;
const b1=tg(s1),b2=tg(s2),b3=tg(s3),b4=tg(s4),a1=tg(xin-k*s4);
const d1=1/(1+g*b1),d2=1/(1+g*b2),d3=1/(1+g*b3),d4=1/(1+g*b4);
const G1=g*a1*d1,G2=g*b1*d2,G3=g*b2*d3,G4=g*b3*d4;
const S1=s1*d1,S2=s2*d2,S3=s3*d3,S4=s4*d4;
const G34=G3*G4,G234=G2*G34;
const y4=(G1*G234*xin+G234*S1+G34*S2+G4*S3+S4)/(1+k*G1*G234);
const x0=xin-k*y4,y1=G1*x0+S1,y2=G2*y1+S2,y3=G3*y2+S3;
s1=2*y1-s1;s2=2*y2-s2;s3=2*y3-s3;s4=2*y4-s4;
buf[n]=(c0*a1*x0+c1*y1+c2*y2+c3*y3+c4*y4)*idrv;
}
if(right){v.r1=s1;v.r2=s2;v.r3=s3;v.r4=s4;}else{v.s1=s1;v.s2=s2;v.s3=s3;v.s4=s4;}
}
ladderEco(v,buf,right){
if((v.D||this.Dc).mix===KF2_VMIX)return this.kf2Vow(v,buf,right);
const mx=(v.D||this.Dc).mix,c0=mx[0],c1=mx[1],c2=mx[2],c3=mx[3],c4=mx[4];
let s1,s2,s3,s4;
if(right){s1=v.r1;s2=v.r2;s3=v.r3;s4=v.r4;}else{s1=v.s1;s2=v.s2;s3=v.s3;s4=v.s4;}
let g=v.gA,k=v.kA;const dg=(v.gB-v.gA)/CR,dk=(v.kB-v.kA)/CR,drv=v.drv,comp=v.comp,idrv=1/Math.sqrt(drv);
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
g+=dg;k+=dk;
const xin=buf[n]*drv*comp+1e-20;
const d=1/(1+g),G=g*d,a1=tg(xin-k*s4),G1=G*a1,G2=G*G,G3=G2*G;
const y4=(G1*G3*xin+G3*s1*d+G2*s2*d+G*s3*d+s4*d)/(1+k*G1*G3);
const x0=xin-k*y4,y1=G1*x0+s1*d,y2=G*y1+s2*d,y3=G*y2+s3*d;
s1=2*y1-s1;s2=2*y2-s2;s3=2*y3-s3;s4=2*y4-s4;
buf[n]=(c0*a1*x0+c1*y1+c2*y2+c3*y3+c4*y4)*idrv;
}
if(right){v.r1=s1;v.r2=s2;v.r3=s3;v.r4=s4;}else{v.s1=s1;v.s2=s2;v.s3=s3;v.s4=s4;}
}
v6aMsg(o){
const q=this.v6q;
if(o.stop){if(q)for(const m of q)for(const k of o.stop)this.v6aDrop(m,k);return;}
if(o.off){this.v6q=null;return;}
const k=Array.isArray(o.k)?o.k:[],n=k.length,mk=Array.isArray(o.mk)&&o.mk.length?o.mk:null;
const m={f0:Math.round(+o.t0*SR),f1:Math.round(+o.t1*SR),fa:Math.round((o.at==null?+o.t1:+o.at)*SR),c:o.c|0,
k,v:Float64Array.from(o.v||[]),l:Uint8Array.from(o.l||[]),h:Uint8Array.from(o.h||[]),a:null,x:new Uint8Array(n),
mk,mv:mk?Float64Array.from(o.mkv||[]):null,ma:null,mx:mk?new Uint8Array(mk.length):null,
d:o.d&&typeof o.d==='object'?o.d:null,fm:o.fm||null,md:o.mkd&&typeof o.mkd==='object'?o.mkd:null,dd:false,hot:false,o:{},mo:{}};
if(!(m.f1>=m.f0))m.f1=m.f0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){if(m.h[i])m.hot=true;m.o[k[i]]=m.v[i];}
if(mk)for(let i=0;i<mk.length;i++)m.mo[mk[i]]=m.mv[i];
(q||(this.v6q=[])).push(m);
}
v6aDrop(m,key){
let z=m.k.indexOf(key);if(z>=0&&!m.x[z]){m.x[z]=1;delete m.o[key];}
z=m.mk?m.mk.indexOf(key):-1;if(z>=0&&!m.mx[z]){m.mx[z]=1;delete m.mo[key];}
}
v6aHot(){
const P=this.P;
for(let o=0;o<NOSC;o++){
const on='o'+(o+1);
this.oSemi[o]=P[on+'oct']*12+P[on+'semi']+P[on+'fine']/100;this.oRat[o]=Math.pow(2,this.oSemi[o]/12);
this.oLvl[o]=P[on+'lvl'];this.oPw[o]=P[on+'pw'];
}
this.cutN0=69+12*Math.log2(clamp(P.cutoff,10,22000)/440);
this.drv0=.6*Math.pow(2,clamp(P.drive,0,1)*4);this.outG0=1/(1+clamp(P.drive,0,1)*1.3);
this.fmBypassCalc();
}
v6aMkGet(path){
const q=String(path).split('.');if(q[0]!=='t')return NaN;
const tm=this.mk.tm[+q[1]];let o=tm&&tm.T;for(let i=2;o!=null&&i<q.length;i++)o=o[q[i]];
return typeof o==='number'?o:NaN;
}
v6aStep(f,full){
const q=this.v6q,P=this.P;let hot=false;
for(let j=0;j<q.length;j++){
const m=q[j];
if(f<m.f0)continue;
if(!m.a){
m.a=new Float64Array(m.k.length);
for(let i=0;i<m.k.length;i++){const x=P[m.k[i]];m.a[i]=typeof x==='number'&&isFinite(x)?x:m.v[i];}
if(m.mk){m.ma=new Float64Array(m.mk.length);for(let i=0;i<m.mk.length;i++){const x=this.v6aMkGet(m.mk[i]);m.ma[i]=isFinite(x)?x:m.mv[i];}}
for(let jj=0;jj<j;jj++){const o=q[jj];for(const k of m.k)this.v6aDrop(o,k);if(m.mk)for(const k of m.mk)this.v6aDrop(o,k);}
}
if(full||m.hot){
const n=m.f1>m.f0?(f-m.f0)/(m.f1-m.f0):1,x=n>=1?1:n<=0?0:n,s=m.c?x:x*x*(3-2*x),end=x>=1;
const fl=full||end;
let any=false;
for(let i=0;i<m.k.length;i++){
if(m.x[i]||(!fl&&!m.h[i]))continue;
const a=m.a[i],b=m.v[i];
let v=end?b:m.l[i]&&a>0&&b>0?a*Math.pow(b/a,s):a+(b-a)*s;
if(!(v===v)||!isFinite(v))v=b;
if(v<1e-30&&v>-1e-30)v=0;
if(fl){m.o[m.k[i]]=v;any=true;}else{P[m.k[i]]=v;hot=true;}
}
if(fl&&any)this.setParams(m.o);
if(fl&&m.mk){
let mn=false;
for(let i=0;i<m.mk.length;i++){if(m.mx[i])continue;const a=m.ma[i],b=m.mv[i];m.mo[m.mk[i]]=end?b:a+(b-a)*s;mn=true;}
if(mn)this.mk.set(m.mo);
}
if(!m.dd&&f>=m.fa){
m.dd=true;
if(m.d||m.fm)this.setReq(m.d||{},!!(m.d&&Object.keys(m.d).length),m.fm);
if(m.md)this.mk.set(m.md);
}
if(end&&m.dd){q.splice(j,1);j--;}
}
}
if(hot)this.v6aHot();
if(this.v6pr&&(full||!q.length)){
const k=this.v6pr,v=new Array(k.length);
for(let i=0;i<k.length;i++){const x=k[i];v[i]=x.charCodeAt(0)===116&&x.charCodeAt(1)===46?this.v6aMkGet(x):+P[x];}
this.port.postMessage({v6aPr:{f,v,n:q.length}});
}
if(!q.length)this.v6q=null;
}
dcOut(x,k,N){
const c=this.dcC,s=this.dcS;let x1=s[k],y1=s[k+1];
for(let n=0;n<N;n++){const xi=x[n],y=xi-x1+c*y1;x1=xi;y1=y;x[n]=y;}
if(x1===0&&y1<1e-9&&y1>-1e-9)y1=0;
s[k]=x1;s[k+1]=y1;
}
tempoAt(frame){
const tn=this.tnext;if(!tn.length)return;
const t=frame*ISR;
while(tn.length&&tn[0].at<=t){const e=tn.shift();this.bpm=e.bpm;this.songStart=e.start;}
}
process(inputs,outputs){
const out=outputs[0],L=out[0],R=out[1]||out[0],N=L.length,f0=currentFrame;
const t0=this.hasPerf?performance.now():Date.now();
L.fill(0);if(R!==L)R.fill(0);
if(this.v6q)this.v6aStep(f0,true);
if(this.srOn||this.mo)this.srBlock(f0);
if(this.xfHeld&&this.xfNeedFm&&--this.xfWait<=0){this.xfNeedFm=false;this.xfTry();}
let any=this.q.length>0||this.ghosts.length>0;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){any=true;break;}
if(this.P.eng===3)this.mk.inSet(inputs&&inputs[0]);else if(this.mk.inE1||this.mk.inE2)this.mk.inSet(null);
if(!any&&(this.mk.busy()||this.mk.fxHot()||this.mk.vocHot()))any=true;
if(this.pend&&!any){const o=this.pend;this.pend=null;this.setParams(o);this.fmPendApply();this.xfState=0;this.xg=this.xg1=1;}
if(!any&&!this.xfHeld&&(this.xfState===3||this.pendS||this.sxfOn)){
this.xfState=0;this.xg=this.xg1=1;this.sxfFin=false;if(this.sxfG){this.sxfG.xfState=5;this.sxfG=null;}this.sxfEnd();
}
if(any){
for(let off=0;off<N;off+=CR){
this.curF=f0+off;
if(off&&this.v6q)this.v6aStep(f0+off,false);
this.runEvents(f0+off+CR);
this.xfBlock();
this.control(f0+off);
const sx=this.sxfOn&&this.sxfG&&this.sxfG.xfState===4&&this.ghosts.includes(this.sxfG)?this.sxfG:null,sk=this.sxkN,bk=++this.sxBlk;
if(sx)sx.control(f0+off);
for(const v of this.V)if(v.on){
if(!this.sxfOn||v.sxk!==sk){this.renderVoice(v,L,R,off);continue;}
const c=v.sxo;this.nL.fill(0);this.nR.fill(0);this.oL.fill(0);this.oR.fill(0);
this.renderVoice(v,this.nL,this.nR,0);
if(c){if(sx&&c.on)sx.renderVoice(c,this.oL,this.oR,0);c.sxBlk=bk;this.sxMix(c,v,L,R,off);}
else{const nL=this.nL,nR=this.nR;for(let i=0;i<CR;i++){L[off+i]+=nL[i];R[off+i]+=nR[i];}}
}
if(sx)for(const c of sx.V)if(c.on&&c.sxBlk!==bk){
this.nL.fill(0);this.nR.fill(0);this.oL.fill(0);this.oR.fill(0);sx.renderVoice(c,this.oL,this.oR,0);c.sxBlk=bk;this.sxMix(c,null,L,R,off);
}
for(const g of this.ghosts)if(g.xfState===4){
if(g===this.sxfG&&this.sxfOn)continue;
if(this.xfHeld)g.xg=g.xg1;else g.xfBlock();
g.control(f0+off);
const Z=this.xfZC&&this.xfZC.g===g&&!this.xfZC.ok?this.xfZC:null;
for(const v of g.V)if(v.on){
if(!Z||v!==Z.v){g.renderVoice(v,L,R,off);continue;}
const zl=this.zL,zr=this.zR;zl.fill(0);zr.fill(0);
g.renderVoice(v,zl,zr,0);
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){const x=zl[n]+zr[n];L[off+n]+=zl[n];R[off+n]+=zr[n];if(Z.prev<0&&x>=0)Z.ok=true;Z.prev=x;}
if((Z.left-=CR)<=0||!v.on)Z.ok=true;
}
if(Z&&Z.ok)this.xfTry();
}
if(this.mk.busy()||this.mk.fxHot()||this.mk.vocHot()||this.mk.tap==='lfo')this.mk.block(L,R,off,f0+off);else if(this.P.eng===3)this.mk.idle(CR,f0+off);
}
this.mk.fxEnd(L,R);
for(let i=this.ghosts.length-1;i>=0;i--){const g=this.ghosts[i];if(g.xfState!==4||!g.V.some(v=>v.on)){this.ghosts.splice(i,1);this.dropGhost(g);}}
if(R===L)for(let n=0;n<N;n++)L[n]*=.5;
}else{
this.tempoAt(f0);
const P=this.P,spb=60/this.bpm,r1=P.l1sync>0?1/(P.l1sync*spb):P.l1rate,r2=P.l2sync>0?1/(P.l2sync*spb):P.l2rate;
const og1=this.gl1,og2=this.gl2;
this.gl1=(this.gl1+r1*N*ISR)%1;this.gl2=(this.gl2+r2*N*ISR)%1;
if(P.eng===3)this.mk.idle(N,f0);
if(this.gl1<og1)this.gsh1=Math.random()*2-1;
if(this.gl2<og2)this.gsh2=Math.random()*2-1;
}
const dcs=this.dcS;
if(!this.mk.tap&&(any||dcs[0]!==0||dcs[1]!==0||dcs[2]!==0||dcs[3]!==0)){this.dcOut(L,0,N);if(R!==L)this.dcOut(R,2,N);}
if(this.ended.length){this.port.postMessage({end:this.ended});this.ended=[];}
if(this.scopeOn&&f0>=this.scopeNext){
this.scopeNext=f0+((SR/30)|0);
const sc=this.sc;
if(!this.scHas){sc.fill(0);sc[S_LFO1]=(this.P.l1shape|0)===5?this.lf5(1,this.g51||0,this.P.l1sync):this.lfoVal(this.P.l1shape|0,this.gl1,this.gsh1);sc[S_LFO2]=(this.P.l2shape|0)===5?this.lf5(2,this.g52||0,this.P.l2sync):this.lfoVal(this.P.l2shape|0,this.gl2,this.gsh2);}
let nv=0;for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)nv++;
if(this.scopeDst){if(!nv)this.scD.fill(0);this.port.postMessage({scope:Array.from(sc),t:f0*ISR,nv,dst:Array.from(this.scD),ks:this.scHas&&this.ksV?Array.from(this.ksV):null});}
else this.port.postMessage({scope:Array.from(sc),t:f0*ISR,nv,ks:this.scHas&&this.ksV?Array.from(this.ksV):null});
this.scHas=false;
}
if(this.mk.scopeOn&&f0>=this.mk.scopeNext){this.mk.scopeNext=f0+((SR/30)|0);this.port.postMessage({mkScope:{voices:this.mk.scope(),t:f0*ISR,g1:this.mk.g1,g2:this.mk.g2,seq:this.mk.sqScope(),vocBands:this.mk.mode===3?this.mk.vc.bands():undefined}});}
{const V=this.mk.vc;if(V.lvOn){
if(this.P.eng===3){const A=this.mk.inE1,B=this.mk.inE2;for(let n=0;n<N;n++){const a=A?Math.abs(A[n]):0,b=B?Math.abs(B[n]):0;if(a>V.lvPk[0])V.lvPk[0]=a;if(b>V.lvPk[1])V.lvPk[1]=b;}}
if(f0>=(V.lvNext||0)){V.lvNext=f0+((SR/30)|0);this.port.postMessage({mkLv:{i:V.lvPk.slice(),b:this.mk.mode===3?V.bands():null,h:V.hold?1:0}});V.lvPk[0]=V.lvPk[1]=0;}}}
if(this.lvOn&&f0>=this.lvNext){
this.lvNext=f0+((SR/30)|0);
const lv=[0,0,0,0,0,0];
if(this.P.eng===1)for(const v of this.V)if(v.on&&v.fade===0)for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
const g=v.fg1[k];if(!(g>GAIN_THRESH))continue;
const x=((Math.log2(g)+14)*256+224)/32-28;if(x>lv[k])lv[k]=x;
}
for(let k=0;k<6;k++)lv[k]=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,lv[k]/99));
this.port.postMessage({fmxLv:lv,t:f0*ISR});
}
const dq=(this.hasPerf?performance.now():Date.now())-t0;this.busy+=dq;this.busyN+=N;if(dq>this.qMax)this.qMax=dq;
if(this.busyN>=SR){
let nv=this.mk.count();for(const v of this.V)if(v.on)nv++;
this.port.postMessage({load:this.busy/(this.busyN*ISR*1000),voices:nv,fr:this.busyN,wall:Date.now(),qmax:this.qMax});this.busy=0;this.busyN=0;this.qMax=0;
}
return this.alive;
}
}
const SRCORE=(function(){
'use strict';
const H=0.02,SNAP=1e-6,TAU=0.020;
const fin=v=>typeof v==='number'&&v===v&&v!==Infinity&&v!==-Infinity;
const cl01=v=>(v<0?0:v>1?1:v);
function toN(law,lo,hi,kk,v){
let n;
if(law==='exp')n=Math.log(v/lo)/Math.log(hi/lo);
else if(law==='db')n=v>0?(20*Math.log10(v)+40)/52:0;
else if(law==='hdec')n=v>=59?1:0.96*Math.log(Math.max(v,0.2)/0.2)/Math.log(100);
else if(law==='pow')n=Math.pow(Math.max(0,(v-lo)/(hi-lo)),1/kk);
else if(law==='bipow'){const u=Math.sign(v)*Math.pow(Math.min(1,Math.abs(v)/hi),1/kk);n=(u+1)/2;}
else n=(v-lo)/(hi-lo);
return fin(n)?cl01(n):0;
}
function fromN(law,lo,hi,kk,n){
n=cl01(n);
if(law==='exp')return lo*Math.pow(hi/lo,n);
if(law==='db')return n<=0?0:Math.min(hi,Math.pow(10,(52*n-40)/20));
if(law==='hdec')return n>=0.985?60:Math.min(20,0.2*Math.pow(100,n/0.96));
if(law==='pow')return lo+(hi-lo)*Math.pow(n,kk);
if(law==='bipow'){const u=2*n-1;return hi*Math.sign(u)*Math.pow(Math.abs(u),kk);}
return lo+(hi-lo)*n;
}
function path(n,d,x){
let px=0,pd=0;
for(let i=0;i<n.length;i++){
const p=n[i];
if(x<=p)return p>px?pd+(d[i]-pd)*(x-px)/(p-px):d[i];
px=p;pd=d[i];
}
return pd;
}
function stufe(n,x,cur){
const m=n.length,pt=i=>(i===0?0:n[i-1]);
let c=cur,h=H;
if(!(c>=0&&c<=m)){c=0;h=0;}
while(c<m&&x>(pt(c)+pt(c+1))/2+h)c++;
while(c>0&&x<(pt(c-1)+pt(c))/2-h)c--;
return c;
}
const alpha=sr=>1-Math.exp(-128/(TAU*sr));
function smooth(xs,x,a){let y=xs+(x-xs)*a;if(!fin(y))y=x;if(Math.abs(x-y)<SNAP)y=x;if(Math.abs(y)<1e-30)y=0;return y;}
function rampAt(r,t){
if(!r)return null;
if(t>=r.t1||!(r.t1>r.t0))return r.x1;
if(t<=r.t0)return r.x0;
return r.x0+(r.x1-r.x0)*(t-r.t0)/(r.t1-r.t0);
}
function rampRest(e0,dt){const e=e0*Math.exp(-dt/TAU);return Math.abs(e)<SNAP?0:e;}
function endVal(law,lo,hi,kk,base,dl){
if(dl===0)return base;
const v=fromN(law,lo,hi,kk,toN(law,lo,hi,kk,base)+dl);
return fin(v)?(Math.abs(v)<1e-30?0:v):base;
}
const sCurve=u=>(u<=0?0:u>=1?1:u*u*(3-2*u));
return{toN,fromN,path,stufe,alpha,smooth,rampAt,rampRest,endVal,sCurve,H,TAU,fin};
})();
function lfSeg(T,i,u){
const N=T.length>>2,a=i*4;
if(i>=N-1)return T[a+1];
const x0=T[a],x1=T[a+4],y0=T[a+1],y1=T[a+5],L=x1-x0;
if(!(L>0))return y1;
const s=(u-x0)/L,t=T[a+2];
if(t===0)return y0;
if(t===1)return y0+(y1-y0)*s;
const kap=6*T[a+3];
return y0+(y1-y0)*(Math.abs(kap)<1e-4?s:(Math.exp(kap*s)-1)/(Math.exp(kap)-1));
}
function lfFind(T,u){
const N=T.length>>2;let lo=0,hi=N-2;
while(lo<hi){const m=(lo+hi+1)>>1;if(T[m*4]<=u)lo=m;else hi=m-1;}
while(lo<N-2&&T[(lo+1)*4]<=u)lo++;
return lo;
}
function lfT(T,ph,cyc,wrap){
const N=T.length>>2;if(N<2)return 0;
const u=ph-Math.floor(ph),i=lfFind(T,u),v=lfSeg(T,i,u);
if(!(cyc>0))return v;
const x0=T[i*4],here=T[i*4+1];
if(x0===0&&!wrap)return v;
const left=x0===0?lfSeg(T,lfFind(T,1-1e-9),1):lfSeg(T,lfFind(T,x0-1e-9),x0);
if(left===here)return v;
const Ls=((i+1<N?T[(i+1)*4]:1)-x0)*cyc,emin=2*CR/SR;
const ej=Math.min(Ls,Math.max(emin,Math.min(0.001,Ls/4))),du=(u-x0)*cyc;
if(!(ej>0)||du>=ej)return v;
return left+(v-left)*(du/ej);
}

},
