else{t+=dt*CR;t-=Math.floor(t);}
const ln=this.kfS?this.kfS[v.idx*KF_NK+k]:null;if(!ln)return t;
const c=ln.c;
if(ln.st!==KF_F){
ln.st=KF_F;
const VP=v.D?v.VP:this.lbP?this.PR:this.P,hel=+VP.strHel,dmp=+VP.strDmp;
if(dt!==ln.dt||hel!==ln.hel||dmp!==ln.dm){
let Pp=dt>1e-9?1/dt:KF_PMAX;while(Pp>KF_PMAX)Pp*=.5;
const rP=Pp/ln.Pf;
if(hel===ln.hel&&dmp===ln.dm&&rP>.997&&rP<1.003){
ln.dt=dt;let Dl=Pp-ln.pd;if(Dl<2.5)Dl=2.5;
const di=Math.floor(Dl),u=1-(Dl-di),u1=u+1,um=u-1,u2=u-2,K=ln.k;
const h0=-u*um*u2/6,h1=u1*um*u2/2,h2=-u1*u*u2/2,h3=u1*u*um/6;
const lr=h3+h2*K[0]+h1*K[2]+h0*K[4],li=h2*K[1]+h1*K[3]+h0*K[5];
let rho=ln.gq/Math.sqrt(lr*lr+li*li);if(rho>KF_RHO)rho=KF_RHO;
ln.P=Pp;ln.D=Dl;ln.di=di;ln.h0=h0;ln.h1=h1;ln.h2=h2;ln.h3=h3;ln.a0=rho*(1-ln.sS);ln.a1=rho*ln.sS;
}else{
ln.dt=dt;ln.hel=hel;ln.dm=dmp;
const w0=2*Math.PI/Pp,cw=Math.cos(w0),sw=Math.sin(w0),c2=2*cw*cw-1,s2=2*sw*cw,c3=c2*cw-s2*sw,s3=s2*cw+c2*sw;
const gt=Math.pow(10,-3*Pp/(SR*KF_T60(dmp)));
let S2=.5-.48*clamp(hel,0,1),hl=1,re=1,im=0,Dl=2.5,di=2,h0=0,h1=0,h2=0,h3=1;
for(let pass=0;pass<2;pass++){
const q=gt/(KF_RHO*hl);
if(q>=1)S2=0;else{const c4=2*(1-q*q)/(1-cw);if(c4<1){const sm=.5*(1-Math.sqrt(1-c4));if(S2>sm)S2=sm;}}
re=1-S2+S2*cw;im=S2*sw;
Dl=Pp-Math.atan2(im,re)/w0;if(Dl<2.5)Dl=2.5;
di=Math.floor(Dl);const u=1-(Dl-di),u1=u+1,um=u-1,u2=u-2;
h0=-u*um*u2/6;h1=u1*um*u2/2;h2=-u1*u*u2/2;h3=u1*u*um/6;
const lr=h3+h2*cw+h1*c2+h0*c3,li=h2*sw+h1*s2+h0*s3,hn=Math.sqrt(lr*lr+li*li);
if(pass===0&&gt/(Math.sqrt(re*re+im*im)*hn)<=KF_RHO){hl=hn;break;}
hl=hn;
}
const gq=gt/Math.sqrt(re*re+im*im);let rho=gq/hl;if(rho>KF_RHO)rho=KF_RHO;
ln.P=Pp;ln.D=Dl;ln.di=di;ln.h0=h0;ln.h1=h1;ln.h2=h2;ln.h3=h3;ln.a0=rho*(1-S2);ln.a1=rho*S2;
const K=ln.k;K[0]=cw;K[1]=sw;K[2]=c2;K[3]=s2;K[4]=c3;K[5]=s3;
ln.Pf=Pp;ln.pd=Math.atan2(im,re)/w0;ln.gq=gq;ln.sS=S2;
}
}
if(ln.pl)this.kf2Pluck(ln,ln.P,ln.D,VP);
if(ln.zc>ln.di+CR+4)return t;
const b=ln.b,m=KF_SN-1,h0=ln.h0,h1=ln.h1,h2=ln.h2,h3=ln.h3,a0=ln.a0,a1=ln.a1;let w=ln.w,z=ln.z,zc=ln.zc;
let q=w-ln.di+1,x0=b[(q-3)&m],x1=b[(q-2)&m],x2=b[(q-1)&m];
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
const x3=b[q&m],y=h0*x0+h1*x1+h2*x2+h3*x3;
let o=a0*y+a1*z;if(o<1e-15&&o>-1e-15){o=0;zc++;}else zc=0;
z=y;b[w]=o;w=(w+1)&m;q++;c[n]=o;x0=x1;x1=x2;x2=x3;
bl[n]+=o*gl;if(st)br[n]+=o*gr;
}
ln.w=w;ln.z=z;ln.zc=zc;
return t;
}
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){const y=c[n];bl[n]+=y*gl;if(st)br[n]+=y*gr;}
return t;
},
kf2Pluck(ln,Pp,Dl,VP){
const b=ln.b,m=KF_SN-1,T=this.kfX.tmp,hel=clamp(+VP.strHel,0,1),anr=clamp(+VP.strAnr,0,1);
if(ln.pl===2){b.fill(0);ln.z=0;}
const Pi=Math.max(2,Math.min(KF_SN-8,Math.round(Pp))),L=Math.min(KF_SN-4,Math.ceil(Dl)+4);
const cL=.1+.9*Math.pow(hel,1.5),W=Math.max(2,Math.round(Pi*(.03+.22*(1-hel))));
let s=ln.rs,yl=0,mn=0,mp=0;
for(let j=0;j<64+Pi;j++){s^=s<<13;s^=s>>>17;s^=s<<5;yl+=cL*((s|0)*4.656612873077393e-10-yl);if(j>=64){T[j-64]=yl;mn+=yl;}}
ln.rs=s>>>0||1;mn/=Pi;
for(let j=0;j<W;j++)mp+=.5-.5*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*j/W);
mp/=Pi;
let en=0,ep=0;
for(let j=0;j<Pi;j++){const x=T[j]-mn,p=(j<W? .5-.5*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*j/W):0)-mp;en+=x*x;ep+=p*p;}
const ga=Math.cos(anr*Math.PI/2)/Math.sqrt(en/Pi+1e-30),gb=Math.sin(anr*Math.PI/2)/Math.sqrt(ep/Pi+1e-30);
let e2=0;
for(let j=0;j<Pi;j++){const x=(T[j]-mn)*ga+((j<W? .5-.5*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*j/W):0)-mp)*gb;T[j]=x;e2+=x*x;}
const g=KF_RMS/Math.sqrt(e2/Pi+1e-30);
for(let j=0;j<L;j++)b[(ln.w-L+j)&m]+=T[j%Pi]*g;
ln.pl=0;ln.zc=0;
},
kf2Vow(v,buf,right){
const Z=v.kfV,D=v.D||this.Dc,o=right?8:0;
const g0=v.gB>0?v.gB:1e-6,fc=Math.atan(g0)*SR/Math.PI;
let x=(69+12*Math.log2(fc/440)-KF_NA)/KF_ST;x=x>0?(x<4?x:4):0;
const r=clamp(v.kB/4,0,1.15),bs=KF_BS(r),i0=x>=4?3:Math.floor(x),fr=x-i0;
const TT=KF_TT;
for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const f=Math.exp(KF_LF[i0*4+k]+(KF_LF[(i0+1)*4+k]-KF_LF[i0*4+k])*fr);TT[k]=Math.tan(Math.PI*Math.min(f,.45*SR)/SR);TT[4+k]=KF_B[k]*bs/f;}
const tg0=TT[0],tg1=TT[1],tg2=TT[2],tg3=TT[3],tk0=TT[4],tk1=TT[5],tk2=TT[6],tk3=TT[7];
const tn=kfNorm(x,r)*KF_G[0]*KF_G[1]*KF_G[2]*KF_G[3];
if(Z[25]===0){Z[16]=tg0;Z[17]=tg1;Z[18]=tg2;Z[19]=tg3;Z[20]=tk0;Z[21]=tk1;Z[22]=tk2;Z[23]=tk3;Z[24]=tn;Z[25]=1;}
const iC=1/CR;
let g_0=Z[16],g_1=Z[17],g_2=Z[18],g_3=Z[19],k_0=Z[20],k_1=Z[21],k_2=Z[22],k_3=Z[23],gn=Z[24];
const dg0=(tg0-g_0)*iC,dg1=(tg1-g_1)*iC,dg2=(tg2-g_2)*iC,dg3=(tg3-g_3)*iC;
const dk0=(tk0-k_0)*iC,dk1=(tk1-k_1)*iC,dk2=(tk2-k_2)*iC,dk3=(tk3-k_3)*iC,dn=(tn-gn)*iC;
let s0=Z[o],s1=Z[o+1],s2=Z[o+2],s3=Z[o+3],s4=Z[o+4],s5=Z[o+5],s6=Z[o+6],s7=Z[o+7];
const drv=v.drv,idrv=1/Math.sqrt(drv);
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
g_0+=dg0;g_1+=dg1;g_2+=dg2;g_3+=dg3;k_0+=dk0;k_1+=dk1;k_2+=dk2;k_3+=dk3;gn+=dn;
const xi=buf[n]*drv+1e-20;
let xx=xi*tg(xi),a1,a2,a3,v1,v2,v3;
a1=1/(1+g_0*(g_0+k_0));a2=g_0*a1;a3=g_0*a2;v3=xx-s1;v1=a1*s0+a2*v3;v2=s1+a2*s0+a3*v3;s0=2*v1-s0;s1=2*v2-s1;xx=v2;
a1=1/(1+g_1*(g_1+k_1));a2=g_1*a1;a3=g_1*a2;v3=xx-s3;v1=a1*s2+a2*v3;v2=s3+a2*s2+a3*v3;s2=2*v1-s2;s3=2*v2-s3;xx=v2;
a1=1/(1+g_2*(g_2+k_2));a2=g_2*a1;a3=g_2*a2;v3=xx-s5;v1=a1*s4+a2*v3;v2=s5+a2*s4+a3*v3;s4=2*v1-s4;s5=2*v2-s5;xx=v2;
a1=1/(1+g_3*(g_3+k_3));a2=g_3*a1;a3=g_3*a2;v3=xx-s7;v1=a1*s6+a2*v3;v2=s7+a2*s6+a3*v3;s6=2*v1-s6;s7=2*v2-s7;xx=v2;
buf[n]=xx*gn*idrv;
}
Z[o]=s0;Z[o+1]=s1;Z[o+2]=s2;Z[o+3]=s3;Z[o+4]=s4;Z[o+5]=s5;Z[o+6]=s6;Z[o+7]=s7;
if(!D.stereo||right){Z[16]=tg0;Z[17]=tg1;Z[18]=tg2;Z[19]=tg3;Z[20]=tk0;Z[21]=tk1;Z[22]=tk2;Z[23]=tk3;Z[24]=tn;}
},
});
registerProcessor('dirigent-analog',AnalogCore);
const DSR=29761;
function p2(n){let p=1;while(p<n)p<<=1;return p;}
function rd(b,m,p){const i=p|0,f=p-i;return b[i&m]*(1-f)+b[(i+1)&m]*f;}
const TL=[266,2974,1913,1996,1990,187,1066],TR=[353,3627,1228,2673,2111,335,121];
class Line{
constructor(maxLen){this.b=new Float32Array(p2(maxLen+4));this.m=this.b.length-1;}
}
class DirigentHall extends AudioWorkletProcessor{
static get parameterDescriptors(){
const k='k-rate';
return[
{name:'decay',defaultValue:3,minValue:0.2,maxValue:60,automationRate:k},
{name:'size',defaultValue:1,minValue:0.35,maxValue:1.6,automationRate:k},
{name:'damp',defaultValue:7000,minValue:400,maxValue:20000,automationRate:k},
{name:'lowcut',defaultValue:70,minValue:10,maxValue:600,automationRate:k},
{name:'tone',defaultValue:12000,minValue:500,maxValue:20000,automationRate:k},
{name:'mod',defaultValue:0.5,minValue:0,maxValue:1,automationRate:k},
{name:'width',defaultValue:1,minValue:0,maxValue:2,automationRate:k},
{name:'freeze',defaultValue:0,minValue:0,maxValue:1,automationRate:k},
];
}
constructor(){
super();
const r=sampleRate/DSR,MAXS=1.6,X=32;
this.r=r;
this.dIn=[142,107,379,277].map(n=>Math.round(n*r));
this.ins=this.dIn.map(n=>new Line(n));
this.base={ap1:672,d1:4453,ap2:1800,d2:3720,ap3:908,d3:4217,ap4:2656,d4:3163};
this.L={};
for(const k in this.base)this.L[k]=new Line(Math.ceil((this.base[k]*MAXS+X+2)*r));
this.w=1<<20;
this.size=1;
this.lpL=0;this.lpR=0;this.hpL=0;this.hpR=0;this.bw=0;
this.s=0;this.c=1;
this.sleep=0;this.tl=new Int32Array(7);this.tr=new Int32Array(7);
this.port.onmessage=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.clear){for(const k in this.L)this.L[k].b.fill(0);this.ins.forEach(l=>l.b.fill(0));this.lpL=this.lpR=this.hpL=this.hpR=this.bw=0;}};
}
process(inputs,outputs,P){
const out=outputs[0],oL=out[0],oR=out[1]||out[0],n=oL.length;
const inp=inputs[0]&&inputs[0][0];
const freeze=P.freeze[0]>0.5;
let inPeak=0;
if(inp)for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=inp[i]<0?-inp[i]:inp[i];if(a>inPeak)inPeak=a;}
if(!freeze&&inPeak===0&&this.sleep>2){oL.fill(0);if(oR!==oL)oR.fill(0);return true;}
const r=this.r,sr=sampleRate;
const tgtSize=P.size[0];
this.size+=Math.max(-0.002,Math.min(0.002,tgtSize-this.size));
const S=this.size*r,B=this.base;
const lap1=B.ap1*S,ld1=B.d1*S,lap2=B.ap2*S,ld2=B.d2*S,lap3=B.ap3*S,ld3=B.d3*S,lap4=B.ap4*S,ld4=B.d4*S;
const rt=freeze?1e9:P.decay[0];
const kd=-3/(sr*rt);
const gA1=Math.pow(10,kd*(lap1+ld1)),gB1=Math.pow(10,kd*(lap2+ld2));
const gA2=Math.pow(10,kd*(lap3+ld3)),gB2=Math.pow(10,kd*(lap4+ld4));
const dampA=freeze?1:1-Math.exp(-2*Math.PI*Math.min(P.damp[0],0.45*sr)/sr);
const lowA=freeze?0:1-Math.exp(-2*Math.PI*P.lowcut[0]/sr);
const bwA=1-Math.exp(-2*Math.PI*Math.min(P.tone[0],0.45*sr)/sr);
const mod=P.mod[0],exc=mod*16*r,lfoW=2*Math.PI*(0.35+0.65*mod)/sr;
const width=P.width[0],inGain=freeze?0:1;
const dd1=-0.7,dd2=0.5;
const tL=this.tl,tR=this.tr;
for(let k=0;k<7;k++){tL[k]=Math.round(TL[k]*S);tR[k]=Math.round(TR[k]*S);}
const l0=tL[0],l1=tL[1],l2=tL[2],l3=tL[3],l4=tL[4],l5=tL[5],l6=tL[6];
const r0=tR[0],r1=tR[1],r2=tR[2],r3=tR[3],r4=tR[4],r5=tR[5],r6=tR[6];
const I0=this.ins[0].b,I1=this.ins[1].b,I2=this.ins[2].b,I3=this.ins[3].b;
const M0=this.ins[0].m,M1=this.ins[1].m,M2=this.ins[2].m,M3=this.ins[3].m;
const n0=this.dIn[0],n1=this.dIn[1],n2=this.dIn[2],n3=this.dIn[3];
const L=this.L;
const AP1=L.ap1.b,D1=L.d1.b,AP2=L.ap2.b,D2=L.d2.b,AP3=L.ap3.b,D3=L.d3.b,AP4=L.ap4.b,D4=L.d4.b;
const mA1=L.ap1.m,mD1=L.d1.m,mA2=L.ap2.m,mD2=L.d2.m,mA3=L.ap3.m,mD3=L.d3.m,mA4=L.ap4.m,mD4=L.d4.m;
let w=this.w,lpL=this.lpL,lpR=this.lpR,hpL=this.hpL,hpR=this.hpR,bw=this.bw,s=this.s,c=this.c;
let energy=0;
for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
s+=lfoW*c;c-=lfoW*s;
let x=(inp?inp[i]:0)*inGain;
bw+=bwA*(x-bw);x=bw;
let d,v;
d=I0[(w-n0)&M0];v=x-0.75*d;I0[w&M0]=v;x=d+0.75*v;
d=I1[(w-n1)&M1];v=x-0.75*d;I1[w&M1]=v;x=d+0.75*v;
d=I2[(w-n2)&M2];v=x-0.625*d;I2[w&M2]=v;x=d+0.625*v;
d=I3[(w-n3)&M3];v=x-0.625*d;I3[w&M3]=v;x=d+0.625*v;
x+=(w&1)?1e-20:-1e-20;
const fbFromR=rd(D4,mD4,w-(ld4))*gB2,fbFromL=rd(D2,mD2,w-(ld2))*gB1;
let a=x+fbFromR;
d=rd(AP1,mA1,w-(lap1+exc*(1+s)));v=a-dd1*d;AP1[w&mA1]=v;a=d+dd1*v;
D1[w&mD1]=a;
a=rd(D1,mD1,w-(ld1))*gA1;
lpL+=dampA*(a-lpL);a=lpL;
hpL+=lowA*(a-hpL);a-=hpL;
d=rd(AP2,mA2,w-(lap2));v=a-dd2*d;AP2[w&mA2]=v;a=d+dd2*v;
D2[w&mD2]=a;
let b=x+fbFromL;
d=rd(AP3,mA3,w-(lap3+exc*(1+c)));v=b-dd1*d;AP3[w&mA3]=v;b=d+dd1*v;
D3[w&mD3]=b;
b=rd(D3,mD3,w-(ld3))*gA2;
lpR+=dampA*(b-lpR);b=lpR;
hpR+=lowA*(b-hpR);b-=hpR;
d=rd(AP4,mA4,w-(lap4));v=b-dd2*d;AP4[w&mA4]=v;b=d+dd2*v;
D4[w&mD4]=b;
const yl=D3[(w-l0)&mD3]+D3[(w-l1)&mD3]-AP4[(w-l2)&mA4]+D4[(w-l3)&mD4]-D1[(w-l4)&mD1]-AP2[(w-l5)&mA2]-D2[(w-l6)&mD2];
const yr=D1[(w-r0)&mD1]+D1[(w-r1)&mD1]-AP2[(w-r2)&mA2]+D2[(w-r3)&mD2]-D3[(w-r4)&mD3]-AP4[(w-r5)&mA4]-D4[(w-r6)&mD4];
const mid=(yl+yr)*0.3,side=(yl-yr)*0.3*width;
const ol=mid+side,or=mid-side;
oL[i]=ol;if(oR!==oL)oR[i]=or;
energy+=ol*ol+or*or;
w++;
}
if(w>0x3f000000)w-=0x20000000;
this.w=w;this.lpL=lpL;this.lpR=lpR;this.hpL=hpL;this.hpR=hpR;this.bw=bw;
const m=Math.sqrt(s*s+c*c);this.s=s/m;this.c=c/m;
this.sleep=(inPeak===0&&energy<1e-12*n)?this.sleep+1:0;
return true;
}
}
registerProcessor('dirigent-hall',DirigentHall);
class SynqMeter extends AudioWorkletProcessor{
constructor(o){
super();
const p=(o&&o.processorOptions)||{};
this.n=Math.max(1,(o&&o.numberOfInputs)|0);
this.win=Math.max(1,p.win|0||Math.round(sampleRate/30/128));
this.pk=new Float32Array(this.n);this.sq=new Float64Array(this.n);
this.W=1+2*this.n;this.batch=Math.max(1,p.batch|0||1);this.total=+p.total||0;
this.out=new Float32Array(this.batch*this.W);this.msg={mtr:this.out,seq:0,n:0};this.bi=0;
this.k=0;this.on=p.on!==false;this.alive=true;
this.port.onmessage=e=>{
const m=e.data;if(!m)return;
if(m.on!=null){this.on=!!m.on;this.k=0;this.bi=0;this.pk.fill(0);this.sq.fill(0);}
if(m.dispose)this.alive=false;
};
}
process(inputs){
if(!this.on)return this.alive;
const n=this.n,pk=this.pk,sq=this.sq;
for(let k=0;k<n;k++){
const chs=inputs[k],nc=chs?chs.length:0;if(!nc)continue;
let p=pk[k],s=0;
for(let c=0;c<nc;c++){
const d=chs[c],L=d.length;
for(let i=0;i<L;i++){const x=d[i],a=x<0?-x:x;if(a>p)p=a;s+=x*x;}
}
pk[k]=p;sq[k]+=s/nc;
}
if(++this.k>=this.win){
const out=this.out,N=this.win*128,o=this.bi*this.W;
out[o]=currentTime+128/sampleRate;
for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const r=Math.sqrt(sq[k]/N),p=pk[k];out[o+1+2*k]=p>=0&&p<1e9?p:0;out[o+2+2*k]=r>=0&&r<1e9?r:0;pk[k]=0;sq[k]=0;}
this.k=0;
if(++this.bi>=this.batch||(this.total&&currentFrame+128>=this.total)){this.msg.n=this.bi;this.msg.seq++;this.port.postMessage(this.msg);this.bi=0;}
}
return this.alive;
}
}
registerProcessor('synq-meter',SynqMeter);
const KF1_BA=Math.exp(-2*Math.PI*30/SR);
const KF1_A=new Float64Array(CR),KF1_B=new Float64Array(CR),KF1_W=new Float64Array(CR);
const KF1_NG=new Float64Array(4);
{
const fc=Math.min(16000,.4*SR),w0=2*Math.PI*fc/SR,al=Math.sin(w0)/Math.SQRT2,cw=Math.cos(w0),a0=1+al;
const B0=(1-cw)/2/a0,B1=(1-cw)/a0,A1=-2*cw/a0,A2=(1-al)/a0,f=new Float64Array(16);

}

}
function kf1In(c,r,m){return(m>>((((c-r)%12)+12)%12))&1;}
function kf1Snap(x,r,m,z){
if(!(m&4095))m=4095;
if(!(Math.abs(x)<1e4)){z[14]=z[15]=0;return x;}

z[14]=(a+q)/2;z[15]=(q+c)/2;z[16]=q;

}

const KF1_XF=Math.max(1,Math.round(.01*SR));
class KfKorn extends AudioWorkletProcessor{
constructor(o){
super();
this.b=16;this.r=1;this.ob=16;this.or=1;this.m=0;this.mT=0;this.xf=0;this.alive=true;
this.ph=new Float64Array(2);this.h=new Float64Array(2);this.oph=new Float64Array(2);this.oh=new Float64Array(2);
this.port.onmessage=e=>this.msg(e.data);
const p=o&&o.processorOptions;if(p&&p.set)this.msg(p.set);
}
msg(d){
if(!d)return;
if(d.dispose){this.alive=false;return;}
const b=d.b==null?this.b:Math.max(2,Math.min(16,Math.round(+d.b)||16)),r=d.r==null?this.r:Math.max(1/32,Math.min(1,+d.r||1));
if(b!==this.b||r!==this.r){
if(this.m>0||this.mT>0){this.ob=this.b;this.or=this.r;this.oph.set(this.ph);this.oh.set(this.h);this.xf=KF1_XF;}
this.b=b;this.r=r;
}
if(d.m!=null)this.mT=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,+d.m||0));
}
process(inputs,outputs){
const I=inputs[0],O=outputs[0];if(!O||!O.length)return this.alive;
const nI=I?I.length:0;
if(!nI){for(let c=0;c<O.length;c++)O[c].fill(0);return this.alive;}
if(this.m===0&&this.mT===0){for(let c=0;c<O.length;c++)O[c].fill(0);this.xf=0;return this.alive;}
const q=Math.pow(2,this.b-1),iq=1/q,oq=Math.pow(2,this.ob-1),ioq=1/oq,r=this.r,or=this.or,L=O[0].length,dm=1/KF1_XF;
let m=0,xf=0;
for(let c=0;c<O.length;c++){
const x=I[c<nI?c:nI-1],y=O[c];
let ph=this.ph[c],h=this.h[c],oph=this.oph[c],oh=this.oh[c];m=this.m;xf=this.xf;
for(let i=0;i<L;i++){
const s=x[i];
ph+=r;if(ph>=1){ph-=Math.floor(ph);h=s;}
let w=Math.round(h*q)*iq;
if(xf>0){oph+=or;if(oph>=1){oph-=Math.floor(oph);oh=s;}const a=xf*dm;w=w+(Math.round(oh*oq)*ioq-w)*a;xf--;}
if(m!==this.mT){m+=m<this.mT?dm:-dm;if(Math.abs(m-this.mT)<dm)m=this.mT;}
y[i]=(w-s)*m;
}
this.ph[c]=ph;this.h[c]=h;this.oph[c]=oph;this.oh[c]=oh;
}
this.m=m;this.xf=xf;
return this.alive;
}
}

if(n>=3)x=Math.round((x+1)*.5*(n-1))/(n-1)*2-1;
if(P.ksG1>0){if(fresh)z[0]=x;else z[0]+=(x-z[0])*(1-Math.exp(-3*step*r1/P.ksG1));x=z[0];}
src[S_LFO1]=x;
x=src[S_LFO2];n=P.ksR2|0;
if(n>=3)x=Math.round((x+1)*.5*(n-1))/(n-1)*2-1;
if(P.ksG2>0){if(fresh)z[1]=x;else z[1]+=(x-z[1])*(1-Math.exp(-3*step*r2/P.ksG2));x=z[1];}
src[S_LFO2]=x;
},
ks4Ctl(v,P,src,dst,step){
const r1=P.ksR1,r2=P.ksR2;
if((r1===1||r1===2)&&!(P.kfSh1&&(P.l1shape|0)===4))this.ks4Ras(S_LFO1,r1,src,dst);
if((r2===1||r2===2)&&!(P.kfSh2&&(P.l2shape|0)===4))this.ks4Ras(S_LFO2,r2,src,dst);
if(P.ksPe!==0)dst[D_PITCH]+=P.ksPe*v.mOut/24;
if(this.ks4F){
const q=P.ksF2q|0,e=q===1?v.mOut:q===2?src[S_LFO1]:v.fOut;
const n=this.ks4C0+P.ksF2e*72*e,g=lut(GTAN,n<0?0:n),z=v.ks4;
if(v.t0<=step*1.5){z[4]=g;z[6]=z[7]=z[8]=z[9]=0;}else z[4]=z[5];
z[5]=g;
}
},
ks4Ras(s,r,src,dst){
let raw=0,any=false;
for(let i=0;i<NSLOT;i++)if(this.msrc[i]===s&&this.mdst[i]===D_PITCH){raw+=this.mamt[i]*src[s];any=true;}
if(!any)return;
const x=raw*24;let q;
if(r===1)q=Math.round(x);
else{const k=Math.floor(x/12),f=x-12*k;q=12*k+(f<3.5?0:f<9.5?7:12);}
dst[D_PITCH]+=(q-x)/24;
},
ks4Pre(v,st){
if((this.P.ksF2w|0)===1){KS4_X.set(this.bl);if(st)KS4_Y.set(this.br);}
return true;
},
ks4Post(v,st){
const P=this.P,typ=P.ksF2|0,m=v.ksmF?v.ksmF[0]:clamp(+P.ksF2m,0,1),k=v.ksmF?v.ksmF[1]:2-1.95*clamp(+P.ksF2r||0,0,1),z=v.ks4;
if((P.ksF2w|0)===1){this.ks4Svf(KS4_X,this.bl,z,6,typ,k,m,true);if(st)this.ks4Svf(KS4_Y,this.br,z,8,typ,k,m,true);}
else{this.ks4Svf(this.bl,this.bl,z,6,typ,k,m,false);if(st)this.ks4Svf(this.br,this.br,z,8,typ,k,m,false);}
},
ks4Svf(x,out,z,j,typ,k,m,add){
let g=z[4],s1=z[j],s2=z[j+1];
const dg=(z[5]-z[4])/CR;
for(let n=0;n<CR;n++){
g+=dg;
const a1=1/(1+g*(g+k)),xi=x[n],v1=a1*(s1+g*(xi-s2)),v2=s2+g*v1;
s1=2*v1-s1;s2=2*v2-s2;
const y=typ===1?v2:typ===2?xi-k*v1-v2:typ===3?k*v1:xi-k*v1;
out[n]=add?out[n]+m*y:xi+m*(y-xi);
}
if(s1<1e-20&&s1>-1e-20)s1=0;if(s2<1e-20&&s2>-1e-20)s2=0;
z[j]=s1;z[j+1]=s2;
},
});
</script>
